# Provisions

## Intent

Use this generated page to resolve a provision ID to its heading, owning page, and verification mark.

Run `litespec-maintain provisions` after any provision change. The repository validator fails when this page and the active standards disagree.

The active release states 1749 provisions across 6 areas.

| ID | Provision | Kind | Mark | Evidence |

| litespec/backend/architecture#use-five-application-parts | [Use Five Application Parts](../backend/architecture.md#use-five-application-parts) | rule | guidance | The solution file lists Domain, Application.Abstractions, Application, Infrastructure, and WebApi as the application parts, with no sixth part. |
| litespec/backend/domain#align-id-representations-at-every-boundary | [Align ID Representations at Every Boundary](../backend/domain.md#align-id-representations-at-every-boundary) | convention | guidance | Each identifier reads as one value in Domain, persistence, transport, and generated clients. |
| litespec/backend/domain#call-the-marked-act-from-each-command-handler | [Call the Marked Act from Each Command Handler](../backend/domain.md#call-the-marked-act-from-each-command-handler) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `use-case-act-missing` (level S) for a command handler changing an entity or saga aggregate that calls no root method marked with its use case. The check runs in the CLI only, with no analyzer rule: the handler and the root method sit in different projects, and one compilation sees one project. |
| litespec/backend/domain#create-valid-aggregates-through-named-factories | [Create Valid Aggregates Through Named Factories](../backend/domain.md#create-valid-aggregates-through-named-factories) | rule | guidance | Each aggregate root creates instances through a factory named for its business action, checking identity and recording events. |
| litespec/backend/domain#declare-cross-record-keys-on-the-aggregate | [Declare Cross-Record Keys on the Aggregate](../backend/domain.md#declare-cross-record-keys-on-the-aggregate) | rule | guidance | Each aggregate with a cross-record uniqueness rule exposes its current keys through `UniquenessKeys`, with no other uniqueness code in Domain. |
| litespec/backend/domain#define-collection-value-semantics-explicitly | [Define Collection Value Semantics Explicitly](../backend/domain.md#define-collection-value-semantics-explicitly) | rule | guidance | Each collection value implements content equality with a matching hash under its declared ordering rule. |
| litespec/backend/domain#describe-every-public-domain-contract-in-documentation | [Describe Every Public Domain Contract in Documentation](../backend/domain.md#describe-every-public-domain-contract-in-documentation) | rule | guidance | Each public Domain type and member documents the business constraint, result, or refusal it represents. |
| litespec/backend/domain#expose-repositories-for-one-aggregate-only | [Expose Repositories for One Aggregate Only](../backend/domain.md#expose-repositories-for-one-aggregate-only) | rule | guidance | Each domain repository serves one aggregate root with aggregate and Domain types only, and no composable query, session, or generic CRUD surface. |
| litespec/backend/domain#express-transitions-through-business-methods | [Express Transitions Through Business Methods](../backend/domain.md#express-transitions-through-business-methods) | rule | guidance | Each public mutation checks state and protects its invariants, and each lifecycle move also replaces state and records its transition event. |
| litespec/backend/domain#give-each-use-case-its-own-act | [Give Each Use Case Its Own Act](../backend/domain.md#give-each-use-case-its-own-act) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `mark-count-invalid` (level S), and `dotnet build` reports `LS5003`, for a root method carrying more than one use-case mark; `mark-unknown` and `LS5002` report a mark on a method that changes no state. |
| litespec/backend/domain#identify-aggregates-with-strongly-typed-version-7-values | [Identify Aggregates with Strongly Typed Version 7 Values](../backend/domain.md#identify-aggregates-with-strongly-typed-version-7-values) | rule | guidance | Each aggregate identity is a dedicated type over one version 7 UUID, with no conversion, alias, or creation path around its validation. |
| litespec/backend/domain#keep-binary-floating-point-out-of-exact-quantities | [Keep Binary Floating Point Out of Exact Quantities](../backend/domain.md#keep-binary-floating-point-out-of-exact-quantities) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS1008` for a `double` or `float` type in Domain. |
| litespec/backend/domain#keep-child-entities-inside-the-aggregate-boundary | [Keep Child Entities Inside the Aggregate Boundary](../backend/domain.md#keep-child-entities-inside-the-aggregate-boundary) | rule | guidance | Child entities expose no mutable state outside their root, with collections as read-only views. |
| litespec/backend/domain#keep-domain-independent-of-outer-layers | [Keep Domain Independent of Outer Layers](../backend/domain.md#keep-domain-independent-of-outer-layers) | rule | guidance | Domain code references the language standard library and project-owned Domain types, with no outer-layer reference. |
| litespec/backend/domain#model-every-aggregate-lifecycle-with-state-types | [Model Every Aggregate Lifecycle with State Types](../backend/domain.md#model-every-aggregate-lifecycle-with-state-types) | rule | guidance | Each aggregate exposes one state property backed by one closed hierarchy of per-state types. |
| litespec/backend/domain#model-every-closed-set-of-domain-values-as-a-closed-type-hierarchy | [Model Every Closed Set of Domain Values as a Closed Type Hierarchy](../backend/domain.md#model-every-closed-set-of-domain-values-as-a-closed-type-hierarchy) | rule | guidance | Each closed set is one type hierarchy whose cases the type system enumerates, with label lookups by name. |
| litespec/backend/domain#model-money-with-exact-decimal-values | [Model Money with Exact Decimal Values](../backend/domain.md#model-money-with-exact-decimal-values) | rule | guidance | Each monetary amount is an exact decimal in the shared `Money` value with the stated currency and JSON shape. |
| litespec/backend/domain#organize-a-module-by-aggregate-and-concept | [Organize a Module by Aggregate and Concept](../backend/domain.md#organize-a-module-by-aggregate-and-concept) | convention | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS1013` for a domain event outside its aggregate's `Events` folder. |
| litespec/backend/domain#pass-nondeterministic-values-into-domain | [Pass Nondeterministic Values into Domain](../backend/domain.md#pass-nondeterministic-values-into-domain) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS1009` for a clock, randomness, or identifier read in Domain. |
| litespec/backend/domain#place-every-constituent-type-in-its-aggregates-folder | [Place Every Constituent Type in Its Aggregate's Folder](../backend/domain.md#place-every-constituent-type-in-its-aggregates-folder) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `constituent-parent-missing` (level A) for an unmarked Domain type whose folder holds no aggregate root and is not the shared kernel, and the model output lists each constituent under its aggregate. |
| litespec/backend/domain#raise-immutable-facts-as-domain-events | [Raise Immutable Facts as Domain Events](../backend/domain.md#raise-immutable-facts-as-domain-events) | rule | guidance | Each domain event is immutable, named for its aggregate and fact, and carries business data only. |
| litespec/backend/domain#raise-the-declared-events-from-the-marked-act | [Raise the Declared Events from the Marked Act](../backend/domain.md#raise-the-declared-events-from-the-marked-act) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `act-event-undeclared` (level S) for a marked method raising an event its use case does not list, and `act-event-unimplemented` (level S) for a listed event no marked method raises. |
| litespec/backend/domain#reference-other-aggregates-by-id | [Reference Other Aggregates by ID](../backend/domain.md#reference-other-aggregates-by-id) | rule | guidance | Each aggregate stores other aggregates as typed identifiers, with no object reference. |
| litespec/backend/domain#reject-business-violations-with-dedicated-refusals | [Reject Business Violations with Dedicated Refusals](../backend/domain.md#reject-business-violations-with-dedicated-refusals) | rule | guidance | Each rejected business rule ends its use case in a dedicated refusal carrying its stable code, with no transport status. |
| litespec/backend/domain#reuse-the-shared-domain-contracts-across-modules | [Reuse the Shared Domain Contracts Across Modules](../backend/domain.md#reuse-the-shared-domain-contracts-across-modules) | convention | guidance | Each shared Domain contract is declared once and reused across modules. |
| litespec/backend/domain#take-identity-and-events-from-the-project-root-contract | [Take Identity and Events from the Project Root Contract](../backend/domain.md#take-identity-and-events-from-the-project-root-contract) | rule | guidance | Each aggregate root takes its identity and event mechanics from the one project-owned root contract. |
| litespec/backend/domain#treat-aggregates-as-consistency-boundaries | [Treat Aggregates as Consistency Boundaries](../backend/domain.md#treat-aggregates-as-consistency-boundaries) | rule | guidance | Each aggregate root is the only public mutation entry point for the invariants its transaction protects. |
| litespec/backend/domain#use-immutable-value-objects-for-domain-concepts | [Use Immutable Value Objects for Domain Concepts](../backend/domain.md#use-immutable-value-objects-for-domain-concepts) | rule | guidance | Each value object is immutable, compares by complete value, and validates every value at creation. |
| litespec/backend/domain#use-one-ubiquitous-language | [Use One Ubiquitous Language](../backend/domain.md#use-one-ubiquitous-language) | rule | guidance | Each Domain name matches a term in `glossary.json` beside the specification. |
| litespec/backend/domain#use-stateless-domain-services-for-ownerless-rules | [Use Stateless Domain Services for Ownerless Rules](../backend/domain.md#use-stateless-domain-services-for-ownerless-rules) | rule | guidance | Each domain service is stateless over Domain values, with no persistence, messaging, logging, clock, authorization, or provider call. |
| litespec/backend/domain#use-these-domain-names | [Use These Domain Names](../backend/domain.md#use-these-domain-names) | convention | guidance | Each Domain name follows the cited workspace naming provisions, with one state-case form kept project-wide. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/api#declare-operation-metadata-in-the-contract | [Declare Operation Metadata in the Contract](../backend/dotnet/api.md#declare-operation-metadata-in-the-contract) | rule | guidance | Each operation: a stable `operationId`, authorization, request, success, and Problem Details metadata, typed results or `Produces` entries, and a summary. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/api#derive-http-statuses-from-refusal-codes | [Derive HTTP Statuses from Refusal Codes](../backend/dotnet/api.md#derive-http-statuses-from-refusal-codes) | rule | guidance | The WebApi global handler: one `IExceptionHandler` mapping each refusal code to its outcome-table status. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/api#exclude-mvc-controllers-from-the-stack | [Exclude MVC Controllers from the Stack](../backend/dotnet/api.md#exclude-mvc-controllers-from-the-stack) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS2003` for a WebApi class deriving from `ControllerBase` or carrying `ApiController`. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/api#keep-programcs-as-composition | [Keep Program.cs as Composition](../backend/dotnet/api.md#keep-programcs-as-composition) | convention | guidance | `Program.cs`: module registration, middleware order, endpoint discovery, health endpoints, OpenAPI, and host startup in the recorded order. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/api#mark-polymorphic-transport-models-with-discriminators | [Mark Polymorphic Transport Models with Discriminators](../backend/dotnet/api.md#mark-polymorphic-transport-models-with-discriminators) | rule | guidance | Each data-bearing set: an abstract base record with one sealed record per case, published as `oneOf` with a string discriminator. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/api#use-one-endpoint-per-operation | [Use One Endpoint per Operation](../backend/dotnet/api.md#use-one-endpoint-per-operation) | rule | guidance | Each mapped route: one `IEndpoint` type converting input, dispatching through its mediator, and mapping the result. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/api#use-the-endpoint-folder-layout | [Use the Endpoint Folder Layout](../backend/dotnet/api.md#use-the-endpoint-folder-layout) | convention | guidance | Each endpoint folder under its module and aggregate: the endpoint, request and response models, and mappings named for the use case. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/application#bind-the-pipeline-to-the-pinned-litebus-apis | [Bind the Pipeline to the Pinned LiteBus APIs](../backend/dotnet/application.md#bind-the-pipeline-to-the-pinned-litebus-apis) | rule | guidance | The command pipeline composition: one pinned LiteBus API per position, for commit, authorization, audit, fault classification, delivery, event staging, and the timestamp. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/application#dispatch-declared-effects-through-their-entry-points | [Dispatch Declared Effects Through Their Entry Points](../backend/dotnet/application.md#dispatch-declared-effects-through-their-entry-points) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `effect-undeclared` for a dispatch no record declares; each event handler dispatches sends through the messenger port, calls through its external-system port, and runs through `ICommandMediator`, after the commit. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/application#keep-command-handlers-to-load-call-and-stage | [Keep Command Handlers to Load, Call and Stage](../backend/dotnet/application.md#keep-command-handlers-to-load-call-and-stage) | rule | guidance | Each command handler: one aggregate loaded or created, one domain method called, staged through its repository, and nothing else; `dotnet build` reports find-or-create as LS2014 and looped stores as LS2015. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/application#keep-query-handlers-free-of-writes | [Keep Query Handlers Free of Writes](../backend/dotnet/application.md#keep-query-handlers-free-of-writes) | rule | guidance | Each query handler: no repository, projecting through `IQuerySession`, staging, storing, deleting and dispatching nothing; `dotnet build` reports a repository in a query handler as LS2011. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/application#report-input-problems-as-validity | [Report Input Problems as Validity](../backend/dotnet/application.md#report-input-problems-as-validity) | rule | guidance | Each validator: findings as `Validity`, with each `ValidationFailure` carrying member, code, and message, and no HTTP status. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/application#run-event-automations-in-dedicated-handlers | [Run Event Automations in Dedicated Handlers](../backend/dotnet/application.md#run-event-automations-in-dedicated-handlers) | rule | guidance | Each event automation in Application: one handler class carrying its record's mark, named `{Action}On{Fact}Handler` in the `On{Fact}` folder. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/application#run-scheduled-automations-as-hosted-operations | [Run Scheduled Automations as Hosted Operations](../backend/dotnet/application.md#run-scheduled-automations-as-hosted-operations) | rule | guidance | Each scheduled or manual automation: one hosted operation carrying its record's mark, honoring selection, batch size, lease, and cursor. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/application#use-the-operation-folder-layout | [Use the Operation Folder Layout](../backend/dotnet/application.md#use-the-operation-folder-layout) | convention | guidance | Each operation folder under its module and aggregate: the message, result, validator, and handler files named for the use case; each event handler in its `On{Fact}` folder. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/application#use-the-pinned-mediator-entry-points | [Use the Pinned Mediator Entry Points](../backend/dotnet/application.md#use-the-pinned-mediator-entry-points) | convention | guidance | Each dispatch site: commands through `ICommandMediator.SendAsync` and queries through `IQueryMediator.QueryAsync`, from the pinned LiteBus version. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/architecture#keep-composition-in-hosts | [Keep Composition in Hosts](../backend/dotnet/architecture.md#keep-composition-in-hosts) | convention | guidance | Service registration calls: each one in `Program.cs` or a host registration module. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/architecture#keep-implementation-types-internal | [Keep Implementation Types Internal](../backend/dotnet/architecture.md#keep-implementation-types-internal) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS2001` for an implementation type that is not `internal sealed`. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/architecture#keep-one-application-assembly | [Keep One Application Assembly](../backend/dotnet/architecture.md#keep-one-application-assembly) | rule | guidance | The Application project files hold every command, query, result, validator, handler, and event handler class, with cross-module contracts in Application.Abstractions. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/architecture#mirror-modules-in-folders-and-namespaces | [Mirror Modules in Folders and Namespaces](../backend/dotnet/architecture.md#mirror-modules-in-folders-and-namespaces) | rule | guidance | The module folders and namespaces in every project: one folder and namespace per module, named for the module. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/architecture#use-five-application-projects | [Use Five Application Projects](../backend/dotnet/architecture.md#use-five-application-projects) | rule | guidance | The solution projects: exactly Domain, Application.Abstractions, Application, Infrastructure, and WebApi. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/architecture#use-one-public-assembly-marker-per-scanned-project | [Use One Public Assembly Marker per Scanned Project](../backend/dotnet/architecture.md#use-one-public-assembly-marker-per-scanned-project) | convention | guidance | Each scanned project: exactly one public static assembly marker. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/config#bind-setting-records-to-options | [Bind Setting Records to Options](../backend/dotnet/config.md#bind-setting-records-to-options) | rule | guidance | Each decider-set or operator-set setting binds a named options class validated at startup; tenant-set values live in stored tenant state. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/config#centralize-net-build-settings | [Centralize .NET Build Settings](../backend/dotnet/config.md#centralize-net-build-settings) | rule | guidance | `apps/api/Directory.Build.props` sets the manifest target framework with nullable types, implicit usings, and warnings as errors. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/config#centralize-nuget-versions | [Centralize NuGet Versions](../backend/dotnet/config.md#centralize-nuget-versions) | rule | guidance | `apps/api/Directory.Packages.props` enables central management with the manifest pins, and project files carry no versions. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/config#commit-the-tool-manifest | [Commit the Tool Manifest](../backend/dotnet/config.md#commit-the-tool-manifest) | rule | guidance | The root `.config/dotnet-tools.json` pins each local tool a documented command invokes, including the Stryker.NET runner. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/config#set-the-sdk-floor-at-the-workspace-root | [Set the SDK Floor at the Workspace Root](../backend/dotnet/config.md#set-the-sdk-floor-at-the-workspace-root) | rule | guidance | The root `global.json` sets the manifest major release's first feature band with `latestMajor` roll-forward. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/config#validate-backend-options-at-startup | [Validate Backend Options at Startup](../backend/dotnet/config.md#validate-backend-options-at-startup) | rule | guidance | Each configuration section binds a named options class validated for required fields, ranges, and formats, failing startup on bad values. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#define-the-shared-domain-contracts-once | [Define the Shared Domain Contracts Once](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#define-the-shared-domain-contracts-once) | convention | guidance | The shared Domain contracts: each declared once, with `DomainException` declaring its abstract `Code` and `AggregateRoot<TId>` deriving from `Entity<TId>`. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#document-every-public-domain-contract | [Document Every Public Domain Contract](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#document-every-public-domain-contract) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS1011` for a public Domain type or member without XML documentation. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#expose-cross-record-keys-from-the-aggregate | [Expose Cross-Record Keys from the Aggregate](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#expose-cross-record-keys-from-the-aggregate) | rule | guidance | Each aggregate with a cross-record uniqueness rule: a `UniquenessKeys` method returning its current keys, with no other uniqueness code in Domain. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#implement-domain-services-as-stateless-classes | [Implement Domain Services as Stateless Classes](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#implement-domain-services-as-stateless-classes) | rule | guidance | Each domain service in the Domain project: a stateless sealed class with Domain-only parameters and no provider calls. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#keep-domain-free-of-outer-layer-concerns | [Keep Domain Free of Outer-Layer Concerns](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#keep-domain-free-of-outer-layer-concerns) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS1001` for Domain code naming an outer-layer type. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#keep-id-representations-aligned-at-every-boundary | [Keep ID Representations Aligned at Every Boundary](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#keep-id-representations-aligned-at-every-boundary) | convention | guidance | Each identifier at Domain, persistence, transport, and client boundaries: the same UUID text form. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#keep-repository-interfaces-in-domain | [Keep Repository Interfaces in Domain](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#keep-repository-interfaces-in-domain) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS1010` for a repository interface exposing a query surface. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#make-money-and-decimal-rules-explicit | [Make Money and Decimal Rules Explicit](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#make-money-and-decimal-rules-explicit) | rule | guidance | Each monetary amount: a `decimal` inside the shared `Money` object, serialized with amount and currency. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#model-child-entities-as-sealed-classes | [Model Child Entities as Sealed Classes](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#model-child-entities-as-sealed-classes) | rule | guidance | Each child entity declaration in the Domain project: a sealed class deriving from `Entity<TId>`, created through root methods. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#model-every-aggregate-lifecycle-with-state-records | [Model Every Aggregate Lifecycle with State Records](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#model-every-aggregate-lifecycle-with-state-records) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS1003` for an aggregate with no `State` property and `LS1004` for a state case left unsealed. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#model-every-closed-set-of-domain-values-without-enums | [Model Every Closed Set of Domain Values Without Enums](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#model-every-closed-set-of-domain-values-without-enums) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS1002` for an enum declared in Domain. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#model-value-objects-as-records | [Model Value Objects as Records](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#model-value-objects-as-records) | rule | guidance | Each value object in the Domain project: an immutable record with static creation and no base type. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#raise-immutable-domain-facts | [Raise Immutable Domain Facts](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#raise-immutable-domain-facts) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS1007` for a domain event that is not an immutable record. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#raise-the-past-tense-of-a-methods-own-verb | [Raise the Past Tense of a Method's Own Verb](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#raise-the-past-tense-of-a-methods-own-verb) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `event-tense-invalid` (level A) for an aggregate method raising an event not ending with the past tense of its verb, allowing a prepositional complement and a `mark` method naming its state. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#reject-business-violations-with-domain-exceptions | [Reject Business Violations with Domain Exceptions](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#reject-business-violations-with-domain-exceptions) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS1005` for a throw outside a `DomainException` subclass and `LS1006` for a domain exception that is misnamed or open. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#use-strongly-typed-version-7-identifiers | [Use Strongly Typed Version 7 Identifiers](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#use-strongly-typed-version-7-identifiers) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS1012` for an `Id` struct outside the strongly typed form, or a raw `Guid` as an aggregate identity. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#use-the-domain-module-layout | [Use the Domain Module Layout](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#use-the-domain-module-layout) | convention | guidance | Each Domain module folder: one plural folder per aggregate holding its states, events, exceptions, and concepts. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/domain#use-the-project-aggregate-root-contract | [Use the Project Aggregate Root Contract](../backend/dotnet/domain.md#use-the-project-aggregate-root-contract) | rule | guidance | Each aggregate root declaration: a sealed class deriving from the project-owned `AggregateRoot<TId>`. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/bdd#assert-observable-outcomes | [Assert Observable Outcomes](../backend/dotnet/ext/bdd.md#assert-observable-outcomes) | rule | guidance | Every BDD scenario in the acceptance suite asserts a response, read, event, record, or other observable outcome. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/bdd#avoid-routine-scenario-duplication | [Avoid Routine Scenario Duplication](../backend/dotnet/ext/bdd.md#avoid-routine-scenario-duplication) | rule | guidance | Unit and validation tests in the narrower suites have no duplicate BDD scenario in the acceptance suite. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/bdd#drive-public-behavior | [Drive Public Behavior](../backend/dotnet/ext/bdd.md#drive-public-behavior) | rule | guidance | Every step definition in the acceptance suite calls the HTTP API or another declared public boundary. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/bdd#exclude-production-internals | [Exclude Production Internals](../backend/dotnet/ext/bdd.md#exclude-production-internals) | rule | guidance | No step definition in the acceptance suite resolves a repository, handler, session, or the production service provider. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/bdd#group-feature-files-by-module | [Group Feature Files by Module](../backend/dotnet/ext/bdd.md#group-feature-files-by-module) | convention | guidance | Feature files or feature classes in `Acceptance.Tests` sit under module folders, or the project documentation names another grouping. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/bdd#isolate-scenario-execution | [Isolate Scenario Execution](../backend/dotnet/ext/bdd.md#isolate-scenario-execution) | rule | guidance | The acceptance suite resets database state between scenarios, and every scenario passes alone and in any order. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/bdd#keep-steps-vocabulary-scoped | [Keep Steps Vocabulary-Scoped](../backend/dotnet/ext/bdd.md#keep-steps-vocabulary-scoped) | convention | guidance | Every reusable step set in the acceptance suite serves one business vocabulary, or the project documentation names another scope. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/bdd#limit-database-assertions | [Limit Database Assertions](../backend/dotnet/ext/bdd.md#limit-database-assertions) | rule | guidance | Every direct database assertion in the acceptance suite names its documented durable boundary. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/bdd#limit-scenario-context | [Limit Scenario Context](../backend/dotnet/ext/bdd.md#limit-scenario-context) | convention | guidance | The scenario context object in the acceptance suite holds scenario state only, or the project documentation names another use. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/bdd#mark-pull-request-scenarios | [Mark Pull-Request Scenarios](../backend/dotnet/ext/bdd.md#mark-pull-request-scenarios) | convention | guidance | The pull-request subset in the acceptance suite stays small and owned, marked separately from proves claims. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/bdd#own-scenario-data | [Own Scenario Data](../backend/dotnet/ext/bdd.md#own-scenario-data) | rule | guidance | Every BDD scenario in the acceptance suite declares its own identifiers, actor context, requests, responses, and expected state. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/bdd#preserve-scenario-failures | [Preserve Scenario Failures](../backend/dotnet/ext/bdd.md#preserve-scenario-failures) | rule | guidance | Every hook in the acceptance suite awaits setup and cleanup and keeps the original scenario failure. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/bdd#prove-a-scenario-from-every-bdd-scenario | [Prove a Scenario from Every BDD Scenario](../backend/dotnet/ext/bdd.md#prove-a-scenario-from-every-bdd-scenario) | rule | guidance | Every BDD scenario in the acceptance suite carries one `proves` claim naming a scenario record. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/bdd#select-shared-critical-examples | [Select Shared Critical Examples](../backend/dotnet/ext/bdd.md#select-shared-critical-examples) | rule | guidance | Every BDD scenario in the acceptance suite traces to a cross-layer example the team discusses in business language. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/bdd#translate-business-phrases | [Translate Business Phrases](../backend/dotnet/ext/bdd.md#translate-business-phrases) | rule | guidance | Every step definition in the acceptance suite delegates to a typed test-driver call. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#apply-migrations-as-release-work | [Apply Migrations as Release Work](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#apply-migrations-as-release-work) | rule | guidance | Which release records skip applying migrations before replica startup? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#avoid-per-aggregate-read-stores | [Avoid Per-Aggregate Read Stores](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#avoid-per-aggregate-read-stores) | rule | guidance | Which per-aggregate read-store interfaces exist beside `IApplicationDbContext`? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#commit-ef-core-command-work-once | [Commit EF Core Command Work Once](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#commit-ef-core-command-work-once) | rule | guidance | Which post-handlers call `SaveChangesAsync` more than once for one owned command? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#configure-ef-core-mappings-in-infrastructure | [Configure EF Core Mappings in Infrastructure](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#configure-ef-core-mappings-in-infrastructure) | rule | guidance | Which EF Core mappings live outside an Infrastructure-owned configuration class? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#configure-optimistic-tokens | [Configure Optimistic Tokens](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#configure-optimistic-tokens) | rule | guidance | Which aggregates miss their configured token, or which commands miss their expected version? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#configure-relational-details-explicitly | [Configure Relational Details Explicitly](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#configure-relational-details-explicitly) | rule | guidance | The mapping review records the explicit configuration of typed IDs, owned values, fields, indexes, constraints, precision, deletes, concurrency tokens, and database names. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#define-ef-core-query-roots | [Define EF Core Query Roots](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#define-ef-core-query-roots) | rule | guidance | Which Application types expose EF Core surface beyond the owned `IApplicationDbContext`? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#exclude-direct-handler-commits | [Exclude Direct Handler Commits](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#exclude-direct-handler-commits) | rule | guidance | Which handlers inject `DbContext` or call `SaveChangesAsync`? Look at the constructor parameters and bodies of each command handler. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#generate-migrations-for-schema-changes | [Generate Migrations for Schema Changes](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#generate-migrations-for-schema-changes) | rule | guidance | Which schema diffs carry no generated migration? Look at the migration list beside the model snapshot. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#implement-query-roots-in-infrastructure | [Implement Query Roots in Infrastructure](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#implement-query-roots-in-infrastructure) | rule | guidance | Does the Infrastructure `DbContext` implement the owned query interface? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#keep-migration-files-together | [Keep Migration Files Together](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#keep-migration-files-together) | rule | guidance | Which migration commits miss their migration, model snapshot, or reviewed SQL? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#map-ef-core-concurrency-conflict | [Map EF Core Concurrency Conflict](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#map-ef-core-concurrency-conflict) | rule | guidance | Which `DbUpdateConcurrencyException` paths escape the Application conflict contract? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#map-unsupported-state-shapes-in-infrastructure | [Map Unsupported State Shapes in Infrastructure](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#map-unsupported-state-shapes-in-infrastructure) | rule | guidance | Which discriminator values map to no Domain state? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#process-changed-aggregate-events | [Process Changed Aggregate Events](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#process-changed-aggregate-events) | rule | guidance | Which domain events from changed aggregates are dropped or miss their declared `onFailure` handling? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#project-ef-core-queries-directly | [Project EF Core Queries Directly](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#project-ef-core-queries-directly) | rule | guidance | Which queries track entities, skip filters, page nondeterministically, skip projection, or feed an invariant from a projection? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#record-provider-selection | [Record Provider Selection](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#record-provider-selection) | rule | guidance | The adoption decision names each command path's provider with its architecture-test evidence. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#register-one-command-post-handler | [Register One Command Post-Handler](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#register-one-command-post-handler) | rule | guidance | Which commands resolve a post-handler other than their selected provider's? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#reject-automatic-command-retry | [Reject Automatic Command Retry](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#reject-automatic-command-retry) | rule | guidance | Which post-handlers retry the complete command automatically after a concurrency exception? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#reject-cross-provider-outbox-storage | [Reject Cross-Provider Outbox Storage](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#reject-cross-provider-outbox-storage) | rule | guidance | Which EF Core commands store their outbox record through a Marten session? Look at the outbox staging calls in each command path. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#reject-mixed-provider-writes | [Reject Mixed Provider Writes](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#reject-mixed-provider-writes) | rule | guidance | Which commands stage writes through both Marten-backed and EF Core-backed repositories? Look at the repository calls in each command handler. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#reject-startup-schema-mutation | [Reject Startup Schema Mutation](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#reject-startup-schema-mutation) | rule | guidance | Which production or staging startup calls `EnsureCreated`, `EnsureDeleted`, or `Migrate`? Look at the startup paths of those environments. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#resolve-cross-provider-invariants | [Resolve Cross-Provider Invariants](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#resolve-cross-provider-invariants) | rule | guidance | Every accepted atomic cross-provider invariant sits on one provider or names a reviewed shared transaction mechanism. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#resolve-the-tenant-at-context-creation | [Resolve the Tenant at Context Creation](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#resolve-the-tenant-at-context-creation) | rule | guidance | Which contexts resolve the tenant after creation or miss a tenant filter on an owned set? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#retain-domain-state-values | [Retain Domain State Values](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#retain-domain-state-values) | rule | guidance | Which persistence mappings reshape the aggregate's single Domain state value? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#retain-marten-query-boundary | [Retain Marten Query Boundary](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#retain-marten-query-boundary) | rule | guidance | Which Marten query handlers leave the `IQuerySession` boundary? Look at the read calls in each Marten-owned query handler. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#retain-unaffected-baseline-persistence | [Retain Unaffected Baseline Persistence](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#retain-unaffected-baseline-persistence) | rule | guidance | Every aggregate path outside the recorded scope keeps its baseline persistence behavior. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#review-migration-effects | [Review Migration Effects](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#review-migration-effects) | rule | guidance | The migration review covers tables, columns, indexes, constraints, data movement, destructive operations, and rollback compatibility. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#review-new-persisted-states | [Review New Persisted States](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#review-new-persisted-states) | rule | guidance | The new state review covers migration, mixed-version behavior, rollback behavior, and an integration fixture. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#stage-aggregate-writes-in-repositories | [Stage Aggregate Writes in Repositories](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#stage-aggregate-writes-in-repositories) | rule | guidance | Which repositories stage changes outside their scoped `DbContext`? Look at the Infrastructure repository implementations. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#stage-ef-core-outbox-records-together | [Stage EF Core Outbox Records Together](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#stage-ef-core-outbox-records-together) | rule | guidance | Which commands stage their outbox record outside their aggregate `DbContext`? Look at the outbox staging calls in each command path. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#test-migration-starting-states | [Test Migration Starting States](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#test-migration-starting-states) | rule | guidance | Which migrations fail from an empty database or from the previous release database? Look at the migration test runs. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#use-direct-state-mapping-only-when-supported | [Use Direct State Mapping Only When Supported](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#use-direct-state-mapping-only-when-supported) | rule | guidance | Which state records fail to materialize, track, or round-trip under the pinned versions? Look at a round-trip read of each sealed state record. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#use-durable-outbox-delivery | [Use Durable Outbox Delivery](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#use-durable-outbox-delivery) | rule | guidance | Which durable EF Core commands commit a Marten session? Look at the session calls in each durable command path. |

| litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#use-snake-case-relational-names | [Use Snake-Case Relational Names](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#use-snake-case-relational-names) | convention | guidance | Which generated migrations carry a database name outside the verified snake-case form? | litespec/backend/dotnet/ext/efcore#use-the-ef-core-infrastructure-layout | [Use the EF Core Infrastructure Layout](../backend/dotnet/ext/efcore.md#use-the-ef-core-infrastructure-layout) | convention | guidance | EF Core Infrastructure paths follow the default layout, or the project documentation names another persistence layout. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/naming#group-event-handlers-by-the-fact-they-handle | [Group Event Handlers by the Fact They Handle](../backend/dotnet/naming.md#group-event-handlers-by-the-fact-they-handle) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS2007` for an event handler or scheduled automation name hiding its kind and `LS2008` for an event handler outside its fact folder. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/naming#keep-implementation-style-consistent | [Keep Implementation Style Consistent](../backend/dotnet/naming.md#keep-implementation-style-consistent) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS3004` for a concrete class with no derived type that is not sealed. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/naming#match-c-files-and-primary-types | [Match C# Files and Primary Types](../backend/dotnet/naming.md#match-c-files-and-primary-types) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS3003` for a C# file whose name differs from its primary type. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/naming#name-asynchronous-methods-completely | [Name Asynchronous Methods Completely](../backend/dotnet/naming.md#name-asynchronous-methods-completely) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS3001` for an async method name without `Async` and `LS3002` for a cancellation token that is misplaced or misnamed. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/naming#name-no-folder-or-type-for-the-absence-of-a-reason | [Name No Folder or Type for the Absence of a Reason](../backend/dotnet/naming.md#name-no-folder-or-type-for-the-absence-of-a-reason) | convention | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `name-reason-missing` (level S) for a folder or type named for the absence of a reason outside the declared shared kernel. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/naming#name-repository-variables-after-their-interface | [Name Repository Variables After Their Interface](../backend/dotnet/naming.md#name-repository-variables-after-their-interface) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS2010` for a repository variable not named after its interface. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/naming#name-result-parts-after-their-result | [Name Result Parts After Their Result](../backend/dotnet/naming.md#name-result-parts-after-their-result) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS2009` for a result part not named after its result. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/packages#keep-application-dependencies-narrow | [Keep Application Dependencies Narrow](../backend/dotnet/packages.md#keep-application-dependencies-narrow) | rule | guidance | The Application project file references only the permitted abstractions and the one read boundary. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/packages#keep-domain-package-free | [Keep Domain Package-Free](../backend/dotnet/packages.md#keep-domain-package-free) | rule | guidance | The Domain project file references no package beyond the base class library. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/packages#pin-every-dependency-centrally | [Pin Every Dependency Centrally](../backend/dotnet/packages.md#pin-every-dependency-centrally) | rule | guidance | Every project file names packages without versions; each version resolves through central management or the lockfile at the manifest or consumer pin. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/packages#raise-the-litebus-handler-analyzers-to-errors | [Raise the LiteBus Handler Analyzers to Errors](../backend/dotnet/packages.md#raise-the-litebus-handler-analyzers-to-errors) | convention | guidance | `.editorconfig` sets `LB1023` and `LB1024` to error and lists `litebus.LB1024.forbidden_types`. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/packages#reference-only-the-litebus-module-required | [Reference Only the LiteBus Module Required](../backend/dotnet/packages.md#reference-only-the-litebus-module-required) | convention | guidance | Each project file references only the LiteBus modules its code dispatches. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/packages#run-litespecanalyzers-in-every-application-project | [Run LiteSpec.Analyzers in Every Application Project](../backend/dotnet/packages.md#run-litespecanalyzers-in-every-application-project) | convention | guidance | Each of the five application project files references `LiteSpec.Analyzers` at the manifest pin with `PrivateAssets="all"`. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/packages#use-the-project-reference-graph | [Use the Project Reference Graph](../backend/dotnet/packages.md#use-the-project-reference-graph) | rule | guidance | Each project file's references match the permitted edges of the graph table, with no added edge. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/packages#use-this-baseline-package-ownership | [Use This Baseline Package Ownership](../backend/dotnet/packages.md#use-this-baseline-package-ownership) | convention | guidance | Each baseline package reference sits in the project the ownership table assigns. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/persistence#apply-optimistic-concurrency-to-every-aggregate-document | [Apply Optimistic Concurrency to Every Aggregate Document](../backend/dotnet/persistence.md#apply-optimistic-concurrency-to-every-aggregate-document) | rule | guidance | Each Marten aggregate document type enables optimistic concurrency, per type or through the document policy. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/persistence#claim-cross-record-keys-with-a-claims-document | [Claim Cross-Record Keys with a Claims Document](../backend/dotnet/persistence.md#claim-cross-record-keys-with-a-claims-document) | rule | guidance | Each cross-record key travels through a claims document written with `session.Insert`, with no `Store` call on the claims type. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/persistence#commit-once-in-the-command-pipeline | [Commit Once in the Command Pipeline](../backend/dotnet/persistence.md#commit-once-in-the-command-pipeline) | rule | guidance | One command post-handler file calls `SaveChangesAsync`; no handler, repository, validator, automation, or endpoint file calls it. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/persistence#enforce-uniqueness-with-marten-unique-indexes | [Enforce Uniqueness with Marten Unique Indexes](../backend/dotnet/persistence.md#enforce-uniqueness-with-marten-unique-indexes) | rule | guidance | Each Marten aggregate document with a uniqueness rule carries a unique index or an owner-derived identity in the store configuration. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/persistence#map-constraint-violations-with-the-postgresql-refusal-classifier | [Map Constraint Violations with the PostgreSQL Refusal Classifier](../backend/dotnet/persistence.md#map-constraint-violations-with-the-postgresql-refusal-classifier) | rule | guidance | The service registration pairs each unique index name with its conflict refusal code in the PostgreSQL refusal classifier. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/persistence#map-marten-concurrency-faults-to-conflict-refusals | [Map Marten Concurrency Faults to Conflict Refusals](../backend/dotnet/persistence.md#map-marten-concurrency-faults-to-conflict-refusals) | rule | guidance | A `ConcurrencyException` at commit reaches the caller as the use case conflict refusal through the registered classifier. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/persistence#query-through-iquerysession | [Query Through IQuerySession](../backend/dotnet/persistence.md#query-through-iquerysession) | rule | guidance | Query handler files project through `IQuerySession` with filter, scope, ordering, and limit applied before materialization. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/persistence#register-one-scoped-session | [Register One Scoped Session](../backend/dotnet/persistence.md#register-one-scoped-session) | convention | guidance | The service registration wires `IDocumentSession` once per WebApi request or Worker operation scope. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/persistence#stage-aggregate-writes-through-repositories | [Stage Aggregate Writes Through Repositories](../backend/dotnet/persistence.md#stage-aggregate-writes-through-repositories) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS2004` for a command handler writing through a document session or database context. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/persistence#use-this-infrastructure-layout | [Use This Infrastructure Layout](../backend/dotnet/persistence.md#use-this-infrastructure-layout) | convention | guidance | Marten configuration, repository, commit, and serialization files sit under the one Infrastructure persistence folder. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/stack#declare-dotnet-as-the-backend-stack | [Declare `dotnet` as the Backend Stack](../backend/dotnet/stack.md#declare-dotnet-as-the-backend-stack) | rule | guidance | The `stacks` array in `litespec.project.json`: `dotnet` listed when the backend runs on .NET. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/stack#declare-marked-events-and-exceptions-as-partial-types | [Declare Marked Events and Exceptions as Partial Types](../backend/dotnet/stack.md#declare-marked-events-and-exceptions-as-partial-types) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `partial-missing` (level S), and `dotnet build` reports `LS5006`, for a marked domain event or rule exception that is not `partial`, in every project that references the analyzer package. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/stack#generate-runtime-values-from-the-records | [Generate Runtime Values from the Records](../backend/dotnet/stack.md#generate-runtime-values-from-the-records) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `runtime-value-drift` (level S) for a hand-written value that differs from its record, and `dotnet build` reports `LS5005` for the same value. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/stack#generate-the-registry-permissions-and-roles-from-the-records | [Generate the Registry, Permissions, and Roles from the Records](../backend/dotnet/stack.md#generate-the-registry-permissions-and-roles-from-the-records) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS5009` for two records generating one constant name, `LS5010` for a permission whose first word is no known verb, `LS5011` for a message two marked handlers serve with different use cases, and `LS5012` for a role allowing a permission no record declares. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/stack#read-net-code-through-the-stack-reader | [Read .NET Code Through the Stack Reader](../backend/dotnet/stack.md#read-net-code-through-the-stack-reader) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports its level beside each level S finding read from C# code and test results. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/stack#write-each-mark-in-the-declared-style | [Write Each Mark in the Declared Style](../backend/dotnet/stack.md#write-each-mark-in-the-declared-style) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `mark-style-invalid` (level S), and `dotnet build` reports `LS5004`, for a mark written in the style the project did not declare. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/stack#write-test-results-the-model-builder-reads | [Write Test Results the Model Builder Reads](../backend/dotnet/stack.md#write-test-results-the-model-builder-reads) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports no scenario proved when no result file exists. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/testing#enforce-architecture-rules | [Enforce Architecture Rules](../backend/dotnet/testing.md#enforce-architecture-rules) | rule | guidance | Architecture test files assert the named structural boundaries, and the Release test pass runs them. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/testing#gate-changed-lines-with-mutation-testing | [Gate Changed Lines with Mutation Testing](../backend/dotnet/testing.md#gate-changed-lines-with-mutation-testing) | rule | guidance | Each pull request shows a Stryker.NET `--since` report with zero surviving mutants on its changed lines. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/testing#generate-model-tests-from-declared-transitions | [Generate Model Tests from Declared Transitions](../backend/dotnet/testing.md#generate-model-tests-from-declared-transitions) | rule | guidance | Each aggregate's model-based test runs on FsCheck against the aggregate's declared transitions. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/testing#keep-the-test-projects-the-size-requires | [Keep the Test Projects the Size Requires](../backend/dotnet/testing.md#keep-the-test-projects-the-size-requires) | rule | guidance | The solution tree under `apps/api/tests/` holds Architecture and Integration test projects, with Acceptance added when size is large. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/testing#prove-each-scenario-with-a-test-that-calls-its-use-case | [Prove Each Scenario with a Test That Calls Its Use Case](../backend/dotnet/testing.md#prove-each-scenario-with-a-test-that-calls-its-use-case) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS4001` for a test method with no assertion. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/testing#seed-property-tests-from-example-tables | [Seed Property Tests from Example Tables](../backend/dotnet/testing.md#seed-property-tests-from-example-tables) | rule | guidance | Each calculation rule's property tests run on FsCheck with cases drawn from the rule's scenario examples table. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/testing#test-http-integration-through-the-real-host | [Test HTTP Integration Through the Real Host](../backend/dotnet/testing.md#test-http-integration-through-the-real-host) | rule | guidance | HTTP integration test files run through `WebApplicationFactory` with the real configuration and cover Problem Details, authentication, and resource authorization. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/testing#use-one-production-faithful-integration-harness | [Use One Production-Faithful Integration Harness](../backend/dotnet/testing.md#use-one-production-faithful-integration-harness) | rule | guidance | The integration test project holds one container fixture, one `ApiFactory`, and one isolation helper. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/workspace#declare-size-and-stacks-in-project-config | [Declare Size and Stacks in Project Config](../backend/dotnet/workspace.md#declare-size-and-stacks-in-project-config) | rule | guidance | `litespec.project.json` declares `size` as `small` or `large` and one stack per frontend beside the `dotnet` backend. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/workspace#keep-net-production-and-test-projects-separate | [Keep .NET Production and Test Projects Separate](../backend/dotnet/workspace.md#keep-net-production-and-test-projects-separate) | rule | guidance | Production project files sit under `apps/api/src/`, test project files under `apps/api/tests/`, and the solution file directly under `apps/api/`. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/workspace#keep-one-workspace-solution-beside-the-api-solution | [Keep One Workspace Solution Beside the API Solution](../backend/dotnet/workspace.md#keep-one-workspace-solution-beside-the-api-solution) | rule | guidance | Any root solution file lists the .NET projects of more than one deployable, while builds, test runs, and configuration name the API solution. |
| litespec/backend/dotnet/workspace#place-a-workspace-apphost-beside-the-applications-it-runs | [Place a Workspace AppHost Beside the Applications It Runs](../backend/dotnet/workspace.md#place-a-workspace-apphost-beside-the-applications-it-runs) | rule | guidance | An AppHost running a frontend sits at `apps/apphost/`; an API-only AppHost sits under `apps/api/src/`. |
| litespec/backend/identifiers#cite-shared-provisions-in-their-published-form | [Cite Shared Provisions in Their Published Form](../backend/identifiers.md#cite-shared-provisions-in-their-published-form) | rule | checkable | `litespec-maintain validate` emits no `ID_UNKNOWN_REFERENCE` diagnostic. |
| litespec/backend/identifiers#follow-the-identifier-form-of-each-record-type | [Follow the Identifier Form of Each Record Type](../backend/identifiers.md#follow-the-identifier-form-of-each-record-type) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `identifier-invalid` for an identifier outside the form its type uses. |
| litespec/backend/identifiers#keep-aggregate-names-unique-across-the-project | [Keep Aggregate Names Unique Across the Project](../backend/identifiers.md#keep-aggregate-names-unique-across-the-project) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `aggregate-name-clash` for an aggregate name used twice. |
| litespec/backend/identifiers#keep-use-case-names-unique-across-the-project | [Keep Use Case Names Unique Across the Project](../backend/identifiers.md#keep-use-case-names-unique-across-the-project) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `use-case-name-clash` for a use-case name used twice. |
| litespec/backend/identifiers#keep-wire-codes-stable | [Keep Wire Codes Stable](../backend/identifiers.md#keep-wire-codes-stable) | rule | guidance | Wire codes in the contract keep the values deployed clients branch on. |
| litespec/backend/identifiers#name-records-with-one-to-six-words | [Name Records with One to Six Words](../backend/identifiers.md#name-records-with-one-to-six-words) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `identifier-invalid` for a name outside one to six lowercase hyphenated words. |
| litespec/backend/identifiers#resolve-every-identifier-citation | [Resolve Every Identifier Citation](../backend/identifiers.md#resolve-every-identifier-citation) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `reference-unknown` for a citation naming no written record. |
| litespec/backend/identifiers#use-every-identifier-once | [Use Every Identifier Once](../backend/identifiers.md#use-every-identifier-once) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `identifier-duplicate` for an identifier used twice. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#add-read-documents-for-query-shaped-data | [Add Read Documents for Query-Shaped Data](../backend/persistence.md#add-read-documents-for-query-shaped-data) | convention | guidance | Read documents record their consistency and rebuild behavior beside their owning use case. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#assign-document-numbers-from-a-sequence | [Assign Document Numbers from a Sequence](../backend/persistence.md#assign-document-numbers-from-a-sequence) | rule | guidance | Each legal document number allocates from a per-owner sequence taken in the command transaction. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#bound-aggregate-document-growth | [Bound Aggregate Document Growth](../backend/persistence.md#bound-aggregate-document-growth) | rule | guidance | Embedded collections in aggregate documents pair with an accepted business bound. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#claim-a-cross-record-key-at-commit | [Claim a Cross-Record Key at Commit](../backend/persistence.md#claim-a-cross-record-key-at-commit) | rule | guidance | Each cross-record uniqueness rule declares keys on its aggregate, claimed and released in the committing transaction. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#collect-events-without-a-public-unit-of-work | [Collect Events Without a Public Unit of Work](../backend/persistence.md#collect-events-without-a-public-unit-of-work) | rule | guidance | Repositories register touched aggregates with the internal buffer the post-handler drains. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#commit-each-command-pipeline-once | [Commit Each Command Pipeline Once](../backend/persistence.md#commit-each-command-pipeline-once) | rule | guidance | One command post-handler commits each pipeline, with no other committer in handlers, repositories, validators, automations, or endpoints. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#control-production-schema-changes | [Control Production Schema Changes](../backend/persistence.md#control-production-schema-changes) | rule | guidance | Hosted schema changes run as a reviewed step before traffic shifts, with no startup migration. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#enforce-uniqueness-in-the-store | [Enforce Uniqueness in the Store](../backend/persistence.md#enforce-uniqueness-in-the-store) | rule | guidance | Each uniqueness rule pairs with a store unique index or an owner-derived identity in the store configuration, with normalized values indexed on the normalized field. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#evolve-stored-document-contracts-explicitly | [Evolve Stored Document Contracts Explicitly](../backend/persistence.md#evolve-stored-document-contracts-explicitly) | rule | guidance | Stored-contract changes pair with a reviewed transformation or an expand-and-contract rollout. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#honor-the-declared-consistency-mode | [Honor the Declared Consistency Mode](../backend/persistence.md#honor-the-declared-consistency-mode) | rule | guidance | Each command commits as its record's `consistency` mode states: one unit of work, per record, or per step with compensation. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#keep-mappings-and-aliases-explicit | [Keep Mappings and Aliases Explicit](../backend/persistence.md#keep-mappings-and-aliases-explicit) | rule | guidance | Store configuration declares a stable alias, identifier mapping, and index list for each stored aggregate. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#keep-serialization-behavior-out-of-domain | [Keep Serialization Behavior Out of Domain](../backend/persistence.md#keep-serialization-behavior-out-of-domain) | rule | guidance | Domain types carry no serialization metadata, with the stored JSON contract owned in Infrastructure. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#keep-uniqueness-out-of-handlers | [Keep Uniqueness Out of Handlers](../backend/persistence.md#keep-uniqueness-out-of-handlers) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS2016` for a handler taking a pipeline-owned collaborator. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#map-a-constraint-violation-to-its-refusal | [Map a Constraint Violation to Its Refusal](../backend/persistence.md#map-a-constraint-violation-to-its-refusal) | rule | guidance | Each unique constraint name pairs with the conflict refusal of its violating use cases in the fault classification map. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#model-a-once-only-rule-as-aggregate-state | [Model a Once-Only Rule as Aggregate State](../backend/persistence.md#model-a-once-only-rule-as-aggregate-state) | rule | guidance | Each at-most-once rule lives as state on its aggregate, with a repeat ending in the refusal or without effect. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#persist-one-explicit-state-object | [Persist One Explicit State Object](../backend/persistence.md#persist-one-explicit-state-object) | convention | guidance | Aggregate documents persist the state object, with every state round-tripping through the serializer. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#query-through-the-read-session | [Query Through the Read Session](../backend/persistence.md#query-through-the-read-session) | rule | guidance | Query handler bodies project through the read session, with filter, scope, ordering, and limit applied first. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#read-collection-queries-in-the-store-with-paging-and-filters | [Read Collection Queries in the Store with Paging and Filters](../backend/persistence.md#read-collection-queries-in-the-store-with-paging-and-filters) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `query-filter-invalid` (level A) for a query handler filtering a repository list in memory. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#review-query-plans-for-new-indexes | [Review Query Plans for New Indexes](../backend/persistence.md#review-query-plans-for-new-indexes) | convention | guidance | New indexes record their accepted query and the plan that justified it. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#stage-aggregate-writes-through-domain-repositories | [Stage Aggregate Writes Through Domain Repositories](../backend/persistence.md#stage-aggregate-writes-through-domain-repositories) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `repository-unimplemented` (level S) for an aggregate a use case changes that has no Infrastructure repository implementing its domain repository. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#test-persistence-against-postgresql | [Test Persistence Against PostgreSQL](../backend/persistence.md#test-persistence-against-postgresql) | rule | guidance | Persistence tests run against the pinned PostgreSQL with no in-memory substitute. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#use-database-naming-conventions | [Use Database Naming Conventions](../backend/persistence.md#use-database-naming-conventions) | rule | guidance | Generated database identifiers use snake_case across schemas, tables, columns, and indexes. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#use-one-persistence-folder | [Use One Persistence Folder](../backend/persistence.md#use-one-persistence-folder) | convention | guidance | Store configuration, repositories, and commit behavior sit under the one persistence folder. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#use-one-session-per-command | [Use One Session per Command](../backend/persistence.md#use-one-session-per-command) | convention | guidance | The repositories and the commit handler in one command resolve one session. |
| litespec/backend/persistence#version-every-aggregate | [Version Every Aggregate](../backend/persistence.md#version-every-aggregate) | rule | guidance | Each stored aggregate carries a version the store compares at commit, with a stale write ending in the use case conflict refusal. |
| litespec/backend/testing#assert-architecture-boundaries-with-tests | [Assert Architecture Boundaries with Tests](../backend/testing.md#assert-architecture-boundaries-with-tests) | rule | guidance | Architecture test files assert project references, visibility, folders, and inheritance against the compiled code. |
| litespec/backend/testing#derive-expected-values-from-the-scenario | [Derive Expected Values from the Scenario](../backend/testing.md#derive-expected-values-from-the-scenario) | rule | guidance | Test assertions repeat the expected endings written in the Then of their scenarios. |
| litespec/backend/testing#isolate-integration-state | [Isolate Integration State](../backend/testing.md#isolate-integration-state) | rule | guidance | The testing documentation names one reset strategy, and integration tests share no mutable business state across runs. |
| litespec/backend/testing#keep-assertions-focused | [Keep Assertions Focused](../backend/testing.md#keep-assertions-focused) | convention | guidance | Test bodies assert their named outcome without whole-object snapshots. |
| litespec/backend/testing#keep-the-test-suites-the-size-requires | [Keep the Test Suites the Size Requires](../backend/testing.md#keep-the-test-suites-the-size-requires) | rule | guidance | The test folders hold architecture and integration suites, with acceptance added for large projects only. |
| litespec/backend/testing#mark-the-test-that-proves-each-scenario | [Mark the Test That Proves Each Scenario](../backend/testing.md#mark-the-test-that-proves-each-scenario) | rule | guidance | The book pairs each scenario with its proving test, the boundary call in its body, and its latest passing run. |
| litespec/backend/testing#match-tests-by-fully-qualified-name | [Match Tests by Fully Qualified Name](../backend/testing.md#match-tests-by-fully-qualified-name) | rule | guidance | Test result files record namespace, containing types, and method for each test, with no data-row arguments. |
| litespec/backend/testing#mirror-production-module-names | [Mirror Production Module Names](../backend/testing.md#mirror-production-module-names) | convention | guidance | Test trees list the same module folders as production, apart from the exempt harness files. |
| litespec/backend/testing#name-tests-by-observable-behavior | [Name Tests by Observable Behavior](../backend/testing.md#name-tests-by-observable-behavior) | convention | guidance | Test names state operation, condition, and expected result on classes named for production types. |
| litespec/backend/testing#prove-each-rule-through-its-scenarios | [Prove Each Rule Through Its Scenarios](../backend/testing.md#prove-each-rule-through-its-scenarios) | rule | guidance | Rule claims sit on tests beside a proved scenario claim for the same use case. |
| litespec/backend/testing#prove-every-aggregate-with-a-model-based-test | [Prove Every Aggregate with a Model-Based Test](../backend/testing.md#prove-every-aggregate-with-a-model-based-test) | rule | guidance | Aggregates pair with model-based test files walking their declared transition tables. |
| litespec/backend/testing#prove-every-calculation-with-property-tests | [Prove Every Calculation with Property Tests](../backend/testing.md#prove-every-calculation-with-property-tests) | rule | guidance | Calculation rules pair with property test files seeded from their scenario example rows. |
| litespec/backend/testing#refuse-surviving-mutants-on-changed-lines | [Refuse Surviving Mutants on Changed Lines](../backend/testing.md#refuse-surviving-mutants-on-changed-lines) | rule | guidance | The mutation report for each change shows zero survivors on its changed lines. |
| litespec/backend/testing#share-one-production-faithful-integration-harness | [Share One Production-Faithful Integration Harness](../backend/testing.md#share-one-production-faithful-integration-harness) | rule | guidance | The integration suite holds one container fixture, one host factory, and one isolation helper. |
| litespec/backend/testing#test-application-coordination | [Test Application Coordination](../backend/testing.md#test-application-coordination) | rule | guidance | Application test files substitute repositories, clocks, actor accessors, and ports, and assert loaded, called, staged, and returned values. |
| litespec/backend/testing#test-domain-in-isolation | [Test Domain in Isolation](../backend/testing.md#test-domain-in-isolation) | rule | guidance | Domain test files take explicit inputs, with no database, host, container, clock, or mock in their bodies. |
| litespec/backend/testing#test-http-integration-through-the-real-application-host | [Test HTTP Integration Through the Real Application Host](../backend/testing.md#test-http-integration-through-the-real-application-host) | rule | guidance | HTTP integration tests run through the real host factory with production configuration, covering Problem Details bodies, authentication, and authorization. |
| litespec/backend/testing#use-builders-for-valid-defaults | [Use Builders for Valid Defaults](../backend/testing.md#use-builders-for-valid-defaults) | convention | guidance | Test data builders produce valid state by default with business-named customization. |
| litespec/backend/testing#use-evidence-rather-than-one-coverage-target | [Use Evidence Rather Than One Coverage Target](../backend/testing.md#use-evidence-rather-than-one-coverage-target) | rule | guidance | No build gate names a coverage percentage, while line and branch numbers stay available for review. |

Look at the permission and entry point lists for H2 sections. |

| litespec/core/access#name-the-actor-for-every-external-system | [Name the Actor for Every External System](../core/access.md#name-the-actor-for-every-external-system) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `record-invalid` for an external system without `actor`. |

Look at the scenarios on the use cases that carry the pattern. |

Look at the assignments beside the actor holds and the use cases that act under them. |
| litespec/core/access#record-every-role-hold-with-its-scope | [Record Every Role Hold with Its Scope](../core/access.md#record-every-role-hold-with-its-scope) | rule | guidance | Which actor holds lack a role or a scope? Look at `holds` on each actor page. |

Look at the security queue in the Behavior diff. |

| litespec/core/access#scope-every-role | [Scope Every Role](../core/access.md#scope-every-role) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `record-invalid` for a role without `scope` and `field-invalid` for a scope outside kebab-case form. |
| litespec/core/access#state-every-permission-with-its-meaning | [State Every Permission with Its Meaning](../core/access.md#state-every-permission-with-its-meaning) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `record-invalid` for a permission without `meaning`. |

Look at `scope` on each role page. |
| litespec/core/access#write-a-summary-on-every-actor-and-role-page | [Write a Summary on Every Actor and Role Page](../core/access.md#write-a-summary-on-every-actor-and-role-page) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails an actor or role page without an H2 `Summary`. |
| litespec/core/access#write-a-summary-on-every-external-system-page | [Write a Summary on Every External System Page](../core/access.md#write-a-summary-on-every-external-system-page) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails an external system page without an H2 `Summary`. |
| litespec/core/agent#advise-without-gating | [Advise Without Gating](../core/agent.md#advise-without-gating) | rule | guidance | The agent output records findings as advice, with approval and review left to people. |
| litespec/core/agent#apply-guidance-precedence | [Apply Guidance Precedence](../core/agent.md#apply-guidance-precedence) | rule | guidance | The design applies the project exception, the extension, the deeper page, and the shallower page in that order. |
| litespec/core/agent#escalate-decisions-that-specify-forbids | [Escalate Decisions That Specify Forbids](../core/agent.md#escalate-decisions-that-specify-forbids) | rule | guidance | Each figure the decider sets that the change needs sits in a draft question, not in an agent-written decision. |
| litespec/core/agent#follow-the-local-pattern-before-the-model-query | [Follow the Local Pattern Before the Model Query](../core/agent.md#follow-the-local-pattern-before-the-model-query) | rule | guidance | The change touches no record carrying `risk` or an approval and names the local pattern it followed. |
| litespec/core/agent#inspect-existing-work-before-editing | [Inspect Existing Work Before Editing](../core/agent.md#inspect-existing-work-before-editing) | rule | guidance | `git status` output precedes the edits, with user changes, untracked files, and unrelated generated output untouched. |
| litespec/core/agent#inspect-local-examples-after-standards | [Inspect Local Examples After Standards](../core/agent.md#inspect-local-examples-after-standards) | convention | guidance | The change names the neighboring files that shaped it after the standards. |
| litespec/core/agent#keep-agent-working-files-under-agents | [Keep Agent Working Files Under Agents](../core/agent.md#keep-agent-working-files-under-agents) | convention | guidance | Every agent working file sits under `.agents/` or the path the working-files convention names. |
| litespec/core/agent#load-applicable-extensions | [Load Applicable Extensions](../core/agent.md#load-applicable-extensions) | rule | guidance | The change names each extension loaded before editing inside its boundary. |
| litespec/core/agent#make-coherent-scoped-changes | [Make Coherent Scoped Changes](../core/agent.md#make-coherent-scoped-changes) | rule | guidance | Every changed artifact traces to the authorized request. |
| litespec/core/agent#mark-transcribed-records-during-adoption | [Mark Transcribed Records During Adoption](../core/agent.md#mark-transcribed-records-during-adoption) | rule | guidance | Each record transcribed from code carries `provenance: transcribed` and stays in draft until its Intent parent is approved. |
| litespec/core/agent#raise-questions-in-ballots-with-recommendations | [Raise Questions in Ballots with Recommendations](../core/agent.md#raise-questions-in-ballots-with-recommendations) | rule | guidance | Each open question sits in a ballot with a recommendation. |
| litespec/core/agent#read-the-full-record-before-changing-a-boundary | [Read the Full Record Before Changing a Boundary](../core/agent.md#read-the-full-record-before-changing-a-boundary) | convention | guidance | Each file generation or boundary change cites the full record it matched. |
| litespec/core/agent#replace-only-declared-conventions | [Replace Only Declared Conventions](../core/agent.md#replace-only-declared-conventions) | rule | guidance | Each convention replacement resolves to a recorded local convention in the project documentation. |
