| litespec/core/layers#write-each-layer-from-the-layer-above-it | [Write Each Layer from the Layer Above It](../core/layers.md#write-each-layer-from-the-layer-above-it) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` emits no `behavior-unrecorded` finding for a refusal literal, a screen call to no endpoint, or an engine step mapping to no use case, and reports `mark-missing` for a code element that carries no mark. |
| litespec/core/maintaining#list-every-stack-shape-and-extension-in-the-manifest | [List Every Stack Shape and Extension in the Manifest](../core/maintaining.md#list-every-stack-shape-and-extension-in-the-manifest) | rule | checkable | `litespec-maintain validate` fails when a stack, shape, extension, or pin the release carries is missing from the manifest. |
| litespec/core/maintaining#mark-the-tools-with-the-provisions-they-follow | [Mark the Tools with the Provisions They Follow](../core/maintaining.md#mark-the-tools-with-the-provisions-they-follow) | rule | checkable | `litespec-maintain validate` emits `MARK_MISSING` for a class with a method and no `follows` mark in a source project under `apps/`, and `MARK_UNRESOLVED` for a `follows` or `proves` mark in a .NET project under `apps/`, outside its `Fixtures` folders, that names no provision in `docs/reference/provisions.md`. |
| litespec/core/maintaining#publish-a-litespec-release-from-a-tag | [Publish a LiteSpec Release from a Tag](../core/maintaining.md#publish-a-litespec-release-from-a-tag) | rule | checkable | `dotnet test --solution LiteSpec.slnx` fails `ReleaseVersionTests` when the changelog, the template, or either package version differs from the manifest version, and `.github/workflows/release.yml` refuses a tag that does not name the manifest version. |
| litespec/core/maintaining#record-the-reasoning-behind-each-release | [Record the Reasoning Behind Each Release](../core/maintaining.md#record-the-reasoning-behind-each-release) | rule | checkable | `litespec-maintain validate` emits no `RECORD_SCHEMA`, `RECORD_ID_MISMATCH`, `RECORD_SOURCE`, `RECORD_UNKNOWN_REFERENCE`, or `RECORD_DECISION_UNCITED` diagnostic. |
| litespec/core/maintaining#replace-every-citation-on-a-rename | [Replace Every Citation on a Rename](../core/maintaining.md#replace-every-citation-on-a-rename) | rule | checkable | `litespec-maintain validate` emits no `ID_UNKNOWN_REFERENCE` diagnostic. |
| litespec/core/maintaining#state-the-provision-lifecycle | [State the Provision Lifecycle](../core/maintaining.md#state-the-provision-lifecycle) | rule | guidance | Each provision sits in exactly one lifecycle state, with retired identifiers cited nowhere. |
| litespec/core/model#add-a-missing-fact-to-litespec-before-the-book-shows-it | [Add a Missing Fact to LiteSpec Before the Book Shows It](../core/model.md#add-a-missing-fact-to-litespec-before-the-book-shows-it) | rule | guidance | Which facts does the book show that no record field, computed status or check defines? Look at the book beside the model output and the finding codes. |
| litespec/core/model#answer-each-question-on-its-record | [Answer Each Question on Its Record](../core/model.md#answer-each-question-on-its-record) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `decider-missing` for a question missing its decider or date and `question-expired` for every unanswered question past its `decideBy` that cannot auto-approve. |
| litespec/core/model#apply-guidance-precedence | [Apply Guidance Precedence](../core/model.md#apply-guidance-precedence) | rule | guidance | Every departure from a shared provision resolves to an exception record under `docs/governance/exceptions/` with reason and expiry date. |
| litespec/core/model#approve-tier-1-records-by-a-person | [Approve Tier 1 Records by a Person](../core/model.md#approve-tier-1-records-by-a-person) | rule | guidance | Which tier 1 records lack a named personal approver? Look at `approvedBy` on records carrying risk. |
| litespec/core/model#author-one-slice-at-a-time | [Author One Slice at a Time](../core/model.md#author-one-slice-at-a-time) | rule | guidance | Approved Behavior records show approved Intent above them through their `fulfills` and `decides` links. |
| litespec/core/model#compute-proof-statuses-in-the-builder | [Compute Proof Statuses in the Builder](../core/model.md#compute-proof-statuses-in-the-builder) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `use-case-unimplemented`, `proves-unreached`, and `refusal-unimplemented` for a use case whose handler, proof, or refusal code is missing, and `proof-invalid` (level A) for a test that both passed and failed within the runs read. |
| litespec/core/model#connect-every-use-case-to-intent | [Connect Every Use Case to Intent](../core/model.md#connect-every-use-case-to-intent) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `record-link-missing` for a use case with neither `serves` nor `fulfills`, and `use-case-journey-missing` (level G) at large size for a use case that is not background-only and serves no journey step. |
| litespec/core/model#declare-one-mark-style | [Declare One Mark Style](../core/model.md#declare-one-mark-style) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `mark-style-invalid` (level S) for a mark written in the style the project did not declare. |
| litespec/core/model#declare-size-stacks-and-optional-types | [Declare Size Stacks and Optional Types](../core/model.md#declare-size-stacks-and-optional-types) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `size-type-missing` and `record-invalid` for a project missing its declaration or a required type. |
| litespec/core/model#derive-generic-refusals | [Derive Generic Refusals](../core/model.md#derive-generic-refusals) | rule | guidance | Which result records carry a generic refusal id? Look for ids ending in `.forbidden` or `.invalid-input` beside the use case pages. |
| litespec/core/model#end-transcription-at-the-named-decision | [End Transcription at the Named Decision](../core/model.md#end-transcription-at-the-named-decision) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `transcription-expired` for a transcribed record after the adoption decision is approved. |
| litespec/core/model#explain-the-book-from-litespec-definitions | [Explain the Book from LiteSpec Definitions](../core/model.md#explain-the-book-from-litespec-definitions) | rule | guidance | Which explanations in the book are not a glossary definition or a record type question? Look at the book's explanations beside the glossary and the record type table. |
| litespec/core/model#flag-every-record-whose-parent-changed | [Flag Every Record Whose Parent Changed](../core/model.md#flag-every-record-whose-parent-changed) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `parent-reopened` (level A while draft, level G once approved) for a record that cites, fulfills, or serves a changed record and is not yet updated. |
| litespec/core/model#follow-the-authority-order | [Follow the Authority Order](../core/model.md#follow-the-authority-order) | rule | guidance | Conflict decisions under `docs/intent/decisions/` name the higher record that won, in the order this page states. |
| litespec/core/model#give-every-record-one-parent | [Give Every Record One Parent](../core/model.md#give-every-record-one-parent) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `record-parent-missing` (level G) for a record whose required parent is missing. |
| litespec/core/model#give-every-use-case-its-results | [Give Every Use Case Its Results](../core/model.md#give-every-use-case-its-results) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `use-case-success-missing` and `scenario-result-unknown` for a use case missing its success or a scenario naming no result. |
| litespec/core/model#hold-approval-behind-draft-decisions | [Hold Approval Behind Draft Decisions](../core/model.md#hold-approval-behind-draft-decisions) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `approval-invalid` for an approved or reviewed record behind a draft decision. |
| litespec/core/model#hold-approval-behind-open-questions | [Hold Approval Behind Open Questions](../core/model.md#hold-approval-behind-open-questions) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `record-invalid` for an approved or reviewed record carrying an open question. |
| litespec/core/model#hold-each-finding-to-its-own-status | [Hold Each Finding to Its Own Status](../core/model.md#hold-each-finding-to-its-own-status) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` prints `draft` beside a non-blocking level S finding whose subject is itself a draft, shows an inherited draft as a label beside a finding about an approved or reviewed record, and exits zero when only non-blocking findings remain. |
| litespec/core/model#hold-transcribed-records-in-draft | [Hold Transcribed Records in Draft](../core/model.md#hold-transcribed-records-in-draft) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `transcription-status-invalid` for a transcribed record standing reviewed while its fulfills or serves parent is unapproved. |
| litespec/core/model#implement-each-mapped-behavior-record-behind-a-mark | [Implement Each Mapped Behavior Record Behind a Mark](../core/model.md#implement-each-mapped-behavior-record-behind-a-mark) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `aggregate-unimplemented`, `automation-unimplemented`, `external-system-unimplemented`, and `use-case-endpoint-missing` (level S) for a record no mark names, where `aggregate-unimplemented` fires only for an entity or saga aggregate, and `use-case-unreached` (level A) for a browser use case no screen calls. |
| litespec/core/model#keep-one-owner-and-status-on-every-record | [Keep One Owner and Status on Every Record](../core/model.md#keep-one-owner-and-status-on-every-record) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `owner-unknown` and `decider-missing` for a page missing its owner or draft decider. |
| litespec/core/model#keep-rules-and-targets-out-of-technical-decisions | [Keep Rules and Targets Out of Technical Decisions](../core/model.md#keep-rules-and-targets-out-of-technical-decisions) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `decision-basis-missing` (level A) for a technical decision whose `basedOn` names nothing, and counts a quality requirement as fulfilled when a technical decision names it in `basedOn`. |
| litespec/core/model#mark-every-check-with-its-level | [Mark Every Check with Its Level](../core/model.md#mark-every-check-with-its-level) | rule | guidance | Which findings lack a level letter, or block outside their level? Look at the output of `litespec model check`. |
| litespec/core/model#name-book-labels-with-litespec-terms | [Name Book Labels with LiteSpec Terms](../core/model.md#name-book-labels-with-litespec-terms) | rule | guidance | Which book labels are neither a record type, a field name, a status, a glossary term, nor the reads-as phrase the Links table states for a connection? Look at the book beside the glossary and the Links table. |
| litespec/core/model#name-the-lowest-record-each-test-proves | [Name the Lowest Record Each Test Proves](../core/model.md#name-the-lowest-record-each-test-proves) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `mark-missing` and `mark-unknown` (level S) for a test that names no record or a record of a kind a test does not take. |
| litespec/core/model#name-what-every-decision-decides | [Name What Every Decision Decides](../core/model.md#name-what-every-decision-decides) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `decision-decides-missing` for a product decision with an empty `decides` field. |
| litespec/core/model#organize-the-book-by-the-record-layers | [Organize the Book by the Record Layers](../core/model.md#organize-the-book-by-the-record-layers) | rule | guidance | Which book headings or groupings are not a record layer, a record type, or a record the identifier names? Look at the book's outline beside this page's record type table. |
| litespec/core/model#point-every-book-entry-to-its-source | [Point Every Book Entry to Its Source](../core/model.md#point-every-book-entry-to-its-source) | rule | guidance | Which book entries lack a link to their file and line? Look at the book's record, code, test and finding entries. |
| litespec/core/model#raise-modeling-questions-with-a-decider-and-a-date | [Raise Modeling Questions with a Decider and a Date](../core/model.md#raise-modeling-questions-with-a-decider-and-a-date) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `question-expired` (level A) for every unanswered question past its `decideBy` that cannot auto-approve. |
| litespec/core/model#record-a-project-wide-lowering-as-an-exception | [Record a Project-Wide Lowering as an Exception](../core/model.md#record-a-project-wide-lowering-as-an-exception) | rule | guidance | Which project-wide severity lowerings in `.editorconfig` lack an exception record? Look at every `.editorconfig` in the repository beside the exception records. |
| litespec/core/model#record-accepted-gaps-as-exceptions | [Record Accepted Gaps as Exceptions](../core/model.md#record-accepted-gaps-as-exceptions) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `record-invalid` for an exception missing its owner, date, or renewal, `exception-expired` (level G) past its `expiresOn` date, and `exception-finding-unknown` (level G) for an exception naming a finding code no check uses. |
| litespec/core/model#record-approval-as-data | [Record Approval as Data](../core/model.md#record-approval-as-data) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `approval-reopened` for a record whose semantic fields changed after approval. |
| litespec/core/model#record-each-technical-choice-as-a-decision | [Record Each Technical Choice as a Decision](../core/model.md#record-each-technical-choice-as-a-decision) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `decision-unimplemented` (level S) for a technical decision that no code element follows. The check runs in the CLI only, with no analyzer rule: whether any code follows a decision is known only across every project, and one compilation sees one project. |
| litespec/core/model#render-generated-regions-from-the-model | [Render Generated Regions from the Model](../core/model.md#render-generated-regions-from-the-model) | convention | guidance | Which generated regions render code-computed facts? Look inside the generated fences beside the book and the model output. |
| litespec/core/model#report-unverified-use-cases | [Report Unverified Use Cases](../core/model.md#report-unverified-use-cases) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `use-case-unverified` (level A) for a use case whose scenarios are not all proved, and `litespec model check --release` blocks on it. |
| litespec/core/model#resolve-every-page-link | [Resolve Every Page Link](../core/model.md#resolve-every-page-link) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `link-unknown` for a page link naming no file. |
| litespec/core/model#resolve-ties-by-the-later-decision | [Resolve Ties by the Later Decision](../core/model.md#resolve-ties-by-the-later-decision) | rule | guidance | Each tie-breaking decision carries a later approval date than the peer records it settles. |
| litespec/core/model#retire-records-with-replaces | [Retire Records with Replaces](../core/model.md#retire-records-with-replaces) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `field-invalid` for `replaces` outside a decision and `reference-unknown` for an unknown target. |
| litespec/core/model#serve-every-journey-step | [Serve Every Journey Step](../core/model.md#serve-every-journey-step) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `step-use-case-missing` for a journey step with no serving use case, beside `step-screen-missing` for a step a browser or device use case serves that no screen serves. |
| litespec/core/model#show-only-computed-judgements-in-the-book | [Show Only Computed Judgements in the Book](../core/model.md#show-only-computed-judgements-in-the-book) | rule | guidance | Which statuses, counts, orders or findings in the book does no builder output, check or review queue produce? Look at the book beside the model output. |
| litespec/core/model#state-the-common-fields-on-every-record | [State the Common Fields on Every Record](../core/model.md#state-the-common-fields-on-every-record) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `record-invalid` for a record missing a common field. |
| litespec/core/model#use-the-layer-status-vocabulary | [Use the Layer Status Vocabulary](../core/model.md#use-the-layer-status-vocabulary) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `field-invalid` for a status outside the record layer vocabulary. |
| litespec/core/model#write-every-record-in-its-home | [Write Every Record in Its Home](../core/model.md#write-every-record-in-its-home) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `record-invalid` for a record outside its home. |
| litespec/core/model#write-links-on-the-record-that-points | [Write Links on the Record That Points](../core/model.md#write-links-on-the-record-that-points) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `reference-unknown` for a link whose target no record carries. |
| litespec/core/principles#avoid-generic-boundary-names | [Avoid Generic Boundary Names](../core/principles.md#avoid-generic-boundary-names) | rule | guidance | Each changed boundary name carries the narrowest accurate business name, with no bare `bus`, `manager`, `helper`, `processor`, or `service`. |
| litespec/core/principles#classify-record-authority | [Classify Record Authority](../core/principles.md#classify-record-authority) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a record with a missing or unknown status, or a draft without `decider` and `decideBy` date. |
| litespec/core/principles#compute-nothing-above-implementation-from-code | [Compute Nothing Above Implementation from Code](../core/principles.md#compute-nothing-above-implementation-from-code) | rule | guidance | Which records above Implementation restate code instead of a decided promise? | litespec/core/principles#confine-atomic-consistency-to-synchronous-edges | [Confine Atomic Consistency to Synchronous Edges](../core/principles.md#confine-atomic-consistency-to-synchronous-edges) | rule | guidance | Which atomic use cases span an async edge? Look at the consistency and effects fields beside the changed use cases. |
| litespec/core/principles#cover-other-mechanisms-with-extensions | [Cover Other Mechanisms with Extensions](../core/principles.md#cover-other-mechanisms-with-extensions) | rule | guidance | Which mechanisms outside the favored set lack an extension? Look at the selected extensions beside the changed records. |
| litespec/core/principles#declare-every-edge | [Declare Every Edge](../core/principles.md#declare-every-edge) | rule | guidance | Which edges lack a declaration on their record? Look at the `effects` entries with `runs`, `sends`, `emits`, or `calls` beside the changed records. |
| litespec/core/principles#derive-records-only-from-records | [Derive Records Only from Records](../core/principles.md#derive-records-only-from-records) | rule | guidance | Which derived values lack an authored source record? Trace each derived value to the record it cites. |
| litespec/core/principles#end-every-effect-cycle-in-a-domain-event | [End Every Effect Cycle in a Domain Event](../core/principles.md#end-every-effect-cycle-in-a-domain-event) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports no `effect-loop-invalid` finding on the changed records. |
| litespec/core/principles#enter-facts-from-the-world-as-research-or-constraints | [Enter Facts from the World as Research or Constraints](../core/principles.md#enter-facts-from-the-world-as-research-or-constraints) | rule | guidance | Which outside facts lack a cited and dated source? | litespec/core/principles#follow-intent-where-it-has-spoken | [Follow Intent Where It Has Spoken](../core/principles.md#follow-intent-where-it-has-spoken) | rule | guidance | Which code disagrees with a stated promise? Compare the changed code with the Intent records it serves. |

| litespec/core/principles#locate-package-versions-in-the-manifest | [Locate Package Versions in the Manifest](../core/principles.md#locate-package-versions-in-the-manifest) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a project whose stack versions do not resolve from the manifest. |
| litespec/core/principles#locate-record-status-in-frontmatter | [Locate Record Status in Frontmatter](../core/principles.md#locate-record-status-in-frontmatter) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a record whose frontmatter is missing a required common field. |
| litespec/core/principles#map-each-engine-step-to-one-use-case | [Map Each Engine Step to One Use Case](../core/principles.md#map-each-engine-step-to-one-use-case) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports no `behavior-unrecorded` finding on engine steps without a use case. |
| litespec/core/principles#name-boundary-intent | [Name Boundary Intent](../core/principles.md#name-boundary-intent) | rule | guidance | Each changed boundary name states its business action and owner. |
| litespec/core/principles#prefer-direct-owned-dependencies | [Prefer Direct Owned Dependencies](../core/principles.md#prefer-direct-owned-dependencies) | convention | guidance | Each new abstraction names the boundary that justifies it, or the wrapping convention that permits it. |
| litespec/core/principles#prefer-local-code-until-reuse-is-real | [Prefer Local Code Until Reuse Is Real](../core/principles.md#prefer-local-code-until-reuse-is-real) | convention | guidance | Each new shared unit serves two real consumers under one accurate name, or the sharing convention permits it. |
| litespec/core/principles#prove-enforceable-boundaries-mechanically | [Prove Enforceable Boundaries Mechanically](../core/principles.md#prove-enforceable-boundaries-mechanically) | rule | guidance | Each changed enforceable boundary names a reference, test, or lint rule that proves it. |
| litespec/core/principles#prove-scenarios-with-tests | [Prove Scenarios with Tests](../core/principles.md#prove-scenarios-with-tests) | rule | guidance | Each scenario in the model links a passing test that calls its use case and asserts its result. |
| litespec/core/principles#raise-what-a-lower-layer-needs-as-a-question | [Raise What a Lower Layer Needs as a Question](../core/principles.md#raise-what-a-lower-layer-needs-as-a-question) | rule | guidance | Which needs from a lower record layer lack a draft question with a decider and a date? Look at the open questions beside the records that need them. |
| litespec/core/principles#record-stricter-edge-rules-as-conventions | [Record Stricter Edge Rules as Conventions](../core/principles.md#record-stricter-edge-rules-as-conventions) | rule | guidance | Which stricter edge rules lack a cited convention? Look at the project conventions beside the changed records. |
| litespec/core/principles#refuse-approval-behind-a-draft-decision | [Refuse Approval Behind a Draft Decision](../core/principles.md#refuse-approval-behind-a-draft-decision) | rule | guidance | Each decision behind an approved or reviewed record shows approved status in its own record. |
| litespec/core/principles#render-consequences-as-context-outside-approval | [Render Consequences as Context Outside Approval](../core/principles.md#render-consequences-as-context-outside-approval) | rule | guidance | Which ballots or book chapters lack a consequences summary marked as context? Look at the rendered book beside the changed Intent records. |
| litespec/core/principles#require-current-complexity-activation | [Require Current Complexity Activation](../core/principles.md#require-current-complexity-activation) | rule | guidance | Each added package, project, wrapper, process, cache, queue, or pattern cites a current use case, decision, or requirement. |
| litespec/core/principles#retire-public-behavior-deliberately | [Retire Public Behavior Deliberately](../core/principles.md#retire-public-behavior-deliberately) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a retired use case that still names an entry point. |
| litespec/core/principles#separate-authority-from-implementation | [Separate Authority from Implementation](../core/principles.md#separate-authority-from-implementation) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a record that writes its own implemented, verified, stale, reachable, or effective status. |
| litespec/core/principles#state-transaction-sharing-on-every-inline-edge | [State Transaction Sharing on Every Inline Edge](../core/principles.md#state-transaction-sharing-on-every-inline-edge) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports no `record-invalid` finding on the changed inline edges. |
| litespec/core/principles#take-every-name-from-the-intent-above-it | [Take Every Name from the Intent Above It](../core/principles.md#take-every-name-from-the-intent-above-it) | rule | guidance | Which act carries a different name in its use case, method, event, permission, route or screen label than the record above it states? | litespec/core/principles#treat-code-beyond-silent-intent-as-evidence-for-a-question | [Treat Code Beyond Silent Intent as Evidence for a Question](../core/principles.md#treat-code-beyond-silent-intent-as-evidence-for-a-question) | rule | guidance | Which working code outside Intent lacks a question carrying it? Look at the open questions beside the uncovered code. |
| litespec/core/principles#write-each-layer-from-the-layer-above | [Write Each Layer from the Layer Above](../core/principles.md#write-each-layer-from-the-layer-above) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports no `record-link-missing` finding on the changed records. |
| litespec/core/privacy#accept-every-retained-personal-data-class-in-intent | [Accept Every Retained Personal Data Class in Intent](../core/privacy.md#accept-every-retained-personal-data-class-in-intent) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `risk-acceptance-missing` for a pseudonymized, retained, or exempt personal or sensitive class with no Intent acceptance. |
| litespec/core/privacy#classify-every-aggregate-data-field | [Classify Every Aggregate Data Field](../core/privacy.md#classify-every-aggregate-data-field) | rule | guidance | Which aggregate data fields lack a meaning, a classification, or a data class? Look at the Data table on each aggregate page. |

| litespec/ext/devices#simulate-offline-uploads-before-applying | [Simulate Offline Uploads Before Applying](../ext/devices.md#simulate-offline-uploads-before-applying) | rule | guidance | Simulated uploads in the device tests return outcomes with no writes behind them. |
| litespec/ext/devices#state-client-rules-with-scenarios | [State Client Rules with Scenarios](../ext/devices.md#state-client-rules-with-scenarios) | rule | guidance | Each client rule in the protocol blocks points at scenarios that prove it. |
| litespec/ext/devices#version-protocols-by-date | [Version Protocols by Date](../ext/devices.md#version-protocols-by-date) | convention | guidance | Protocol versions read as calendar numbers, or the device documentation names the numbering scheme. |
| litespec/ext/devices#version-the-device-protocol | [Version the Device Protocol](../ext/devices.md#version-the-device-protocol) | rule | guidance | The protocol blocks state numbering, overlap, and the upgrade path devices follow. |

| litespec/ext/integrations#bound-transient-retries | [Bound Transient Retries](../ext/integrations.md#bound-transient-retries) | rule | guidance | Which external clients retry transient failures without bounded attempts and jitter? Look at the retry policies. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#call-only-declared-operations | [Call Only Declared Operations](../ext/integrations.md#call-only-declared-operations) | rule | guidance | Each calls effect names an operation the cited external system declares. |

Look at the integration test suite. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#define-business-action-ports | [Define Business-Action Ports](../ext/integrations.md#define-business-action-ports) | rule | guidance | Each Application port names a business action with no provider in its name or signature. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#exclude-provider-internals | [Exclude Provider Internals](../ext/integrations.md#exclude-provider-internals) | rule | guidance | Which API responses expose provider bodies, credentials, headers, exception types, or internal account identifiers? Look at the API responses. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#group-provider-code | [Group Provider Code](../ext/integrations.md#group-provider-code) | convention | guidance | Each provider's code sits under `Infrastructure/Integrations/{Provider}/`, or the project names another root in its documentation. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#isolate-provider-implementations | [Isolate Provider Implementations](../ext/integrations.md#isolate-provider-implementations) | rule | guidance | Which Application assemblies reference a provider SDK or transport type? Look at the Application project references. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#keep-ports-near-use-cases | [Keep Ports Near Use Cases](../ext/integrations.md#keep-ports-near-use-cases) | convention | guidance | Each business-action port sits with its Application use cases, or the project keeps its ports in one shared folder. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#keep-provider-components-together | [Keep Provider Components Together](../ext/integrations.md#keep-provider-components-together) | convention | guidance | Each provider folder holds its options, client, models, mappings, and registration, or the project names another grouping in its documentation. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#process-duplicate-messages-safely | [Process Duplicate Messages Safely](../ext/integrations.md#process-duplicate-messages-safely) | rule | guidance | Which inbound provider handlers leave more than one accepted effect for a duplicate event? Look at the duplicate-delivery paths. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#protect-non-idempotent-calls | [Protect Non-Idempotent Calls](../ext/integrations.md#protect-non-idempotent-calls) | rule | guidance | Which non-idempotent provider calls retry without provider idempotency and documented replay behavior? Look at the retry paths. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#record-each-provider-as-an-external-system | [Record Each Provider as an External System](../ext/integrations.md#record-each-provider-as-an-external-system) | rule | guidance | Each provider has an external system page in the Behavior systems home naming actor, operations, and erasure. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#reject-formula-leading-import-values | [Reject Formula-Leading Import Values](../ext/integrations.md#reject-formula-leading-import-values) | rule | guidance | Which CSV or spreadsheet imports keep a formula-leading value without rejection or neutralization? Look at the import boundaries. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#reject-per-request-http-clients | [Reject Per-Request HTTP Clients](../ext/integrations.md#reject-per-request-http-clients) | rule | guidance | Which HTTP integrations construct a new HTTP client per request? Look at the HTTP call sites. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#run-provider-calls-from-event-handlers-or-automations | [Run Provider Calls from Event Handlers or Automations](../ext/integrations.md#run-provider-calls-from-event-handlers-or-automations) | rule | guidance | Each provider call runs from an event handler or an automation, and each inline call carries an exception naming its compensation. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#simulate-integration-boundaries | [Simulate Integration Boundaries](../ext/integrations.md#simulate-integration-boundaries) | rule | guidance | Which integration tests call a live provider instead of WireMock.Net or a protocol-specific local endpoint? Look at the integration test suite. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#translate-provider-failure-outcomes | [Translate Provider Failure Outcomes](../ext/integrations.md#translate-provider-failure-outcomes) | rule | guidance | Which provider errors miss their mapping to project-owned results and stable diagnostics? Look at the integration failure mappings. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#trust-inbound-integration-events-by-declared-trust | [Trust Inbound Integration Events by Declared Trust](../ext/integrations.md#trust-inbound-integration-events-by-declared-trust) | rule | guidance | Each inbound integration event declares trigger-only or payload trust with its correlation. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#use-managed-http-clients | [Use Managed HTTP Clients](../ext/integrations.md#use-managed-http-clients) | rule | guidance | Which HTTP integrations skip `HttpClientFactory` or applicable service discovery? Look at the HTTP registrations. |
| litespec/ext/integrations#verify-inbound-provider-messages | [Verify Inbound Provider Messages](../ext/integrations.md#verify-inbound-provider-messages) | rule | guidance | Which inbound provider handlers skip signature, timestamp tolerance, replay protection, content type, size, schema, or event identity checks? Look at the inbound handlers. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#audit-manual-replay | [Audit Manual Replay](../ext/jobs.md#audit-manual-replay) | rule | guidance | Each manual replay adds an audited attempt under its original occurrence identity. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#bound-automation-duration | [Bound Automation Duration](../ext/jobs.md#bound-automation-duration) | rule | guidance | Each automation handler carries a duration bound, and the occurrence store shows completions inside it. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#bound-automation-retries | [Bound Automation Retries](../ext/jobs.md#bound-automation-retries) | rule | guidance | Each schedule declares bounded attempts with cancellation-aware backoff and a poison state. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#carry-the-schedule-on-the-automation-record | [Carry the Schedule on the Automation Record](../ext/jobs.md#carry-the-schedule-on-the-automation-record) | rule | guidance | Each scheduled automation record carries a structured schedule with its timezone. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#define-business-local-clock-behavior | [Define Business-Local Clock Behavior](../ext/jobs.md#define-business-local-clock-behavior) | rule | guidance | Each business-local schedule records its IANA zone with skipped and repeated local-time behavior. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#define-misfire-recovery | [Define Misfire Recovery](../ext/jobs.md#define-misfire-recovery) | rule | guidance | Each schedule states skipped, once-replayed, or interval-replayed behavior for missed occurrences. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#derive-stable-occurrence-identity | [Derive Stable Occurrence Identity](../ext/jobs.md#derive-stable-occurrence-identity) | rule | guidance | Occurrence identities in the store combine schedule ID with scheduled instant. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#dispatch-application-behavior | [Dispatch Application Behavior](../ext/jobs.md#dispatch-application-behavior) | rule | guidance | Each automation handler calls an Application Command or an approved Application port. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#exclude-direct-storage-and-http-execution | [Exclude Direct Storage and HTTP Execution](../ext/jobs.md#exclude-direct-storage-and-http-execution) | rule | guidance | Automation sources mutate no Marten session or DbContext and call no WebApi endpoint. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#exclude-machine-local-time | [Exclude Machine-Local Time](../ext/jobs.md#exclude-machine-local-time) | rule | guidance | Production automation sources read no machine-local clock. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#generate-monotonic-fencing-values | [Generate Monotonic Fencing Values](../ext/jobs.md#generate-monotonic-fencing-values) | rule | guidance | Fencing values in the store rise per occurrence, and a stale value refuses completion. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#observe-automation-cancellation | [Observe Automation Cancellation](../ext/jobs.md#observe-automation-cancellation) | rule | guidance | Each automation handler threads cancellation into its waits and stops its current step when cancellation fires. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#persist-occurrence-recovery-points | [Persist Occurrence Recovery Points](../ext/jobs.md#persist-occurrence-recovery-points) | rule | guidance | Each occurrence row carries last scheduled, started, and completed times. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#persist-occurrence-state | [Persist Occurrence State](../ext/jobs.md#persist-occurrence-state) | rule | guidance | Each occurrence row carries scheduled, processing, completed, or dead-letter state with safe execution details. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#place-handlers-with-owned-behavior | [Place Handlers with Owned Behavior](../ext/jobs.md#place-handlers-with-owned-behavior) | convention | guidance | Each automation handler sits under the module that owns its behavior, or the project keeps its handlers in one shared automations folder. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#prefer-utc-schedules | [Prefer UTC Schedules](../ext/jobs.md#prefer-utc-schedules) | rule | guidance | Each schedule states UTC or names a business-local time requirement with its IANA time zone. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#prevent-duplicate-completion | [Prevent Duplicate Completion](../ext/jobs.md#prevent-duplicate-completion) | rule | guidance | The lease admits one completion per occurrence across overlapping replicas. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#provide-operational-recovery-procedures | [Provide Operational Recovery Procedures](../ext/jobs.md#provide-operational-recovery-procedures) | rule | guidance | Runbook records cover inspection, manual replay, and disable for each schedule. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#remove-occurrences-past-retention | [Remove Occurrences Past Retention](../ext/jobs.md#remove-occurrences-past-retention) | rule | guidance | The store holds no occurrence older than its schedule's retention setting record. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#renew-only-owned-leases | [Renew Only Owned Leases](../ext/jobs.md#renew-only-owned-leases) | rule | guidance | Replica logs show no renewal after a failed renewal, and the loser stops its occurrence. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#restrict-completion-by-fencing-value | [Restrict Completion by Fencing Value](../ext/jobs.md#restrict-completion-by-fencing-value) | rule | guidance | Completion writes fail on a stale fencing value in the store. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#resume-driven-work-through-the-cursor | [Resume Driven Work Through the Cursor](../ext/jobs.md#resume-driven-work-through-the-cursor) | rule | guidance | Each automation driving a saga names its resume position in its cursor, with a scenario covering the remainder after a halfway failure. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#run-manual-automations-under-their-guard | [Run Manual Automations Under Their Guard](../ext/jobs.md#run-manual-automations-under-their-guard) | rule | guidance | Each manual automation run stays inside its runsWhere under its guard. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#run-one-command-per-item-in-selection | [Run One Command per Item in Selection](../ext/jobs.md#run-one-command-per-item-in-selection) | rule | guidance | Each automation run runs one command per item in its selection within its batch size, each in its own unit of work. |

| litespec/ext/jobs#separate-schedule-definitions | [Separate Schedule Definitions](../ext/jobs.md#separate-schedule-definitions) | convention | guidance | Schedule definitions sit apart from Command behavior, or the project keeps them beside it. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#stop-automations-during-graceful-shutdown | [Stop Automations During Graceful Shutdown](../ext/jobs.md#stop-automations-during-graceful-shutdown) | rule | guidance | The Worker shutdown path stops schedule polling before it drains running occurrences. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#store-execution-leases | [Store Execution Leases](../ext/jobs.md#store-execution-leases) | rule | guidance | The lease rows in PostgreSQL carry owner, expiry, and fencing value for each occurrence. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#test-time-transitions | [Test Time Transitions](../ext/jobs.md#test-time-transitions) | rule | guidance | Automation tests inject the clock and cover representative time-zone transitions. |

| litespec/ext/lifecycle#audit-lifecycle-changes | [Audit Lifecycle Changes](../ext/lifecycle.md#audit-lifecycle-changes) | rule | guidance | Each lifecycle operation writes an audit record, or a standalone record where audit is not selected. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#authorize-lifecycle-access-paths | [Authorize Lifecycle Access Paths](../ext/lifecycle.md#authorize-lifecycle-access-paths) | rule | guidance | Administrative and restore queries run through named paths with explicit authorization. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#authorize-lifecycle-changes | [Authorize Lifecycle Changes](../ext/lifecycle.md#authorize-lifecycle-changes) | rule | guidance | Each lifecycle operation checks its documented authorization before changing the record. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#bound-purge-and-archive-work | [Bound Purge and Archive Work](../ext/lifecycle.md#bound-purge-and-archive-work) | rule | guidance | Purge and archive work resumes after interruption within declared bounds, with visible progress. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#default-to-hard-delete | [Default to Hard Delete](../ext/lifecycle.md#default-to-hard-delete) | rule | guidance | Deletions with no retention or restore requirement remove the row in the delete transaction. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#define-post-deletion-references | [Define Post-Deletion References](../ext/lifecycle.md#define-post-deletion-references) | rule | guidance | The specification states post-deletion behavior for references, exports, and unique constraints. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#define-relational-lifecycle-behavior | [Define Relational Lifecycle Behavior](../ext/lifecycle.md#define-relational-lifecycle-behavior) | rule | guidance | The relational implementation states columns, query filters, partial indexes, migrations, and named filter behavior. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#define-storage-lifecycle-behavior | [Define Storage Lifecycle Behavior](../ext/lifecycle.md#define-storage-lifecycle-behavior) | rule | guidance | The document implementation states document state, filtered queries, indexes, purge transformations, and contract evolution. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#exclude-inactive-records-from-normal-reads | [Exclude Inactive Records from Normal Reads](../ext/lifecycle.md#exclude-inactive-records-from-normal-reads) | rule | guidance | Normal queries read only the active state, with deleted and archived records absent. |

| litespec/ext/lifecycle#model-retained-lifecycle-states | [Model Retained Lifecycle States](../ext/lifecycle.md#model-retained-lifecycle-states) | rule | guidance | Each retained record carries a business reason with a named state transition. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#name-soft-delete-timestamps | [Name Soft-Delete Timestamps](../ext/lifecycle.md#name-soft-delete-timestamps) | convention | guidance | Soft-delete timestamps use `deleted_at_utc`, or the project names another column in its documentation. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#preserve-legal-hold-data | [Preserve Legal-Hold Data](../ext/lifecycle.md#preserve-legal-hold-data) | rule | guidance | Retention runs leave held data in place past its retention date. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#record-lifecycle-scope | [Record Lifecycle Scope](../ext/lifecycle.md#record-lifecycle-scope) | rule | guidance | Each lifecycle record names classification, retention, deletion trigger, purge owner, hold, export, restore, backup, owner, and status. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#record-retention-progress | [Record Retention Progress](../ext/lifecycle.md#record-retention-progress) | rule | guidance | Retention runs persist progress that the next run resumes from. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#record-the-release-of-a-legal-hold | [Record the Release of a Legal Hold](../ext/lifecycle.md#record-the-release-of-a-legal-hold) | rule | guidance | Each legal-hold release records actor, data covered, reason, and resumed retention. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#reject-hidden-lifecycle-flags | [Reject Hidden Lifecycle Flags](../ext/lifecycle.md#reject-hidden-lifecycle-flags) | rule | guidance | Each lifecycle transition runs through explicit behavior rather than a storage-only flag. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#separate-query-shaped-history | [Separate Query-Shaped History](../ext/lifecycle.md#separate-query-shaped-history) | convention | guidance | Query-shaped history lives in a read model, or the project keeps it on the aggregate. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#test-provider-lifecycle-paths | [Test Provider Lifecycle Paths](../ext/lifecycle.md#test-provider-lifecycle-paths) | rule | guidance | Tests read through normal and lifecycle query paths on the selected provider. |
| litespec/ext/locale#avoid-catalog-only-locale-claims | [Avoid Catalog-Only Locale Claims](../ext/locale.md#avoid-catalog-only-locale-claims) | rule | guidance | Shipped locales come from the locale decision, with catalog files as translations only. |
| litespec/ext/locale#avoid-duplicate-localized-urls | [Avoid Duplicate Localized URLs](../ext/locale.md#avoid-duplicate-localized-urls) | rule | guidance | Each localized content item serves from one indexable URL. |
| litespec/ext/locale#complete-or-fall-back-catalog-values | [Complete or Fall Back Catalog Values](../ext/locale.md#complete-or-fall-back-catalog-values) | rule | guidance | Each locale holds its required keys or resolves them through its declared fallback. |
| litespec/ext/locale#define-one-locale-route-shape | [Define One Locale Route Shape](../ext/locale.md#define-one-locale-route-shape) | rule | guidance | The application routes every locale through one documented route shape. |
| litespec/ext/locale#follow-the-validation-message-grammar | [Follow the Validation Message Grammar](../ext/locale.md#follow-the-validation-message-grammar) | rule | guidance | Each validation message states field label, fault, and accepted input without technical wording. |
| litespec/ext/locale#format-values-with-active-locale | [Format Values with Active Locale](../ext/locale.md#format-values-with-active-locale) | rule | guidance | Dates, numbers, currency, plurals, lists, and relative time render through active-locale formatters. |
| litespec/ext/locale#handle-unavailable-locale-segments | [Handle Unavailable Locale Segments](../ext/locale.md#handle-unavailable-locale-segments) | rule | guidance | Unsupported or missing locale segments redirect under the documented product policy. |
| litespec/ext/locale#keep-locale-selection-explicit | [Keep Locale Selection Explicit](../ext/locale.md#keep-locale-selection-explicit) | convention | guidance | The URL or a documented session preference carries the locale, or the project keeps it in ambient request state. |

| litespec/ext/locale#localize-public-presentation-content | [Localize Public Presentation Content](../ext/locale.md#localize-public-presentation-content) | rule | guidance | Public metadata, form labels, validation messages, and user-safe errors render in the active locale. |
| litespec/ext/locale#name-messages-by-meaning | [Name Messages by Meaning](../ext/locale.md#name-messages-by-meaning) | rule | guidance | Catalog keys use semantic names citing glossary terms, with no source-language sentences as keys. |
| litespec/ext/locale#preserve-locale-neutral-business-data | [Preserve Locale-Neutral Business Data](../ext/locale.md#preserve-locale-neutral-business-data) | rule | guidance | Domain values and API contracts stay locale-neutral outside explicitly localized exchanges. |
| litespec/ext/locale#record-supported-locale-behavior | [Record Supported Locale Behavior](../ext/locale.md#record-supported-locale-behavior) | rule | guidance | The locale decision lists supported locales, default locale, fallback chain, user selection, browser detection, owner, and status. |
| litespec/ext/locale#resolve-every-shown-identifier | [Resolve Every Shown Identifier](../ext/locale.md#resolve-every-shown-identifier) | rule | guidance | Shown codes render glossary labels, with raw codes absent except user-facing reference numbers. |

| litespec/ext/locale#use-bcp-47-identifiers | [Use BCP 47 Identifiers](../ext/locale.md#use-bcp-47-identifiers) | convention | guidance | Locale identifiers follow BCP 47, or the project names another identifier form in its documentation. |
| litespec/ext/locale#use-one-catalog-root | [Use One Catalog Root](../ext/locale.md#use-one-catalog-root) | convention | guidance | Catalogs sit under one application-owned root, or the project names another root in its documentation. |
| litespec/ext/mcp#confirm-every-writing-tool | [Confirm Every Writing Tool](../ext/mcp.md#confirm-every-writing-tool) | rule | guidance | Each writing tool refuses until the person confirms the write. |

| litespec/ext/mcp#pass-server-instructions-at-initialize | [Pass Server Instructions at Initialize](../ext/mcp.md#pass-server-instructions-at-initialize) | rule | guidance | The initialize response carries a non-empty `instructions` value stating transport, audience, write surface, and refusal behavior. |

| litespec/ext/mcp#state-the-distribution-and-update-path | [State the Distribution and Update Path](../ext/mcp.md#state-the-distribution-and-update-path) | rule | guidance | The server documentation states install and update steps. |

| litespec/ext/mcp#write-one-tool-description-for-one-decision | [Write One Tool Description for One Decision](../ext/mcp.md#write-one-tool-description-for-one-decision) | rule | guidance | The declared tools directory shows imperative titles, declarative behavior, positive parameter polarity, and stated side effects. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#accept-repeated-delivery | [Accept Repeated Delivery](../ext/outbox.md#accept-repeated-delivery) | rule | guidance | Each event handler keeps correct state when the same message arrives twice. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#audit-outbox-replay | [Audit Outbox Replay](../ext/outbox.md#audit-outbox-replay) | rule | guidance | Each replay records actor, reason, time, and failure without editing the payload. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#avoid-automatic-durable-bus-adoption | [Avoid Automatic Durable-Bus Adoption](../ext/outbox.md#avoid-automatic-durable-bus-adoption) | convention | guidance | Dispatch runs on project-owned Marten records, or the project names a pinned durable bus package as its transport in its documentation. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#await-target-acceptance | [Await Target Acceptance](../ext/outbox.md#await-target-acceptance) | rule | guidance | Completion records follow target acceptance, with no early completion. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#back-off-unavailable-store-polling | [Back off Unavailable-Store Polling](../ext/outbox.md#back-off-unavailable-store-polling) | rule | guidance | Polling during a store outage backs off with rate-limited logging. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#bound-delivery-retries | [Bound Delivery Retries](../ext/outbox.md#bound-delivery-retries) | rule | guidance | Retries run bounded exponential backoff with jitter. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#classify-message-kinds | [Classify Message Kinds](../ext/outbox.md#classify-message-kinds) | rule | guidance | Each outbox record names integration event, journey-step command, or provider side effect, with integration events citing their integration-event record. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#classify-permanent-delivery-failure | [Classify Permanent Delivery Failure](../ext/outbox.md#classify-permanent-delivery-failure) | rule | guidance | Permanent failures stop at the retry bound with safe investigation and replay data retained. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#declare-published-facts-as-integration-events | [Declare Published Facts as Integration Events](../ext/outbox.md#declare-published-facts-as-integration-events) | rule | guidance | Each emits effect names an integration-event record carrying direction, payload, and trust. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#define-claim-ownership | [Define Claim Ownership](../ext/outbox.md#define-claim-ownership) | rule | guidance | Each claim takes owner, expiry, and fencing value in a short transaction. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#deliver-each-event-to-each-handler-separately | [Deliver Each Event to Each Handler Separately](../ext/outbox.md#deliver-each-event-to-each-handler-separately) | rule | guidance | Each event reaches each of its handlers as a separate delivery with its own retries and dead letter. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#deploy-readers-before-writers | [Deploy Readers Before Writers](../ext/outbox.md#deploy-readers-before-writers) | rule | guidance | Releases deploy compatible readers before additive writers. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#dispatch-claimed-records-in-worker | [Dispatch Claimed Records in Worker](../ext/outbox.md#dispatch-claimed-records-in-worker) | rule | guidance | The Worker claims pending records, dispatches bounded batches, and records each outcome. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#dispatch-journey-step-commands-in-a-new-scope | [Dispatch Journey-Step Commands in a New Scope](../ext/outbox.md#dispatch-journey-step-commands-in-a-new-scope) | convention | guidance | Journey-step commands run through `ICommandMediator` in a fresh Worker scope, or the project runs them inline in the dispatcher scope. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#distinguish-dependency-outage | [Distinguish Dependency Outage](../ext/outbox.md#distinguish-dependency-outage) | rule | guidance | Store availability reads as a gauge while claimed-message outcomes read as a counter. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#emit-excluded-automations-after-commit | [Emit Excluded Automations After Commit](../ext/outbox.md#emit-excluded-automations-after-commit) | rule | guidance | Excluded automations emit after commit success with no staged outbox record. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#exclude-manually-repeatable-automations | [Exclude Manually Repeatable Automations](../ext/outbox.md#exclude-manually-repeatable-automations) | rule | guidance | Automations whose loss the operators accept and can repeat manually carry no outbox records. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#exclude-request-transaction-dispatch | [Exclude Request-Transaction Dispatch](../ext/outbox.md#exclude-request-transaction-dispatch) | rule | guidance | Request paths commit eligibility only, with dispatch living in the Worker. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#gate-dispatch-on-readiness | [Gate Dispatch on Readiness](../ext/outbox.md#gate-dispatch-on-readiness) | rule | guidance | Unready hosts stop dispatch and dead-store polling. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#group-outbox-infrastructure | [Group Outbox Infrastructure](../ext/outbox.md#group-outbox-infrastructure) | convention | guidance | Outbox infrastructure sits under `Infrastructure/Messaging/Outbox/`, or the project names another home in its documentation. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#identify-repeated-delivery | [Identify Repeated Delivery](../ext/outbox.md#identify-repeated-delivery) | rule | guidance | Each event handler checks message ID or business key at its Command or side-effect boundary. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#isolate-the-worker-host | [Isolate the Worker Host](../ext/outbox.md#isolate-the-worker-host) | convention | guidance | The outbox Worker host lives in its own project, or the project hosts dispatch inside its request host. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#keep-provider-mapping-with-integration | [Keep Provider Mapping with Integration](../ext/outbox.md#keep-provider-mapping-with-integration) | convention | guidance | Provider mapping sits in the owning integration, or the project keeps it in the shared dispatch path. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#model-outbox-lifecycle-states | [Model Outbox Lifecycle States](../ext/outbox.md#model-outbox-lifecycle-states) | rule | guidance | Outbox lifecycle states form sealed records over an abstract base with documented transitions. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#plan-undispatched-rollback-records | [Plan Undispatched Rollback Records](../ext/outbox.md#plan-undispatched-rollback-records) | rule | guidance | The rollback Runbook covers records the new writer staged but nobody dispatched. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#preserve-message-identity-during-retry | [Preserve Message Identity During Retry](../ext/outbox.md#preserve-message-identity-during-retry) | rule | guidance | Message ID stays identical across retry and replay. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#protect-active-worker-compatibility | [Protect Active Worker Compatibility](../ext/outbox.md#protect-active-worker-compatibility) | rule | guidance | Every active Worker reads the type and schema version of new messages. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#publish-backlog-indicators | [Publish Backlog Indicators](../ext/outbox.md#publish-backlog-indicators) | rule | guidance | The deployment publishes pending count, failed count, oldest pending age, attempts, dispatch duration, and success rate. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#record-durable-delivery-behavior | [Record Durable Delivery Behavior](../ext/outbox.md#record-durable-delivery-behavior) | rule | guidance | Each delivery specification documents guarantee, downstream boundary, duplicate behavior, retry horizon, recovery owner, record owner, and status. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#reject-independent-outbox-commits | [Reject Independent Outbox Commits](../ext/outbox.md#reject-independent-outbox-commits) | rule | guidance | Writer sources stage through the business session with no separate connection or commit. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#release-claim-transactions-before-calls | [Release Claim Transactions Before Calls](../ext/outbox.md#release-claim-transactions-before-calls) | rule | guidance | Claim transactions close before the Worker makes network calls. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#requeue-expired-claims | [Requeue Expired Claims](../ext/outbox.md#requeue-expired-claims) | rule | guidance | Expired claims return to eligible work for a live Worker. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#restrict-completion-to-current-lease-owners | [Restrict Completion to Current Lease Owners](../ext/outbox.md#restrict-completion-to-current-lease-owners) | rule | guidance | Record updates succeed only under the current lease and fencing value. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#restrict-message-retry-to-claimed-records | [Restrict Message Retry to Claimed Records](../ext/outbox.md#restrict-message-retry-to-claimed-records) | rule | guidance | Retry and poison handling touch claimed records only. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#serialize-durable-messages | [Serialize Durable Messages](../ext/outbox.md#serialize-durable-messages) | rule | guidance | Each pending durable message lands serialized in an outbox record. |

| litespec/ext/outbox#stage-after-commit-effects-in-the-outbox | [Stage After-Commit Effects in the Outbox](../ext/outbox.md#stage-after-commit-effects-in-the-outbox) | rule | guidance | Each after-commit emits and runs effect stages in the outbox in the same transaction as its changes, with each domain event staged in the same commit as its aggregate. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#stage-messages-with-business-work | [Stage Messages with Business Work](../ext/outbox.md#stage-messages-with-business-work) | rule | guidance | Pending durable messages stage in the same scoped session as their aggregate changes. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#store-failure-and-completion-evidence | [Store Failure and Completion Evidence](../ext/outbox.md#store-failure-and-completion-evidence) | rule | guidance | Each outbox record retains its last error summary and completion time. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#store-required-message-fields | [Store Required Message Fields](../ext/outbox.md#store-required-message-fields) | rule | guidance | Each outbox record carries identity, kind, type, schema version, time, payload, tenant, state, attempt, and next-attempt data. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#support-coexisting-message-producers | [Support Coexisting Message Producers](../ext/outbox.md#support-coexisting-message-producers) | rule | guidance | The dispatcher reads records from every coexisting application version. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#version-message-types | [Version Message Types](../ext/outbox.md#version-message-types) | rule | guidance | Each message carries a stable type name with an explicit schema version. |

| litespec/ext/report#support-report-cancellation | [Support Report Cancellation](../ext/report.md#support-report-cancellation) | rule | guidance | Report work cancels on request and expired output clears in the cancellation tests. |

| litespec/ext/site#declare-each-static-site-in-the-project-record | [Declare Each Static Site in the Project Record](../ext/site.md#declare-each-static-site-in-the-project-record) | rule | guidance | Each static site the project ships appears as a `sites` entry in `litespec.project.json` with its name, path, and generator. |
| litespec/ext/site#default-to-a-light-scheme-with-a-reader-chosen-dark-scheme | [Default to a Light Scheme with a Reader-Chosen Dark Scheme](../ext/site.md#default-to-a-light-scheme-with-a-reader-chosen-dark-scheme) | convention | guidance | Light is the initial scheme with a reader-chosen dark scheme, or the site-scheme convention names other schemes. |
| litespec/ext/site#generate-a-sitemap-covering-every-page | [Generate a Sitemap Covering Every Page](../ext/site.md#generate-a-sitemap-covering-every-page) | rule | guidance | The generated sitemap lists every page with its language alternates. |
| litespec/ext/site#generate-the-site-with-astro | [Generate the Site with Astro](../ext/site.md#generate-the-site-with-astro) | convention | guidance | Astro generates the site with prerendered static output, or the site-generator convention names another generator. |
| litespec/ext/site#give-every-page-one-main-heading-and-landmark-elements | [Give Every Page One Main Heading and Landmark Elements](../ext/site.md#give-every-page-one-main-heading-and-landmark-elements) | rule | guidance | Each built page carries one top-level heading with header, navigation, main, and footer landmarks. |
| litespec/ext/site#keep-each-repeated-fact-in-one-data-source | [Keep Each Repeated Fact in One Data Source](../ext/site.md#keep-each-repeated-fact-in-one-data-source) | rule | guidance | Each repeated fact lives in one data source, with each product word as a term entry. |
| litespec/ext/site#list-every-page-for-language-models | [List Every Page for Language Models](../ext/site.md#list-every-page-for-language-models) | rule | guidance | The generated page list names every page with its address and description. |
| litespec/ext/site#load-every-script-from-the-site-origin | [Load Every Script from the Site Origin](../ext/site.md#load-every-script-from-the-site-origin) | rule | guidance | Every client script tag in the built pages points at the site origin. |
| litespec/ext/site#load-nothing-from-a-third-party-origin | [Load Nothing from a Third-Party Origin](../ext/site.md#load-nothing-from-a-third-party-origin) | rule | guidance | No built page references a font, script, style, image, or analytics address outside the site origin. |
| litespec/ext/site#mark-pages-with-json-ld-structured-data | [Mark Pages with JSON-LD Structured Data](../ext/site.md#mark-pages-with-json-ld-structured-data) | rule | guidance | Each built page carries its JSON-LD graph for the organization, the site, and the page. |
| litespec/ext/site#mark-unfinished-facts-with-visible-placeholders | [Mark Unfinished Facts with Visible Placeholders](../ext/site.md#mark-unfinished-facts-with-visible-placeholders) | rule | guidance | Each missing fact shows a visible placeholder naming it on its page. |
| litespec/ext/site#name-every-page-with-a-title-and-a-description | [Name Every Page with a Title and a Description](../ext/site.md#name-every-page-with-a-title-and-a-description) | rule | guidance | Each built page states its own title element and description. |
| litespec/ext/site#point-crawler-rules-at-the-sitemap | [Point Crawler Rules at the Sitemap](../ext/site.md#point-crawler-rules-at-the-sitemap) | rule | guidance | The `robots.txt` at the site root points at the sitemap address. |
| litespec/ext/site#point-every-page-at-its-absolute-canonical-address | [Point Every Page at Its Absolute Canonical Address](../ext/site.md#point-every-page-at-its-absolute-canonical-address) | rule | guidance | Each built page links its absolute canonical address. |
| litespec/ext/site#publish-a-markdown-copy-beside-every-page | [Publish a Markdown Copy Beside Every Page](../ext/site.md#publish-a-markdown-copy-beside-every-page) | rule | guidance | Each built page links a Markdown copy served beside it. |
| litespec/ext/site#publish-full-page-text-for-language-models | [Publish Full Page Text for Language Models](../ext/site.md#publish-full-page-text-for-language-models) | rule | guidance | The generated full-text file holds the text of every default-language page. |
| litespec/ext/site#read-prices-and-claims-from-brief-and-business-requirements | [Read Prices and Claims from Brief and Business Requirements](../ext/site.md#read-prices-and-claims-from-brief-and-business-requirements) | convention | guidance | Prices and claims read the brief and business requirements, or the site-facts convention names other records. |
| litespec/ext/site#read-site-prices-and-claims-from-consumer-records | [Read Site Prices and Claims from Consumer Records](../ext/site.md#read-site-prices-and-claims-from-consumer-records) | rule | guidance | Each site price and claim traces to the consumer record the site convention names. |
| litespec/ext/site#reference-language-alternates-from-every-translated-page | [Reference Language Alternates from Every Translated Page](../ext/site.md#reference-language-alternates-from-every-translated-page) | rule | guidance | Each translated page links its language alternates with tags and a default fallback. |
| litespec/ext/site#reject-a-release-build-carrying-a-placeholder | [Reject a Release Build Carrying a Placeholder](../ext/site.md#reject-a-release-build-carrying-a-placeholder) | rule | guidance | A release build fails while a placeholder remains on any page. |
| litespec/ext/site#render-components-on-the-server-with-astro-or-svelte | [Render Components on the Server with Astro or Svelte](../ext/site.md#render-components-on-the-server-with-astro-or-svelte) | convention | guidance | Astro or Svelte sources render on the server, or the site-component convention names other sources. |
| litespec/ext/site#render-every-language-from-one-page-structure | [Render Every Language from One Page Structure](../ext/site.md#render-every-language-from-one-page-structure) | rule | guidance | One page structure renders every language, with each language supplying only text. |
| litespec/ext/site#render-every-page-to-static-files | [Render Every Page to Static Files](../ext/site.md#render-every-page-to-static-files) | rule | guidance | The build output holds every page as a static file needing no server. |
| litespec/ext/site#state-allowed-sources-in-a-content-security-policy | [State Allowed Sources in a Content Security Policy](../ext/site.md#state-allowed-sources-in-a-content-security-policy) | rule | guidance | The published content security policy names every source the pages load. |
| litespec/ext/site#use-tailwind-for-site-styles | [Use Tailwind for Site Styles](../ext/site.md#use-tailwind-for-site-styles) | convention | guidance | Tailwind styles build the site, or the site-style convention names another stylesheet source. |
| litespec/ext/stage#declare-the-release-stage | [Declare the Release Stage](../ext/stage.md#declare-the-release-stage) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` refuses a `stage` selection with a missing or unknown `releaseStage`. |
| litespec/ext/stage#defer-named-provisions-until-promotion | [Defer Named Provisions Until Promotion](../ext/stage.md#defer-named-provisions-until-promotion) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` refuses a deferral exception naming an unknown provision, and any deferral in `production`; `litespec model check` reports `exception-expired` for an exception past its `expiresOn`. |
| litespec/ext/stage#end-transcription-at-the-named-decision | [End Transcription at the Named Decision](../ext/stage.md#end-transcription-at-the-named-decision) | rule | guidance | The project file names the transcription-ending decision, and no transcribed record remains after its approval. |
| litespec/ext/stage#hold-transcribed-records-in-draft | [Hold Transcribed Records in Draft](../ext/stage.md#hold-transcribed-records-in-draft) | rule | guidance | Each transcribed Behavior record stays in draft until its fulfills or serves parent is approved. |
| litespec/ext/stage#promote-through-a-recorded-decision | [Promote Through a Recorded Decision](../ext/stage.md#promote-through-a-recorded-decision) | rule | guidance | The promotion decision under `docs/intent/decisions/` carries approval before the first deployment holds customer data and ends every deferred provision. |
| litespec/ext/stage#record-rested-on-draft-intent | [Record Rested-On Draft Intent](../ext/stage.md#record-rested-on-draft-intent) | rule | guidance | The promotion decision names each draft decision and requirement that a reviewed Behavior record rests on. |
| litespec/ext/stage#resolve-rested-on-draft-intent-at-promotion | [Resolve Rested-On Draft Intent at Promotion](../ext/stage.md#resolve-rested-on-draft-intent-at-promotion) | rule | guidance | Each named draft intent carries approval or retirement before the first deployment holds customer data. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#apply-cross-tenant-disclosure-policy | [Apply Cross-Tenant Disclosure Policy](../ext/tenancy.md#apply-cross-tenant-disclosure-policy) | rule | guidance | Each cross-tenant response follows the documented not-found or forbidden policy, shown in the API tests. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#audit-actions-taken-under-delegation | [Audit Actions Taken Under Delegation](../ext/tenancy.md#audit-actions-taken-under-delegation) | rule | guidance | Each action under delegation records the delegation grant used. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#avoid-unplanned-isolation-models | [Avoid Unplanned Isolation Models](../ext/tenancy.md#avoid-unplanned-isolation-models) | rule | guidance | Each mixed isolation model traces to a decision with a data evolution plan. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#bound-every-delegation-in-time | [Bound Every Delegation in Time](../ext/tenancy.md#bound-every-delegation-in-time) | rule | guidance | Each delegation carries an expiry or a revocation path. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#declare-delegation-grants-on-both-sides | [Declare Delegation Grants on Both Sides](../ext/tenancy.md#declare-delegation-grants-on-both-sides) | rule | guidance | Each delegation declares a delegator grant naming who may be acted for and an acting grant naming who may act. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#define-tenant-isolation | [Define Tenant Isolation](../ext/tenancy.md#define-tenant-isolation) | rule | guidance | The adoption decision names shared-table, schema, or database isolation with its guarantee and operating trade-off. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#isolate-administrative-cross-tenant-access | [Isolate Administrative Cross-Tenant Access](../ext/tenancy.md#isolate-administrative-cross-tenant-access) | rule | guidance | Each administrative cross-tenant use case names a platform-scope permission, and the audit trail holds a record for every cross-tenant run. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#pass-tenant-scope-explicitly | [Pass Tenant Scope Explicitly](../ext/tenancy.md#pass-tenant-scope-explicitly) | convention | guidance | Domain code reads no ambient tenant state, or the tenant-scope convention names how the Domain receives scope. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#protect-tenant-diagnostics | [Protect Tenant Diagnostics](../ext/tenancy.md#protect-tenant-diagnostics) | rule | guidance | The error, log, metric, and trace outputs hold no other tenant's identifiers or data. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#prove-delegated-calls-with-scenarios | [Prove Delegated Calls with Scenarios](../ext/tenancy.md#prove-delegated-calls-with-scenarios) | rule | guidance | Each delegation proves an allowed call and a refused call with scenarios. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#record-an-all-tenant-operation | [Record an All-Tenant Operation](../ext/tenancy.md#record-an-all-tenant-operation) | rule | guidance | The record of each all-tenant run names actor, operation, reason, tenant count, and reached tenant identifiers up to the adoption bound. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#record-delegation-grants-with-roles | [Record Delegation Grants with Roles](../ext/tenancy.md#record-delegation-grants-with-roles) | convention | guidance | Each delegation grant sits on its role page, or the delegation-records convention names where grants live. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#record-the-tenant-adoption-decision | [Record the Tenant Adoption Decision](../ext/tenancy.md#record-the-tenant-adoption-decision) | rule | guidance | The tenant adoption decision under `docs/intent/decisions/` names source, storage model, isolation guarantee, administrative access, evolution path, and enumeration bound. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#reject-unrestricted-tenant-input | [Reject Unrestricted Tenant Input](../ext/tenancy.md#reject-unrestricted-tenant-input) | rule | guidance | The tenant resolver code accepts a tenant identifier only from an authenticated source, never from request data. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#resolve-tenant-identity-from-trusted-context | [Resolve Tenant Identity from Trusted Context](../ext/tenancy.md#resolve-tenant-identity-from-trusted-context) | rule | guidance | Each trusted host boundary resolves the tenant from verified claims, host mapping, or another authenticated source. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#review-delegated-cross-tenant-grants-through-the-security-queue | [Review Delegated Cross-Tenant Grants Through the Security Queue](../ext/tenancy.md#review-delegated-cross-tenant-grants-through-the-security-queue) | rule | guidance | Which delegated cross-tenant grants shipped without security review, or gained a tier from cross scope alone? Look at the security queue in the Behavior diff. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#scope-delegated-grants-to-named-permissions | [Scope Delegated Grants to Named Permissions](../ext/tenancy.md#scope-delegated-grants-to-named-permissions) | rule | guidance | Each delegation names its delegated permissions, with none open to all permissions. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#scope-every-use-case-to-its-tenant-reach | [Scope Every Use Case to Its Tenant Reach](../ext/tenancy.md#scope-every-use-case-to-its-tenant-reach) | rule | guidance | Each use case declares own, cross, public, or none in its access tenant scope, with cross grants in the security queue. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#scope-tenant-operations | [Scope Tenant Operations](../ext/tenancy.md#scope-tenant-operations) | rule | guidance | The backup, restore, replay, deletion, export, support, and investigation procedures each state one-tenant or all-tenant scope. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#scope-tenant-owned-records | [Scope Tenant-Owned Records](../ext/tenancy.md#scope-tenant-owned-records) | rule | guidance | The documents, queries, constraints, cache keys, outbox records, automations, exports, and storage paths in code each carry tenant scope. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#use-one-tenant-accessor | [Use One Tenant Accessor](../ext/tenancy.md#use-one-tenant-accessor) | convention | guidance | One current-tenant accessor serves the trusted host boundary, or the tenant-access convention names where readers find the tenant. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#use-selected-tenant-storage-support | [Use Selected Tenant Storage Support](../ext/tenancy.md#use-selected-tenant-storage-support) | rule | guidance | The session-creation code of each provider resolves the tenant through that provider's tenancy support. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#use-typed-tenant-identities | [Use Typed Tenant Identities](../ext/tenancy.md#use-typed-tenant-identities) | convention | guidance | Tenant references carry the `TenantId` type, or the tenant-identity convention names another type. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#verify-actor-and-target-tenancy | [Verify Actor and Target Tenancy](../ext/tenancy.md#verify-actor-and-target-tenancy) | rule | guidance | The authorization tests fail a protected operation whose actor and target tenants mismatch. |
| litespec/ext/workflow-engine#declare-the-engine-carrier | [Declare the Engine Carrier](../ext/workflow-engine.md#declare-the-engine-carrier) | rule | guidance | The engine-carried aggregates set carriedBy workflow on their aggregate records. |
| litespec/ext/workflow-engine#keep-engine-retry-configuration-in-implementation | [Keep Engine Retry Configuration in Implementation](../ext/workflow-engine.md#keep-engine-retry-configuration-in-implementation) | rule | guidance | Retry counts, backoff curves, and step timeouts live in worker code with none in the records. |
| litespec/ext/workflow-engine#map-each-engine-step-to-one-use-case | [Map Each Engine Step to One Use Case](../ext/workflow-engine.md#map-each-engine-step-to-one-use-case) | rule | guidance | The engine definition lists one use case beside each step with no step sharing or missing. |
| litespec/ext/workflow-engine#name-engine-steps-after-use-cases | [Name Engine Steps After Use Cases](../ext/workflow-engine.md#name-engine-steps-after-use-cases) | convention | guidance | Engine steps carry their use-case ids, or the workflow documentation records the naming convention. |
| litespec/ext/workflow-engine#reject-unmapped-engine-steps | [Reject Unmapped Engine Steps](../ext/workflow-engine.md#reject-unmapped-engine-steps) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `behavior-unrecorded` for an engine step that maps to no use case. |
| litespec/ext/workflow-engine#state-each-step-compensation | [State Each Step Compensation](../ext/workflow-engine.md#state-each-step-compensation) | rule | guidance | Which mapped steps lack a compensation object naming its use case or its reason? Look at the map beside the process. |

## frontend

| ID | Provision | Kind | Mark | Evidence |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| litespec/frontend/blazor/browser#bound-what-is-stored | [Bound What Is Stored](../frontend/blazor/browser.md#bound-what-is-stored) | rule | guidance | Each aggregate persisting a growing collection states its bound and eviction rule. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/browser#keep-a-stable-serialization-contract | [Keep a Stable Serialization Contract](../frontend/blazor/browser.md#keep-a-stable-serialization-contract) | convention | guidance | A record carrying an unknown property still reads. |
