| litespec/frontend/blazor/browser#provide-a-migration-for-every-changed-shape | [Provide a Migration for Every Changed Shape](../frontend/blazor/browser.md#provide-a-migration-for-every-changed-shape) | rule | guidance | Each changed persisted shape ships a migration reading every released version into the current one. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/browser#provide-export-and-import | [Provide Export and Import](../frontend/blazor/browser.md#provide-export-and-import) | rule | guidance | An export reimports to identical durable state, transforms older versions, and refuses newer ones. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/browser#record-the-classification | [Record the Classification](../frontend/blazor/browser.md#record-the-classification) | convention | guidance | Durable and device-local keys use separate prefixes. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/browser#select-the-storage-mechanism-per-shape | [Select the Storage Mechanism per Shape](../frontend/blazor/browser.md#select-the-storage-mechanism-per-shape) | convention | guidance | Each persisted shape uses key-value storage, an indexed store, or the cache API per its access pattern. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/browser#treat-reads-as-fallible | [Treat Reads as Fallible](../frontend/blazor/browser.md#treat-reads-as-fallible) | rule | guidance | The adapter returns unavailable, absent, corrupt, and present as typed outcomes. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/browser#version-and-scope-every-storage-key | [Version and Scope Every Storage Key](../frontend/blazor/browser.md#version-and-scope-every-storage-key) | rule | guidance | Each storage key is a constant beside its adapter carrying product, area, and version. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/components#apply-one-styling-system | [Apply One Styling System](../frontend/blazor/components.md#apply-one-styling-system) | convention | guidance | Each color, spacing, and font value is a defined token. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/components#declare-every-parameter-type | [Declare Every Parameter Type](../frontend/blazor/components.md#declare-every-parameter-type) | rule | guidance | Each component parameter declares a feature-owned concrete type. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/components#keep-accessibility-in-the-component | [Keep Accessibility in the Component](../frontend/blazor/components.md#keep-accessibility-in-the-component) | rule | guidance | Keyboard paths, focus, labels, and announcements work in each interactive component. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/components#keep-components-presentational | [Keep Components Presentational](../frontend/blazor/components.md#keep-components-presentational) | rule | guidance | No component resolves storage, interop, or a Domain service. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/components#name-for-the-boundary-role | [Name for the Boundary Role](../frontend/blazor/components.md#name-for-the-boundary-role) | convention | guidance | Each feature component name matches its rendered role. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/components#own-the-layer-contract | [Own the Layer Contract](../frontend/blazor/components.md#own-the-layer-contract) | rule | guidance | No view model exposes a Domain or Application type. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/components#place-one-component-per-file | [Place One Component per File](../frontend/blazor/components.md#place-one-component-per-file) | rule | guidance | Each `.razor` file declares one routable or reusable component. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/components#raise-events-rather-than-mutating | [Raise Events Rather Than Mutating](../frontend/blazor/components.md#raise-events-rather-than-mutating) | rule | guidance | Each child component reports upward through a callback parameter. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/components#resolve-browser-behavior-through-an-interface | [Resolve Browser Behavior Through an Interface](../frontend/blazor/components.md#resolve-browser-behavior-through-an-interface) | rule | guidance | No component injects the interop runtime directly. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/data#classify-state | [Classify State](../frontend/blazor/data.md#classify-state) | rule | guidance | Each aggregate introducing state names durable, device-local, or derived on its page. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/data#keep-derived-values-single-sourced | [Keep Derived Values Single-Sourced](../frontend/blazor/data.md#keep-derived-values-single-sourced) | rule | guidance | Each derived value has one computation site in Domain or Application. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/data#keep-forms-typed | [Keep Forms Typed](../frontend/blazor/data.md#keep-forms-typed) | convention | guidance | Each form binds a feature-owned model and maps it to a command on submission. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/data#load-persisted-state-once | [Load Persisted State Once](../frontend/blazor/data.md#load-persisted-state-once) | rule | guidance | Persisted state loads into the typed store during startup, before the first render. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/data#make-refusals-explicit | [Make Refusals Explicit](../frontend/blazor/data.md#make-refusals-explicit) | rule | guidance | Each handler union covers success and every named refusal. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/data#prefer-explicit-refresh | [Prefer Explicit Refresh](../frontend/blazor/data.md#prefer-explicit-refresh) | convention | guidance | The store updates from each command result without a follow-up query. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/data#route-use-cases-through-application | [Route Use Cases Through Application](../frontend/blazor/data.md#route-use-cases-through-application) | rule | guidance | No component resolves a Domain service or storage adapter. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/data#validate-at-the-boundary | [Validate at the Boundary](../frontend/blazor/data.md#validate-at-the-boundary) | rule | guidance | Each form refusal originates from its Application validator. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/rendering#declare-routes-on-pages | [Declare Routes on Pages](../frontend/blazor/rendering.md#declare-routes-on-pages) | rule | guidance | Each routable component under `Pages/` declares one `@page` template with typed parameters. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/rendering#give-every-route-explicit-states | [Give Every Route Explicit States](../frontend/blazor/rendering.md#give-every-route-explicit-states) | rule | guidance | Each route renders loading, empty, error, forbidden, not-found, and ready, with not-found for bad parameters. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/rendering#keep-navigation-state-in-the-url | [Keep Navigation State in the URL](../frontend/blazor/rendering.md#keep-navigation-state-in-the-url) | convention | guidance | Filters, tabs, and pagination survive a refresh through the route or query string. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/rendering#keep-the-acquisition-surface-static | [Keep the Acquisition Surface Static](../frontend/blazor/rendering.md#keep-the-acquisition-surface-static) | rule | guidance | Each landing page loads without fetching the framework payload. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/rendering#name-routes-for-the-reader | [Name Routes for the Reader](../frontend/blazor/rendering.md#name-routes-for-the-reader) | convention | guidance | Each route template matches its documented use case name. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/rendering#operate-offline-after-first-load | [Operate Offline After First Load](../frontend/blazor/rendering.md#operate-offline-after-first-load) | rule | guidance | The application starts offline after first load, within the limits its use cases state. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/rendering#render-on-the-client-only | [Render on the Client Only](../frontend/blazor/rendering.md#render-on-the-client-only) | rule | guidance | The published output declares no server render mode. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/stack#keep-blazor-outside-the-ui-contract | [Keep Blazor Outside the UI Contract](../frontend/blazor/stack.md#keep-blazor-outside-the-ui-contract) | rule | guidance | The consumer's stack selection and CI jobs apply no floorplan, token, or route-suite check, while its `@page` components carry screen marks the .NET reader reads. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/stack#publish-static-webassembly-output | [Publish Static WebAssembly Output](../frontend/blazor/stack.md#publish-static-webassembly-output) | rule | guidance | The published output holds static files with no server assembly or server render mode. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/stack#record-a-first-load-budget | [Record a First-Load Budget](../frontend/blazor/stack.md#record-a-first-load-budget) | convention | guidance | A limit entry in `docs/behavior/operations/limits.md` records the compressed transfer size budget the publish job enforces. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/stack#verify-with-the-net-toolchain | [Verify with the .NET Toolchain](../frontend/blazor/stack.md#verify-with-the-net-toolchain) | convention | guidance | The CI client job runs `dotnet build` and `dotnet test`. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/structure#isolate-module-internals | [Isolate Module Internals](../frontend/blazor/structure.md#isolate-module-internals) | rule | guidance | No cross-module reference resolves an internal feature type. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/structure#keep-domain-free-of-browser-concerns | [Keep Domain Free of Browser Concerns](../frontend/blazor/structure.md#keep-domain-free-of-browser-concerns) | rule | guidance | Domain and Application reference no browser or interop type. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/structure#keep-imports-directional | [Keep Imports Directional](../frontend/blazor/structure.md#keep-imports-directional) | rule | guidance | The reference graph matches the declared direction from pages inward. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/structure#keep-static-content-out-of-the-assembly | [Keep Static Content Out of the Assembly](../frontend/blazor/structure.md#keep-static-content-out-of-the-assembly) | convention | guidance | Published output serves runtime content from `wwwroot/` rather than embedded resources. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/structure#organize-features-by-module-and-use-case | [Organize Features by Module and Use Case](../frontend/blazor/structure.md#organize-features-by-module-and-use-case) | rule | guidance | Each feature path under `Features/` resolves to a declared module and use case. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/structure#place-interop-adapters-together | [Place Interop Adapters Together](../frontend/blazor/structure.md#place-interop-adapters-together) | convention | guidance | Each browser API surface has one adapter type in `Infrastructure/Interop/` with a matching script in `wwwroot/interop/`. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/structure#use-the-client-solution-tree | [Use the Client Solution Tree](../frontend/blazor/structure.md#use-the-client-solution-tree) | rule | guidance | The client solution holds Domain, Application, Infrastructure, and Web projects with no server project. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/testing#enforce-the-first-load-budget | [Enforce the First-Load Budget](../frontend/blazor/testing.md#enforce-the-first-load-budget) | rule | guidance | The CI publish job fails when transfer size exceeds the recorded budget. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/testing#keep-browser-capability-tests-honest | [Keep Browser-Capability Tests Honest](../frontend/blazor/testing.md#keep-browser-capability-tests-honest) | convention | guidance | Each capability-dependent feature tests its supported and unsupported paths. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/testing#keep-end-to-end-tests-few | [Keep End-to-End Tests Few](../frontend/blazor/testing.md#keep-end-to-end-tests-few) | convention | guidance | Each browser test maps to a required flow or a scenario-named interaction. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/testing#prove-each-scenario-with-a-test | [Prove Each Scenario with a Test](../frontend/blazor/testing.md#prove-each-scenario-with-a-test) | rule | guidance | Each scenario has a test whose steps call the use case and assert the result. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/testing#replace-interop-in-component-tests | [Replace Interop in Component Tests](../frontend/blazor/testing.md#replace-interop-in-component-tests) | rule | guidance | Each component render substitutes every Application interface. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/testing#test-the-core-without-a-browser | [Test the Core Without a Browser](../frontend/blazor/testing.md#test-the-core-without-a-browser) | rule | guidance | No Domain or Application test project references a browser or interop type. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/testing#use-the-four-client-test-projects | [Use the Four Client Test Projects](../frontend/blazor/testing.md#use-the-four-client-test-projects) | rule | guidance | The client solution holds Domain, Application, Web, and end-to-end test projects that run in CI. |
| litespec/frontend/blazor/testing#verify-real-browser-behavior-end-to-end | [Verify Real Browser Behavior End to End](../frontend/blazor/testing.md#verify-real-browser-behavior-end-to-end) | rule | guidance | The browser suite covers the primary flow, storage loss, offline startup, and export with import. |
| litespec/frontend/components#add-a-missing-composite-to-the-shared-package | [Add a Missing Composite to the Shared Package](../frontend/components.md#add-a-missing-composite-to-the-shared-package) | rule | guidance | A layout, control, or surface drawn by two pages is exported from the shared package. Look in the shared package exports and the drawing pages. |
| litespec/frontend/components#compose-composites-in-page-code | [Compose Composites in Page Code](../frontend/components.md#compose-composites-in-page-code) | rule | checkable | `litespec ui` reports `[litespec/frontend/components#compose-composites-in-page-code]` with the file and the refused import. Run it at the consumer root. |
| litespec/frontend/components#expose-card-and-section-titles-as-headings | [Expose Card and Section Titles as Headings](../frontend/components.md#expose-card-and-section-titles-as-headings) | convention | guidance | Visible card, panel, and section titles render as headings at their level. Look in the route suite output, which resolves each as a heading. |
| litespec/frontend/components#keep-component-inputs-narrow | [Keep Component Inputs Narrow](../frontend/components.md#keep-component-inputs-narrow) | rule | guidance | Composite inputs name values and callbacks only. Look in the shared package component signatures. |
| litespec/frontend/components#keep-domain-values-typed-until-display | [Keep Domain Values Typed Until Display](../frontend/components.md#keep-domain-values-typed-until-display) | convention | guidance | Display strings are produced only at the presentation boundary with explicit locale and zone. Look in the presentation boundary and the components feeding it. |
| litespec/frontend/components#keep-error-boundaries-scoped | [Keep Error Boundaries Scoped](../frontend/components.md#keep-error-boundaries-scoped) | convention | guidance | Route failure renders on the route error surface, and each component boundary keeps its surroundings usable. Look in the route error surfaces and the boundary components. |
| litespec/frontend/components#keep-shared-ui-in-one-package | [Keep Shared UI in One Package](../frontend/components.md#keep-shared-ui-in-one-package) | rule | guidance | One shared UI package exports the primitives and the composites, and application folders hold no copies. Look in the workspace packages and the application sources. |
| litespec/frontend/components#name-components-for-their-role | [Name Components for Their Role](../frontend/components.md#name-components-for-their-role) | convention | guidance | Component names state the role each plays. Look in the shared package and module sources. |
| litespec/frontend/components#place-a-drawing-no-composite-makes-in-an-exception-file | [Place a Drawing No Composite Makes in an Exception File](../frontend/components.md#place-a-drawing-no-composite-makes-in-an-exception-file) | rule | guidance | Custom styling appears only in files whose names mark them as drawings outside the composites, using semantic theme tokens only. Look in the exception files each stack page names. |
| litespec/frontend/components#prefer-a-composite-over-a-primitive | [Prefer a Composite over a Primitive](../frontend/components.md#prefer-a-composite-over-a-primitive) | rule | guidance | Page sources import the composite wherever the shared package provides one. Look in route and module imports. |
| litespec/frontend/components#render-control-states | [Render Control States](../frontend/components.md#render-control-states) | rule | guidance | Each control draws disabled and pending, blocks a second submission while one is in flight, and keeps recovery usable. Look in the shared package components. |
| litespec/frontend/components#render-data-states | [Render Data States](../frontend/components.md#render-data-states) | rule | guidance | Each data component draws loading, empty, error, forbidden, and ready, with each state name a glossary term. Look in the shared package components and the glossary. |
| litespec/frontend/components#sanitize-untrusted-rich-content | [Sanitize Untrusted Rich Content](../frontend/components.md#sanitize-untrusted-rich-content) | rule | guidance | Rich content from untrusted sources renders only with a recorded decision naming the sanitizer, the allowed elements and attributes, the link policy, and the test cases. Look in the decision records and the rendering components. |
| litespec/frontend/components#support-keyboard-focus-labels-and-announcements | [Support Keyboard, Focus, Labels, and Announcements](../frontend/components.md#support-keyboard-focus-labels-and-announcements) | rule | guidance | Keyboard operation, visible focus, semantic elements, programmatic labels, and status announcements hold on interactive UI. Look in the route suite output. |
| litespec/frontend/components#use-a-composite-for-a-content-image | [Use a Composite for a Content Image](../frontend/components.md#use-a-composite-for-a-content-image) | rule | guidance | Content images render through the shared image component with the text policy for their kind. Look in page sources for unwrapped images. |
| litespec/frontend/components#use-composites-for-layout-and-interaction-in-page-code | [Use Composites for Layout and Interaction in Page Code](../frontend/components.md#use-composites-for-layout-and-interaction-in-page-code) | rule | guidance | Route and module sources declare no layout or interactive element. Look in route and module sources; each stack page names the refused elements and the composite each refusal names. |
| litespec/frontend/components#use-declared-visual-variants | [Use Declared Visual Variants](../frontend/components.md#use-declared-visual-variants) | rule | guidance | Repeated variants resolve through the shared variant helper in theme tokens, with no literal values in composites. Look in the shared package components. |
| litespec/frontend/components#write-no-class-name-in-page-code | [Write No Class Name in Page Code](../frontend/components.md#write-no-class-name-in-page-code) | rule | guidance | Route and module sources declare no class or style binding. Look in route and module sources; each stack page names the bindings its lint refuses. |
| litespec/frontend/data#announce-the-outcome-of-an-optimistic-update | [Announce the Outcome of an Optimistic Update](../frontend/data.md#announce-the-outcome-of-an-optimistic-update) | rule | guidance | A reconciled outcome differing from the shown value announces through a live region. Look in the route tests asserting the announcement. |
| litespec/frontend/data#assign-state-to-the-narrowest-owner | [Assign State to the Narrowest Owner](../frontend/data.md#assign-state-to-the-narrowest-owner) | rule | guidance | Values live in the query cache, the address, form state, or component state, at the narrowest applicable owner. Look in the state declarations. |
| litespec/frontend/data#build-a-form-on-the-shared-form-library | [Build a Form on the Shared Form Library](../frontend/data.md#build-a-form-on-the-shared-form-library) | convention | guidance | Forms build on the shared package form entry point and registered field components. Look in the form sources and the shared package form entry point. |
| litespec/frontend/data#generate-the-client-from-the-published-contract | [Generate the Client from the Published Contract](../frontend/data.md#generate-the-client-from-the-published-contract) | rule | guidance | Client, types, schemas, and operation options are generated output from the published OpenAPI document. Look in the generated client directory and the contract it names. |
| litespec/frontend/data#keep-schemas-use-case-specific | [Keep Schemas Use-Case Specific](../frontend/data.md#keep-schemas-use-case-specific) | convention | guidance | Form schemas and view models live in their owning use-case folder until a second use case needs them. Look in the use-case folders and the shared schema locations. |
| litespec/frontend/data#keep-secrets-out-of-browser-storage | [Keep Secrets Out of Browser Storage](../frontend/data.md#keep-secrets-out-of-browser-storage) | rule | guidance | Browser storage and browser-visible variables hold no refresh token, provider secret, or privileged credential. Look in the storage writes and the public variables. |
| litespec/frontend/data#keep-the-client-boundary-in-the-frame | [Keep the Client Boundary in the Frame](../frontend/data.md#keep-the-client-boundary-in-the-frame) | convention | guidance | Client configuration, problem mapping, and download helpers sit only in the frame root. Look in the frame root and the use-case folders. |
| litespec/frontend/data#keep-the-session-in-an-api-owned-cookie | [Keep the Session in an API Owned Cookie](../frontend/data.md#keep-the-session-in-an-api-owned-cookie) | rule | guidance | The browser session lives in an HTTP-only cookie, and frontend sources hold no token. Look in the API session handling and the frontend sources. |
| litespec/frontend/data#localize-copy-through-locale-dictionaries | [Localize Copy Through Locale Dictionaries](../frontend/data.md#localize-copy-through-locale-dictionaries) | rule | guidance | Copy resolves through locale dictionaries with fill and plural support, one chunk per language, typed keys, and a declared fallback. Look in the locale dictionaries and the copy call sites. |
| litespec/frontend/data#make-optimistic-behavior-recoverable | [Make Optimistic Behavior Recoverable](../frontend/data.md#make-optimistic-behavior-recoverable) | rule | guidance | Optimistic updates declare client identity, conflict behavior, rollback, and reconciliation; money, irreversible actions, and uncertain authorization wait for the server. Look in the optimistic update definitions. |
| litespec/frontend/data#map-every-failure-to-one-problem-type | [Map Every Failure to One Problem Type](../frontend/data.md#map-every-failure-to-one-problem-type) | rule | guidance | Failures render as one problem type decided from the code, with the trace identifier copyable in the detail. Look in the frame problem mapping and the error surfaces. |
| litespec/frontend/data#name-the-zone-for-every-timestamp | [Name the Zone for Every Timestamp](../frontend/data.md#name-the-zone-for-every-timestamp) | rule | guidance | Timestamp formatters receive an explicit IANA zone resolved from the shown record. Look in the formatter call sites. |
| litespec/frontend/data#place-a-field-refusal-on-its-field | [Place a Field Refusal on Its Field](../frontend/data.md#place-a-field-refusal-on-its-field) | rule | guidance | Field problems render on their fields through the frame helper with the form left open. Look in the forms and the frame helper. |
| litespec/frontend/data#place-a-refusal-beside-what-was-refused | [Place a Refusal Beside What Was Refused](../frontend/data.md#place-a-refusal-beside-what-was-refused) | rule | guidance | Refusals render beside the refused record or control in the application's sentence for the code, with the code in the markup. Look in the screen's page files beside the refusing controls. |
| litespec/frontend/data#read-a-permission-from-the-session | [Read a Permission from the Session](../frontend/data.md#read-a-permission-from-the-session) | rule | guidance | Offered commands match the permissions the session actor holds at the record scope, with the API deciding each command. Look in the page command guards and the session shape. |
| litespec/frontend/data#regenerate-the-client-rather-than-editing-it | [Regenerate the Client Rather Than Editing It](../frontend/data.md#regenerate-the-client-rather-than-editing-it) | convention | guidance | The committed client equals fresh generation output with no hand edits below the generated directory. Look in the generated directory and the generator command. |
| litespec/frontend/data#route-a-personal-value-in-a-header | [Route a Personal Value in a Header](../frontend/data.md#route-a-personal-value-in-a-header) | rule | guidance | Person-naming values travel in contract-declared headers, not the query string. Look in the contract headers and the call sites. |
| litespec/frontend/data#set-the-write-marker-on-every-request | [Set the Write Marker on Every Request](../frontend/data.md#set-the-write-marker-on-every-request) | rule | guidance | Writes carry the configured marker header of the form `X-<Project>-Request`. Look in the client configuration and the API write acceptance. |
| litespec/frontend/data#update-the-cache-a-command-changed | [Update the Cache a Command Changed](../frontend/data.md#update-the-cache-a-command-changed) | rule | guidance | Successful commands invalidate exactly the queries their answers changed. Look in the command call sites and the cache keys. |
| litespec/frontend/data#use-one-configured-client-per-application | [Use One Configured Client per Application](../frontend/data.md#use-one-configured-client-per-application) | rule | guidance | Reads and writes pass through the one configured client with its base address, credentials, headers, and failure mapping. Look in the frame client configuration and the call sites. |
| litespec/frontend/data#validate-a-form-with-the-generated-schema | [Validate a Form with the Generated Schema](../frontend/data.md#validate-a-form-with-the-generated-schema) | rule | guidance | Forms validate against the generated request schema extended with application rules. Look in the form sources and the generated schemas. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/android/stack#build-with-net-for-android | [Build with .NET for Android](../frontend/mobile/android/stack.md#build-with-net-for-android) | rule | guidance | The Android project targets `net{dotnet}-android` at the manifest pins with plain Android views and no MAUI or Forms screen. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/android/stack#keep-android-outside-the-ui-contract | [Keep Android Outside the UI Contract](../frontend/mobile/android/stack.md#keep-android-outside-the-ui-contract) | rule | guidance | The consumer's stack selection and CI jobs apply no floorplan, token, or route-suite check, while its destination classes carry screen marks the .NET reader reads. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/android/stack#pin-every-android-binding-package | [Pin Every Android Binding Package](../frontend/mobile/android/stack.md#pin-every-android-binding-package) | rule | guidance | Each AndroidX or Google binding resolves to a manifest pin with an approval decision. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/android/stack#record-a-package-size-budget | [Record a Package Size Budget](../frontend/mobile/android/stack.md#record-a-package-size-budget) | convention | guidance | A limit entry in `docs/behavior/operations/limits.md` records the AAB size budget the publish job enforces. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/android/stack#record-the-minimum-android-version | [Record the Minimum Android Version](../frontend/mobile/android/stack.md#record-the-minimum-android-version) | rule | guidance | The app project sets `SupportedOSPlatformVersion` with a decision recording why that floor was chosen. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/android/stack#store-streamed-media-uncompressed | [Store Streamed Media Uncompressed](../frontend/mobile/android/stack.md#store-streamed-media-uncompressed) | convention | guidance | Each media extension the app streams through `AssetManager.OpenFd` appears in `AndroidStoreUncompressedFileExtensions`. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/android/stack#verify-with-the-net-toolchain | [Verify with the .NET Toolchain](../frontend/mobile/android/stack.md#verify-with-the-net-toolchain) | convention | guidance | The CI mobile job runs `dotnet build` and `dotnet publish` with no Gradle or Android Studio step. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/android/views#apply-insets-from-windowinsets | [Apply Insets from WindowInsets](../frontend/mobile/android/views.md#apply-insets-from-windowinsets) | rule | guidance | Views apply system bar and cutout insets from `WindowInsets` as padding or margins. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/android/views#avoid-platform-names-that-collide-with-the-app-namespace | [Avoid Platform Names That Collide with the App Namespace](../frontend/mobile/android/views.md#avoid-platform-names-that-collide-with-the-app-namespace) | convention | guidance | SDK types in the `.Android` root resolve through `using` directives or `global::Android`. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/android/views#build-views-in-code-or-layout-resources-not-both | [Build Views in Code or Layout Resources, Not Both](../frontend/mobile/android/views.md#build-views-in-code-or-layout-resources-not-both) | rule | guidance | Each screen builds in C# code or from a layout resource, with one approach across the app. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/android/views#host-screens-in-one-activity | [Host Screens in One Activity](../frontend/mobile/android/views.md#host-screens-in-one-activity) | rule | guidance | The app holds one activity, with navigation state owned by its controller. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/android/views#keep-text-colors-and-icons-in-resources | [Keep Text, Colors, and Icons in Resources](../frontend/mobile/android/views.md#keep-text-colors-and-icons-in-resources) | rule | guidance | User-visible text resolves to `strings.xml` entries, with colors and icons in color and drawable resources. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/android/views#use-the-android-back-dispatcher | [Use the Android Back Dispatcher](../frontend/mobile/android/views.md#use-the-android-back-dispatcher) | rule | guidance | Back handling routes through the API-level dispatcher to the navigation controller. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/data#call-the-server-only-through-the-generated-client | [Call the Server Only Through the Generated Client](../frontend/mobile/data.md#call-the-server-only-through-the-generated-client) | rule | guidance | Every backend call in a `server` app travels through the generated client in `{ProjectName}.Client`. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/data#classify-state | [Classify State](../frontend/mobile/data.md#classify-state) | rule | guidance | Each aggregate introducing state names durable, device-local, or derived on its page. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/data#copy-picked-media-into-the-app | [Copy Picked Media into the App](../frontend/mobile/data.md#copy-picked-media-into-the-app) | rule | guidance | Imported media lives as a copy in app-private storage, with no stored picker URI. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/data#keep-persisted-state-in-app-private-storage | [Keep Persisted State in App-Private Storage](../frontend/mobile/data.md#keep-persisted-state-in-app-private-storage) | rule | guidance | Persisted state and user-added media live in app-private storage, each file written through a temporary file. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/data#load-persisted-state-once | [Load Persisted State Once](../frontend/mobile/data.md#load-persisted-state-once) | rule | guidance | Persisted state loads into the typed store at startup, with screens showing a loading state until it is ready. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/data#make-refusals-explicit | [Make Refusals Explicit](../frontend/mobile/data.md#make-refusals-explicit) | rule | guidance | Each handler union covers success and every named refusal, and each screen maps every refusal to a message. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/data#route-use-cases-through-application | [Route Use Cases Through Application](../frontend/mobile/data.md#route-use-cases-through-application) | rule | guidance | No screen resolves a Domain service or storage adapter. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/data#update-the-store-from-command-results | [Update the Store from Command Results](../frontend/mobile/data.md#update-the-store-from-command-results) | convention | guidance | The store applies the state each command result carries without re-reading storage. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/device#declare-only-used-permissions | [Declare Only Used Permissions](../frontend/mobile/device.md#declare-only-used-permissions) | rule | guidance | The app manifest declares only permissions current features use. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/device#hold-audio-focus-while-playing-sound | [Hold Audio Focus While Playing Sound](../frontend/mobile/device.md#hold-audio-focus-while-playing-sound) | rule | guidance | Audible playback requests audio focus and pauses on focus loss. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/device#keep-device-work-in-the-foreground | [Keep Device Work in the Foreground](../frontend/mobile/device.md#keep-device-work-in-the-foreground) | convention | guidance | Background services, scheduled jobs, and push handlers each name a project decision, or none exist. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/device#pause-media-in-the-background | [Pause Media in the Background](../frontend/mobile/device.md#pause-media-in-the-background) | rule | guidance | Sound and video pause out of the foreground and resume only on return. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/device#prefer-permissionless-system-pickers | [Prefer Permissionless System Pickers](../frontend/mobile/device.md#prefer-permissionless-system-pickers) | rule | guidance | Photo and file picking travels through the permissionless system picker where the platform offers one. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/device#request-a-permission-at-the-point-of-use | [Request a Permission at the Point of Use](../frontend/mobile/device.md#request-a-permission-at-the-point-of-use) | rule | guidance | Runtime permission requests fire from the action needing them, with none at launch. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/screens#draw-edge-to-edge-with-insets | [Draw Edge to Edge with Insets](../frontend/mobile/screens.md#draw-edge-to-edge-with-insets) | rule | guidance | Screens draw under the system bars with insets applied to controls and text. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/screens#follow-the-system-back-gesture | [Follow the System Back Gesture](../frontend/mobile/screens.md#follow-the-system-back-gesture) | rule | guidance | The system back gesture closes the open overlay first, steps back one screen, and leaves the app only from the first screen. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/screens#honor-reduced-motion | [Honor Reduced Motion](../frontend/mobile/screens.md#honor-reduced-motion) | rule | guidance | Screens with animations off show looping motion and video as a still state. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/screens#meet-touch-and-text-accessibility | [Meet Touch and Text Accessibility](../frontend/mobile/screens.md#meet-touch-and-text-accessibility) | convention | guidance | Interactive views carry content descriptions and platform-minimum targets, and text scales with the system font scale. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/screens#own-navigation-in-one-controller | [Own Navigation in One Controller](../frontend/mobile/screens.md#own-navigation-in-one-controller) | rule | guidance | The current screen, the open overlay, and the history live in one controller, and screens raise events instead of navigating. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/screens#release-media-with-its-screen | [Release Media with Its Screen](../frontend/mobile/screens.md#release-media-with-its-screen) | rule | guidance | Players, animators, and decoded images release or cancel when their view leaves the window. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/screens#render-with-the-platforms-own-views | [Render with the Platform's Own Views](../frontend/mobile/screens.md#render-with-the-platforms-own-views) | rule | guidance | Every product screen renders with the platform's native UI toolkit, with no WebView or cross-platform UI layer. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/screens#restore-the-visitors-place | [Restore the Visitor's Place](../frontend/mobile/screens.md#restore-the-visitors-place) | convention | guidance | Process recreation returns to the screen the saved navigation state names. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/structure#hold-the-domain-in-the-app-only-without-a-server | [Hold the Domain in the App Only Without a Server](../frontend/mobile/structure.md#hold-the-domain-in-the-app-only-without-a-server) | rule | guidance | A `client-only` solution holds Domain, Application, and Infrastructure projects, and a `server` solution holds a Client project with the generated API client and no Domain copy. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/structure#keep-core-projects-platform-free | [Keep Core Projects Platform-Free](../frontend/mobile/structure.md#keep-core-projects-platform-free) | rule | guidance | Core projects target plain .NET and reference no platform SDK type. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/structure#keep-imports-directional | [Keep Imports Directional](../frontend/mobile/structure.md#keep-imports-directional) | rule | guidance | The reference graph matches the declared direction from the platform project inward. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/structure#organize-screens-by-module | [Organize Screens by Module](../frontend/mobile/structure.md#organize-screens-by-module) | rule | guidance | Each screen lives under `Screens/`, in its module folder where the product has more than one module. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/structure#place-platform-adapters-in-the-platform-project | [Place Platform Adapters in the Platform Project](../frontend/mobile/structure.md#place-platform-adapters-in-the-platform-project) | convention | guidance | Each platform API surface has one adapter type in the platform project behind an interface Application owns. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/structure#use-the-mobile-solution-tree | [Use the Mobile Solution Tree](../frontend/mobile/structure.md#use-the-mobile-solution-tree) | rule | guidance | One solution at `apps/mobile/` holds the core projects under `src/` and one app project per platform named `{ProjectName}.{Platform}`. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/testing#prove-screens-on-a-device-or-emulator | [Prove Screens on a Device or Emulator](../frontend/mobile/testing.md#prove-screens-on-a-device-or-emulator) | rule | guidance | Each screen a use case reaches has a device test driving it on an emulator or device. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/testing#run-the-release-build-before-a-release | [Run the Release Build Before a Release](../frontend/mobile/testing.md#run-the-release-build-before-a-release) | convention | guidance | The release record names one physical device per platform and the walk result. |
| litespec/frontend/mobile/testing#test-the-core-as-plain-net | [Test the Core as Plain .NET](../frontend/mobile/testing.md#test-the-core-as-plain-net) | rule | guidance | Core test projects run on the plain .NET runner with no emulator. |
