| litespec/frontend/patterns/record-detail#show-one-primary-action-in-a-record-header | [Show One Primary Action in a Record Header](../frontend/patterns/record-detail.md#show-one-primary-action-in-a-record-header) | rule | checkable | `litespec-maintain validate` emits `PATTERN_INVALID` for a wireframe header holding two primary actions. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#build-the-server-data-cache-per-request | [Build the Server Data Cache per Request](../frontend/rendering.md#build-the-server-data-cache-per-request) | rule | guidance | Server rendering constructs its data cache inside each request. Look in the server request setup. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#keep-address-parameters-validated-by-a-schema | [Keep Address Parameters Validated by a Schema](../frontend/rendering.md#keep-address-parameters-validated-by-a-schema) | rule | guidance | Routes declare address parameters, filters, and page in a validated schema. Look in the route parameter schemas. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#keep-authenticated-caching-explicit | [Keep Authenticated Caching Explicit](../frontend/rendering.md#keep-authenticated-caching-explicit) | rule | guidance | Cached actor-specific reads declare cache key, partition, invalidation owner, and security review beside the read definition. Look in the read definitions setting cache keys. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#keep-layouts-stable | [Keep Layouts Stable](../frontend/rendering.md#keep-layouts-stable) | convention | guidance | Layouts hold only the frame and providers every child route needs. Look in the layout definitions. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#keep-proxy-behavior-at-the-edge | [Keep Proxy Behavior at the Edge](../frontend/rendering.md#keep-proxy-behavior-at-the-edge) | rule | guidance | Proxy rules route only, with identity and authorization decided at the API. Look in the proxy rules. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#keep-server-only-code-identifiable | [Keep Server-Only Code Identifiable](../frontend/rendering.md#keep-server-only-code-identifiable) | convention | guidance | Modules reading request headers, cookies, or server secrets sit in files named and placed for the server. Look in the server file names and places. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#load-each-route-and-each-language-separately | [Load Each Route and Each Language Separately](../frontend/rendering.md#load-each-route-and-each-language-separately) | rule | guidance | Routes and languages ship as separate downloads with no icon set or language loaded whole. Look in the route bundles and the dictionary loader. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#load-route-data-before-rendering | [Load Route Data Before Rendering](../frontend/rendering.md#load-route-data-before-rendering) | rule | guidance | Routes load each read through one shared cache before the view renders. Look in the route loaders and the cache definitions. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#prefetch-a-route-on-intent | [Prefetch a Route on Intent](../frontend/rendering.md#prefetch-a-route-on-intent) | rule | guidance | Intent toward a route prefetches into the shared cache only, with no second router cache. Look in the router prefetch wiring. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#read-route-data-from-the-route-cache | [Read Route Data from the Route Cache](../frontend/rendering.md#read-route-data-from-the-route-cache) | rule | guidance | Views read through the cache entry their route filled, and failed reads reach the route error boundary. Look in the view data reads and the route loaders. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#represent-route-states | [Represent Route States](../frontend/rendering.md#represent-route-states) | rule | guidance | Data-driven routes define loading, empty, error, forbidden, not-found, and ready behavior, and an absent address renders the shared outcome page. Look in the route state definitions. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#use-a-shared-layout-for-a-shell | [Use a Shared Layout for a Shell](../frontend/rendering.md#use-a-shared-layout-for-a-shell) | convention | guidance | Shells sit in shared layouts adding no address segment. Look in the layout definitions. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#keep-a-route-entry-thin | [Keep a Route Entry Thin](../frontend/structure.md#keep-a-route-entry-thin) | rule | guidance | Route entries hold input, load, and component, with reusable logic in modules and no route-to-route reference. Look in the route entries. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#keep-a-use-case-folder-flat | [Keep a Use Case Folder Flat](../frontend/structure.md#keep-a-use-case-folder-flat) | rule | guidance | Use-case folders hold files and no subfolder. Look in the module tree. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#keep-applications-independent | [Keep Applications Independent](../frontend/structure.md#keep-applications-independent) | rule | guidance | Frontends import no source from another frontend. Look in the import statements. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#keep-shared-packages-non-application-specific | [Keep Shared Packages Non Application Specific](../frontend/structure.md#keep-shared-packages-non-application-specific) | rule | guidance | Shared packages hold no page composition, feature state, authentication policy, or application components. Look in the shared package sources. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#locate-cross-route-values-in-the-frame | [Locate Cross-Route Values in the Frame](../frontend/structure.md#locate-cross-route-values-in-the-frame) | rule | guidance | The frame holds client configuration, session read, language choice, permissions, formatting, shell data, and browser uses. Look in the frame. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#mark-each-route-with-its-screen | [Mark Each Route with Its Screen](../frontend/structure.md#mark-each-route-with-its-screen) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `mark-missing` (level S) for a route entry with no mark, `mark-count-invalid` (level S) for one with more than one, and `mark-unknown` (level S) for a mark naming no screen of the route's entry point. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#mirror-the-backend-use-case-path-in-kebab-case | [Mirror the Backend Use Case Path in Kebab Case](../frontend/structure.md#mirror-the-backend-use-case-path-in-kebab-case) | rule | guidance | Use-case folders sit at `{module}/{aggregate}/{use-case}/` in kebab case and resolve to the backend folder of the same names. Look in the module tree and the structure test proving the mirror. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#order-imports-from-routes-to-shared-packages | [Order Imports from Routes to Shared Packages](../frontend/structure.md#order-imports-from-routes-to-shared-packages) | rule | guidance | Imports point from routes to modules to the frame to shared packages. Look in the import statements. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#organize-a-frontend-into-frame-routes-and-modules | [Organize a Frontend into Frame, Routes, and Modules](../frontend/structure.md#organize-a-frontend-into-frame-routes-and-modules) | rule | guidance | Frontends separate frame, routes, and use-case modules. Look in the frontend folder roots. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#place-a-shared-file-in-the-folder-that-owns-it | [Place a Shared File in the Folder That Owns It](../frontend/structure.md#place-a-shared-file-in-the-folder-that-owns-it) | rule | guidance | Files read by several use cases sit flat in the narrowest folder holding every reader. Look in the aggregate and module folders. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#place-tests-at-their-ownership-boundary | [Place Tests at Their Ownership Boundary](../frontend/structure.md#place-tests-at-their-ownership-boundary) | convention | guidance | Logic tests sit beside their module, and browser suites share one tests root per frontend. Look in the module folders and the tests roots. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#reject-a-folder-name-that-owns-nothing | [Reject a Folder Name That Owns Nothing](../frontend/structure.md#reject-a-folder-name-that-owns-nothing) | rule | guidance | No folder under `src` carries a refused name. Look in the folder tree. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#use-explicit-public-entry-points-for-shared-packages | [Use Explicit Public Entry Points for Shared Packages](../frontend/structure.md#use-explicit-public-entry-points-for-shared-packages) | convention | guidance | Applications import only documented public entry points of shared packages. Look in the application imports and the package manifests. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#assert-each-declared-refusal-by-its-code | [Assert Each Declared Refusal by Its Code](../frontend/testing.md#assert-each-declared-refusal-by-its-code) | rule | guidance | Evidence specifications reach each declared refusal and assert it by its result code. Look in the evidence specifications. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#assert-the-result-a-scenario-ends-in | [Assert the Result a Scenario Ends In](../frontend/testing.md#assert-the-result-a-scenario-ends-in) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `proves-unreached` (level S) for a browser test reaching the screen whose assertions in the same test hold neither the refusal code nor the success result's declared text or code. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#isolate-browser-tests | [Isolate Browser Tests](../frontend/testing.md#isolate-browser-tests) | rule | guidance | Browser tests create their own data, authentication context, and expected state, runnable alone in any order. Look in the browser specifications. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#keep-mocks-at-owned-boundaries | [Keep Mocks at Owned Boundaries](../frontend/testing.md#keep-mocks-at-owned-boundaries) | rule | guidance | Mocks stand only at owned boundaries, with generated contract types, private functions, and stack-named libraries unmocked. Look in the test doubles. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#keep-test-support-narrow | [Keep Test Support Narrow](../frontend/testing.md#keep-test-support-narrow) | convention | guidance | Test support holds render helpers, one shared API helper, and locator helpers only. Look in the test support files. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#mark-the-scenario-a-browser-test-proves | [Mark the Scenario a Browser Test Proves](../frontend/testing.md#mark-the-scenario-a-browser-test-proves) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `mark-unknown` (level S) for a `proves` annotation naming no record a test may prove, or naming a journey. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#match-test-level-to-risk | [Match Test Level to Risk](../frontend/testing.md#match-test-level-to-risk) | rule | guidance | Pure logic takes logic tests, navigation and integration take browser specifications, and every address takes the route contract. Look in the test files beside the modules and under the tests roots. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#name-the-journey-a-journey-test-walks | [Name the Journey a Journey Test Walks](../frontend/testing.md#name-the-journey-a-journey-test-walks) | rule | guidance | Which browser tests that walk several screens carry a `journey` annotation naming the journey and a `proves` mark for each scenario they drive and assert? Look in the browser tests and their annotations. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#prove-each-scenario-at-one-boundary | [Prove Each Scenario at One Boundary](../frontend/testing.md#prove-each-scenario-at-one-boundary) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `proves-duplicate` (level A) for a scenario with `proves` marks on more than one test. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#prove-the-module-mirror-in-a-structure-test | [Prove the Module Mirror in a Structure Test](../frontend/testing.md#prove-the-module-mirror-in-a-structure-test) | rule | guidance | The structure test resolves the module tree to backend use-case folders and names no refused folder. Look in the structure test. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#query-by-accessible-behavior | [Query by Accessible Behavior](../frontend/testing.md#query-by-accessible-behavior) | convention | guidance | Element queries use role, label, name, or visible text before test identifiers. Look in the test locators. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#reach-a-screen-that-offers-the-scenarios-use-case | [Reach a Screen That Offers the Scenario's Use Case](../frontend/testing.md#reach-a-screen-that-offers-the-scenarios-use-case) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `proves-unreached` (level S) for a browser test whose `page.goto` targets match no screen offering the claimed scenario's use case. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#read-the-deployment-for-its-identifiers | [Read the Deployment for Its Identifiers](../frontend/testing.md#read-the-deployment-for-its-identifiers) | convention | guidance | Record identifiers come from the running deployment, falling back to an absent identifier. Look in the browser specifications. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#run-browser-checks-through-the-command-line | [Run Browser Checks Through the Command Line](../frontend/testing.md#run-browser-checks-through-the-command-line) | rule | guidance | Browser suites run through the command line per route and per file. Look in the suite runner and the CI invocation. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#test-observable-states | [Test Observable States](../frontend/testing.md#test-observable-states) | rule | guidance | Tests assert observable behavior in the states their situation reaches. Look in the test assertions. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#use-network-mocks-for-component-tests | [Use Network Mocks for Component Tests](../frontend/testing.md#use-network-mocks-for-component-tests) | rule | guidance | Component and logic tests needing HTTP answer through contract-generated network mocks with the generated client in the path. Look in the component tests and the mock generation. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#check-every-route | [Check Every Route](../frontend/ui.md#check-every-route) | rule | guidance | The route suite output shows every discovered route passing each check. Look in the suite output and the CI job logs. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#compose-each-route-from-one-floorplan | [Compose Each Route from One Floorplan](../frontend/ui.md#compose-each-route-from-one-floorplan) | rule | guidance | Routes render exactly one declared floorplan, or record a freestyle decision. Look in the route sources and the suite output reading the floorplan name from each frame. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#declare-a-closed-floorplan-set | [Declare a Closed Floorplan Set](../frontend/ui.md#declare-a-closed-floorplan-set) | rule | guidance | The design contract Patterns section lists every floorplan with its when-to-use rule. Look in the design contract. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#derive-each-page-type-from-a-named-reference | [Derive Each Page Type from a Named Reference](../frontend/ui.md#derive-each-page-type-from-a-named-reference) | rule | guidance | Routes name their page type reference, and freestyle routes name their decision record instead. Look in the route sources. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#govern-behavior-packages | [Govern Behavior Packages](../frontend/ui.md#govern-behavior-packages) | rule | guidance | Behavior packages name their approving decision record and render through baseline components and tokens. Look in the package declarations and the decision records. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#hold-the-declared-base-color | [Hold the Declared Base Color](../frontend/ui.md#hold-the-declared-base-color) | rule | guidance | Token definitions carry only declared baseline values, with each departure recorded as an exception. Look in the token definitions and the exception records. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#keep-a-blocked-command-visible-with-its-reason | [Keep a Blocked Command Visible with Its Reason](../frontend/ui.md#keep-a-blocked-command-visible-with-its-reason) | convention | guidance | Commands blocked only by state or role stay visible and disabled with the reason beside them. Look in the command surfaces. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#keep-layout-inside-floorplans | [Keep Layout Inside Floorplans](../frontend/ui.md#keep-layout-inside-floorplans) | rule | guidance | Route files declare no layout outside floorplan and display composites. Look in the route sources. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#keep-separators-at-the-subtle-step | [Keep Separators at the Subtle Step](../frontend/ui.md#keep-separators-at-the-subtle-step) | rule | guidance | Structural separators and input borders sit at the published border step or lighter. Look in the token definitions and the rendered separators. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#keep-tokens-in-one-definition | [Keep Tokens in One Definition](../frontend/ui.md#keep-tokens-in-one-definition) | rule | guidance | Applications use the shared token definition with no declared value and no literal color in shipped definitions. Look in the application and shared token definitions. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#pin-the-baseline-in-the-shared-package | [Pin the Baseline in the Shared Package](../frontend/ui.md#pin-the-baseline-in-the-shared-package) | rule | guidance | The shared UI package installs the pinned manifest baseline once, and applications install none. Look in the shared package manifest and lock, and the application manifests. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#prove-every-result-a-route-calls | [Prove Every Result a Route Calls](../frontend/ui.md#prove-every-result-a-route-calls) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `refusal-sentence-missing` (level A) for a refusal reachable through a screen's computed `calls` with neither a dictionary sentence nor a browser test asserting its code. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#prove-ui-behavior-and-appearance | [Prove UI Behavior and Appearance](../frontend/ui.md#prove-ui-behavior-and-appearance) | rule | guidance | Changes name the evidence the matrix assigns to their class. Look in the change descriptions and the named evidence. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#publish-a-design-contract | [Publish a Design Contract](../frontend/ui.md#publish-a-design-contract) | rule | checkable | `litespec ui` reports `[litespec/frontend/ui#publish-a-design-contract]` for a missing contract, a missing metadata block, a package mismatch, or a missing required section. Run it at the consumer root. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#record-a-freestyle-route | [Record a Freestyle Route](../frontend/ui.md#record-a-freestyle-route) | rule | guidance | Freestyle routes name the decision record justifying their layout. Look in the freestyle routes and the decision records. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#record-a-frontend-outside-the-controlled-contract | [Record a Frontend Outside the Controlled Contract](../frontend/ui.md#record-a-frontend-outside-the-controlled-contract) | rule | guidance | Frontends outside the controlled platforms declare an entry point record naming their platform and replacement boundaries. Look in the entry point records. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#restrict-styling-to-semantic-tokens | [Restrict Styling to Semantic Tokens](../frontend/ui.md#restrict-styling-to-semantic-tokens) | rule | guidance | Styles use semantic tokens rather than raw values. Look in the stylesheets and the shared package definition. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#select-a-command-surface-by-size | [Select a Command Surface by Size](../frontend/ui.md#select-a-command-surface-by-size) | rule | guidance | Commands use the surface the Concepts table assigns to their input. Look in the command surfaces. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#select-a-tone-from-the-closed-set | [Select a Tone from the Closed Set](../frontend/ui.md#select-a-tone-from-the-closed-set) | rule | guidance | Applications declare their state-to-tone mapping once as an exhaustive table of glossary terms. Look in the application state mapping. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#select-one-visual-authority | [Select One Visual Authority](../frontend/ui.md#select-one-visual-authority) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` reports controlled frontends with no `paths.uiPackage` in `litespec.project.json`, and `litespec ui` reports `[litespec/frontend/ui#select-one-visual-authority]` for a second general-purpose visual dependency. Run either at the consumer root. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#show-the-trace-id-in-error-detail | [Show the Trace ID in Error Detail](../frontend/ui.md#show-the-trace-id-in-error-detail) | rule | guidance | Visible errors show the trace identifier copyably in the detail, from the problem body or the `X-Trace-Id` header. Look in the error surfaces. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#use-the-product-profiles | [Use the Product Profiles](../frontend/ui.md#use-the-product-profiles) | convention | checkable | `litespec ui` reports a controlled frontend with no `ui` block declaring its density profile or a profile outside `public-light`, `application-balanced`, `admin-dense`. Run it at the consumer root. |
| litespec/frontend/web/components#compose-class-names-in-the-shared-package | [Compose Class Names in the Shared Package](../frontend/web/components.md#compose-class-names-in-the-shared-package) | convention | guidance | Every class merge in the workspace imports the merge helper from the shared package, with no merge or variant helper import in route or module code. |
| litespec/frontend/web/components#give-card-and-section-titles-heading-semantics | [Give Card and Section Titles Heading Semantics](../frontend/web/components.md#give-card-and-section-titles-heading-semantics) | convention | checkable | `tests/routes/every-route.spec.ts` resolves each visible card, panel, and section title as a heading at its level. |
| litespec/frontend/web/components#keep-primitives-and-composites-in-one-shared-package | [Keep Primitives and Composites in One Shared Package](../frontend/web/components.md#keep-primitives-and-composites-in-one-shared-package) | rule | guidance | The workspace packages hold the registry source and the composites in one shared package, with no primitive or composite copy under an application. |
| litespec/frontend/web/components#meet-accessibility-requirements | [Meet Accessibility Requirements](../frontend/web/components.md#meet-accessibility-requirements) | rule | checkable | `tests/routes/every-route.spec.ts` tabs through every route and fails on an unreachable control, a missing focus indicator, an undersized target, or a silent status change. |
| litespec/frontend/web/components#place-a-drawing-no-composite-makes-in-a-visual-file | [Place a Drawing No Composite Makes in a Visual File](../frontend/web/components.md#place-a-drawing-no-composite-makes-in-a-visual-file) | rule | guidance | Each visual file named on its stack page uses semantic theme tokens only, and class names appear in no other route or module file. |
| litespec/frontend/web/components#protect-rich-content-boundaries | [Protect Rich Content Boundaries](../frontend/web/components.md#protect-rich-content-boundaries) | rule | guidance | Each stored-rich-content decision record names its sanitizer, allowed elements and attributes, link policy, and test cases, and no component passes untrusted content to the sink its stack page names without one. |
| litespec/frontend/web/components#refuse-page-code-violations-with-eslint | [Refuse Page Code Violations with ESLint](../frontend/web/components.md#refuse-page-code-violations-with-eslint) | rule | checkable | `pnpm eslint` reports no refused element, binding, or layout utility outside visual files in route and module code. |
| litespec/frontend/web/components#use-no-raw-element-in-page-code | [Use No Raw Element in Page Code](../frontend/web/components.md#use-no-raw-element-in-page-code) | rule | checkable | `pnpm eslint` refuses each raw layout or interactive element in route and module code, naming the composite to use. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#assign-state-to-the-narrowest-owner | [Assign State to the Narrowest Owner](../frontend/web/data.md#assign-state-to-the-narrowest-owner) | rule | guidance | Each value lives in the query cache, the address, form state, or the component state its stack page names, whichever is narrowest. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#build-a-form-on-the-shared-form-library | [Build a Form on the Shared Form Library](../frontend/web/data.md#build-a-form-on-the-shared-form-library) | convention | guidance | Each form builds on the form entry point its stack page names with the shared registered field components. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#generate-the-client-from-the-published-contract | [Generate the Client from the Published Contract](../frontend/web/data.md#generate-the-client-from-the-published-contract) | rule | checkable | `pnpm api:types` regenerates the client from the published OpenAPI document, and the contract check fails when the committed output differs. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#keep-the-client-boundary-in-the-frame | [Keep the Client Boundary in the Frame](../frontend/web/data.md#keep-the-client-boundary-in-the-frame) | convention | guidance | Client configuration, problem mapping, and download helpers sit only in the frame root the stack page names. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#localize-copy-through-locale-dictionaries | [Localize Copy Through Locale Dictionaries](../frontend/web/data.md#localize-copy-through-locale-dictionaries) | rule | guidance | All copy resolves through the dictionary library its stack page names, with fill, plurals, one chunk per language, and typed keys with a declared fallback. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#map-every-failure-to-one-problem-type | [Map Every Failure to One Problem Type](../frontend/web/data.md#map-every-failure-to-one-problem-type) | rule | guidance | Each rendered error carries one problem shape decided from its code, with the `traceId` in copyable form in its detail. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#place-a-refusal-beside-what-was-refused | [Place a Refusal Beside What Was Refused](../frontend/web/data.md#place-a-refusal-beside-what-was-refused) | rule | guidance | Each refusal renders beside its refused record or control through the shared alert, in the application's sentence for that code. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#regenerate-the-client-rather-than-editing-it | [Regenerate the Client Rather Than Editing It](../frontend/web/data.md#regenerate-the-client-rather-than-editing-it) | convention | checkable | `pnpm api:types` regenerates the client, and committed output that differs from generated output fails the contract check. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#update-the-cache-a-command-changed | [Update the Cache a Command Changed](../frontend/web/data.md#update-the-cache-a-command-changed) | rule | guidance | Each successful command invalidates the TanStack Query entries its command changed and no others. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#validate-a-form-with-the-generated-schema | [Validate a Form with the Generated Schema](../frontend/web/data.md#validate-a-form-with-the-generated-schema) | rule | guidance | Each form validates against the generated schema extended with the application's own rules, and a specification submitting contract-invalid input sees a client-side refusal. |
| litespec/frontend/web/patterns#implement-a-chosen-pattern-once | [Implement a Chosen Pattern Once](../frontend/web/patterns.md#implement-a-chosen-pattern-once) | rule | checkable | `litespec ui` reports each named React or Svelte component path that does not exist. |
| litespec/frontend/web/patterns#keep-a-visual-registry | [Keep a Visual Registry](../frontend/web/patterns.md#keep-a-visual-registry) | convention | guidance | The product registry renders every product-added option at 390 and 1280 pixels, and the pattern's page on the LiteSpec site draws every LiteSpec option at 390 and 1200. |
| litespec/frontend/web/react/components#keep-component-boundaries-typed | [Keep Component Boundaries Typed](../frontend/web/react/components.md#keep-component-boundaries-typed) | rule | checkable | `pnpm type-check` runs under strict mode and fails on an untyped prop or event. |
| litespec/frontend/web/react/components#write-components-as-functions | [Write Components as Functions](../frontend/web/react/components.md#write-components-as-functions) | rule | guidance | Every component in the workspace is a function component using hooks, with no class component. |
| litespec/frontend/web/react/stack#build-a-device-client-as-one-static-page | [Build a Device Client as One Static Page](../frontend/web/react/stack.md#build-a-device-client-as-one-static-page) | rule | guidance | Each device client ships as one static page with no router. |
| litespec/frontend/web/react/stack#select-a-rendering-mode-by-audience | [Select a Rendering Mode by Audience](../frontend/web/react/stack.md#select-a-rendering-mode-by-audience) | rule | guidance | Each frontend renders in the mode its audience requires: static files for a console, server rendering for an indexed public page. |
| litespec/frontend/web/rendering#build-the-server-query-cache-per-request | [Build the Server Query Cache per Request](../frontend/web/rendering.md#build-the-server-query-cache-per-request) | rule | checkable | `grep -c createQueryClient` on React or `grep -c 'new QueryClient'` on Svelte counts one construction in the request factory. |
| litespec/frontend/web/rendering#define-route-metadata-deliberately | [Define Route Metadata Deliberately](../frontend/web/rendering.md#define-route-metadata-deliberately) | rule | guidance | Each public route defines its title, description, canonical address, and indexing policy, and each private route ships a `noindex` marker. |
| litespec/frontend/web/rendering#keep-authenticated-caching-explicit | [Keep Authenticated Caching Explicit](../frontend/web/rendering.md#keep-authenticated-caching-explicit) | rule | guidance | Each cached actor-specific read declares its cache key, partition, invalidation owner, and security review beside its query definition. |
| litespec/frontend/web/rendering#keep-proxy-behavior-at-the-edge | [Keep Proxy Behavior at the Edge](../frontend/web/rendering.md#keep-proxy-behavior-at-the-edge) | rule | guidance | Every development and edge proxy rule routes only, deciding neither the actor nor what the actor may read. |
| litespec/frontend/web/rendering#keep-search-parameters-validated-by-a-schema | [Keep Search Parameters Validated by a Schema](../frontend/web/rendering.md#keep-search-parameters-validated-by-a-schema) | rule | guidance | Each route declares its search parameters in a validated schema, with no component reading the address directly. |
| litespec/frontend/web/rendering#load-a-route-on-intent | [Load a Route on Intent](../frontend/web/rendering.md#load-a-route-on-intent) | rule | guidance | The router preloads the route a pointer is over, holding no cache beyond the query cache. |
| litespec/frontend/web/rendering#load-route-data-in-the-loader | [Load Route Data in the Loader](../frontend/web/rendering.md#load-route-data-in-the-loader) | rule | guidance | Each route that reads declares a loader prefetching those reads through the query cache. |
| litespec/frontend/web/rendering#read-route-data-with-a-suspense-query | [Read Route Data with a Suspense Query](../frontend/web/rendering.md#read-route-data-with-a-suspense-query) | rule | guidance | Each component read names the cache key its loader filled. |
| litespec/frontend/web/rendering#split-one-chunk-per-route-and-per-language | [Split One Chunk per Route and per Language](../frontend/web/rendering.md#split-one-chunk-per-route-and-per-language) | rule | checkable | `pnpm build` reports one output chunk per route and one per language, with no icon set or language imported whole. |
| litespec/frontend/web/rendering#use-a-pathless-layout-route-for-a-shell | [Use a Pathless Layout Route for a Shell](../frontend/web/rendering.md#use-a-pathless-layout-route-for-a-shell) | convention | guidance | Each shell sits in a shared layout holding the frame every child route shares. |
| litespec/frontend/web/structure#generate-the-records-file-into-the-frame | [Generate the Records File into the Frame](../frontend/web/structure.md#generate-the-records-file-into-the-frame) | rule | checkable | `litespec model build` writes each frontend's file into its frame from its own entry points, and `litespec model check` reports `mark-unknown` (level S) for a mark naming a use case or screen outside its frontend's entry points. |
| litespec/frontend/web/structure#keep-a-route-file-thin | [Keep a Route File Thin](../frontend/web/structure.md#keep-a-route-file-thin) | rule | guidance | Each route file holds only its input, its load, and its component, importing no other route file. |
| litespec/frontend/web/structure#keep-imports-directional | [Keep Imports Directional](../frontend/web/structure.md#keep-imports-directional) | rule | guidance | Every import follows the route to module to frame to package direction. |
| litespec/frontend/web/structure#keep-tests-near-their-ownership-boundary | [Keep Tests Near Their Ownership Boundary](../frontend/web/structure.md#keep-tests-near-their-ownership-boundary) | convention | guidance | Each logic test sits beside its module as `*.test.ts`, and the browser suites share one `tests/` root per frontend. |
| litespec/frontend/web/structure#locate-the-frame-in-the-app-folder | [Locate the Frame in the App Folder](../frontend/web/structure.md#locate-the-frame-in-the-app-folder) | rule | guidance | The frame folder its stack page names holds every cross-route value and browser use. |
| litespec/frontend/web/structure#mark-each-use-case-module-with-its-use-case | [Mark Each Use-Case Module with Its Use Case](../frontend/web/structure.md#mark-each-use-case-module-with-its-use-case) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `mark-missing` (level S) for a use-case folder exporting no `litespec` constant, and `mark-unknown` (level S) for one naming a use case its path does not mirror; for a folder at `{module}/reads/{use-case}/` the mirrored use case is the one its module and use-case segments name, with no aggregate segment. |
| litespec/frontend/web/structure#refuse-stack-specific-folder-names | [Refuse Stack-Specific Folder Names](../frontend/web/structure.md#refuse-stack-specific-folder-names) | rule | guidance | No React folder takes the name `lib`, and no Svelte folder under `src/lib` takes the name `stores`. |
| litespec/frontend/web/structure#use-explicit-public-entry-points-for-workspace-packages | [Use Explicit Public Entry Points for Workspace Packages](../frontend/web/structure.md#use-explicit-public-entry-points-for-workspace-packages) | convention | guidance | Every application import resolves to the package root or a documented subpath its package manifest exports. |
| litespec/frontend/web/structure#use-the-frontend-application-tree | [Use the Frontend Application Tree](../frontend/web/structure.md#use-the-frontend-application-tree) | rule | guidance | Each frontend tree matches the frame, routes, and modules layout its stack page states. |
| litespec/frontend/web/structure#use-the-generated-route-tree | [Use the Generated Route Tree](../frontend/web/structure.md#use-the-generated-route-tree) | convention | guidance | The build and type-check scripts each run the router's generate command, and the generated artifacts stay untracked. |
| litespec/frontend/web/svelte/components#keep-component-boundaries-typed | [Keep Component Boundaries Typed](../frontend/web/svelte/components.md#keep-component-boundaries-typed) | rule | checkable | `svelte-check` runs under strict mode and fails on an untyped prop, event, or snippet. |
| litespec/frontend/web/svelte/components#write-components-in-runes-mode | [Write Components in Runes Mode](../frontend/web/svelte/components.md#write-components-in-runes-mode) | rule | checkable | `svelte-check` fails on a legacy reactive statement or an untyped `$props` call. |
| litespec/frontend/web/svelte/stack#build-a-device-client-as-one-static-page | [Build a Device Client as One Static Page](../frontend/web/svelte/stack.md#build-a-device-client-as-one-static-page) | rule | guidance | Each device client ships as one static page with no router. |
| litespec/frontend/web/svelte/stack#select-a-rendering-mode-by-audience | [Select a Rendering Mode by Audience](../frontend/web/svelte/stack.md#select-a-rendering-mode-by-audience) | rule | guidance | Each frontend renders in the mode its audience requires: static files for a console, server rendering for an indexed public page. |
| litespec/frontend/web/testing#assert-each-declared-refusal-by-its-code | [Assert Each Declared Refusal by Its Code](../frontend/web/testing.md#assert-each-declared-refusal-by-its-code) | rule | guidance | Each evidence specification for a use case declaring a refusal reaches that refusal and asserts its result code in the page. |
| litespec/frontend/web/testing#gate-changed-lines-with-mutation-testing | [Gate Changed Lines with Mutation Testing](../frontend/web/testing.md#gate-changed-lines-with-mutation-testing) | rule | checkable | `pnpm stryker run --incremental` reports zero survived mutants on the changed lines in the pull request's mutation job. |
| litespec/frontend/web/testing#keep-mocks-at-owned-boundaries | [Keep Mocks at Owned Boundaries](../frontend/web/testing.md#keep-mocks-at-owned-boundaries) | rule | guidance | No test replaces a library its stack page names. |
| litespec/frontend/web/testing#match-test-level-to-risk | [Match Test Level to Risk](../frontend/web/testing.md#match-test-level-to-risk) | rule | guidance | Each test uses Vitest beside its module for pure logic and Playwright against a running stack for navigation and integration, with the route contract visiting every address. |
| litespec/frontend/web/testing#prove-the-module-mirror-in-a-structure-test | [Prove the Module Mirror in a Structure Test](../frontend/web/testing.md#prove-the-module-mirror-in-a-structure-test) | rule | guidance | The Vitest structure test resolves each module folder to a backend use case and names no refused folder. |
| litespec/frontend/web/testing#query-by-accessible-behavior | [Query by Accessible Behavior](../frontend/web/testing.md#query-by-accessible-behavior) | convention | guidance | Each browser query scopes to one floorplan through `data-floorplan` and addresses slot content by accessible role and name. |
| litespec/frontend/web/testing#refuse-a-test-without-an-assertion | [Refuse a Test Without an Assertion](../frontend/web/testing.md#refuse-a-test-without-an-assertion) | rule | checkable | `pnpm eslint` reports `vitest/expect-expect` for each test that contains no assertion. |
| litespec/frontend/web/testing#run-browser-checks-through-the-playwright-cli | [Run Browser Checks Through the Playwright CLI](../frontend/web/testing.md#run-browser-checks-through-the-playwright-cli) | rule | guidance | Each browser suite runs through the Playwright CLI, addressable per route and per file. |
| litespec/frontend/web/testing#seed-property-tests-from-example-tables | [Seed Property Tests from Example Tables](../frontend/web/testing.md#seed-property-tests-from-example-tables) | rule | guidance | Each TypeScript calculation's property test imports fast-check and lists the examples-table rows as its seed cases. |
| litespec/frontend/web/testing#use-network-mocks-for-component-tests | [Use Network Mocks for Component Tests](../frontend/web/testing.md#use-network-mocks-for-component-tests) | rule | guidance | Each component or logic test needing HTTP serves answers through `msw` handlers generated from the published contract. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#check-every-route-in-a-browser | [Check Every Route in a Browser](../frontend/web/ui.md#check-every-route-in-a-browser) | rule | checkable | `pnpm playwright test tests/routes/every-route.spec.ts` runs axe and the Concepts checks on each discovered route, and `litespec ui` reports each route missing its suite. The Playwright run is authoritative for floorplan presence, while `litespec ui` owns suite existence. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#compose-each-route-from-one-floorplan | [Compose Each Route from One Floorplan](../frontend/web/ui.md#compose-each-route-from-one-floorplan) | rule | checkable | `pnpm playwright test tests/routes/every-route.spec.ts` asserts `page.locator("[data-floorplan]")` has count 1 on every route, and `litespec ui` reports each route missing its suite. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#derive-each-page-type-from-a-named-reference | [Derive Each Page Type from a Named Reference](../frontend/web/ui.md#derive-each-page-type-from-a-named-reference) | rule | checkable | `pnpm playwright test tests/routes/every-route.spec.ts` asserts `data-registry-item` resolves to a published registry item on every non-freestyle route, and `litespec ui` reports each route missing its suite. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#hold-the-declared-base-color | [Hold the Declared Base Color](../frontend/web/ui.md#hold-the-declared-base-color) | rule | checkable | `pnpm stylelint` reports each token value outside the declared base color ramp. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#keep-layout-inside-floorplans | [Keep Layout Inside Floorplans](../frontend/web/ui.md#keep-layout-inside-floorplans) | rule | checkable | `pnpm eslint` refuses each layout utility in route code, naming the floorplan or composite to use. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#keep-tokens-in-one-stylesheet | [Keep Tokens in One Stylesheet](../frontend/web/ui.md#keep-tokens-in-one-stylesheet) | rule | checkable | `litespec ui` reports each application stylesheet declaring a rule and each shared stylesheet declaring a literal color. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#mark-the-route-suite-and-goldens-with-what-they-prove | [Mark the Route Suite and Goldens with What They Prove](../frontend/web/ui.md#mark-the-route-suite-and-goldens-with-what-they-prove) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `mark-missing` (level S) for a test in a frontend's `routeSuite` or `visualGoldens` with no `proves` annotation, and `mark-unknown` (level S) for one naming a scenario. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#pin-the-baseline-in-the-shared-package | [Pin the Baseline in the Shared Package](../frontend/web/ui.md#pin-the-baseline-in-the-shared-package) | rule | checkable | `litespec ui` compares the committed configuration against the manifest baseline fields and reports each mismatch. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#prove-every-result-a-route-calls | [Prove Every Result a Route Calls](../frontend/web/ui.md#prove-every-result-a-route-calls) | rule | guidance | Which refusals a web screen reaches through its computed `calls` lack a dictionary sentence and a browser test asserting the code? Look at the `refusal-sentence-missing` findings for the frontend. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#prove-ui-behavior-and-appearance | [Prove UI Behavior and Appearance](../frontend/web/ui.md#prove-ui-behavior-and-appearance) | rule | checkable | `pnpm playwright test tests/routes/every-route.spec.ts` runs on the changed route, and review confirms the change names the governance matrix evidence for its class. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#publish-one-registry-item-per-floorplan | [Publish One Registry Item per Floorplan](../frontend/web/ui.md#publish-one-registry-item-per-floorplan) | rule | checkable | `shadcn build` on React or `shadcn-svelte build` on Svelte exits zero, and `registry.json` lists one item per declared floorplan. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#refuse-token-violations-with-stylelint | [Refuse Token Violations with Stylelint](../frontend/web/ui.md#refuse-token-violations-with-stylelint) | rule | checkable | `pnpm stylelint` reports no color, spacing, or type value outside the shared tokens in application or shared stylesheets. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#restrict-css-decisions | [Restrict CSS Decisions](../frontend/web/ui.md#restrict-css-decisions) | rule | checkable | `litespec ui` reports each arbitrary value, raw palette value, important modifier, and inline style with its file. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#select-a-tone-from-the-closed-set | [Select a Tone from the Closed Set](../frontend/web/ui.md#select-a-tone-from-the-closed-set) | rule | checkable | `pnpm type-check` fails on a state with no tone, and the state-to-tone table holds glossary terms only. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#track-source-changes | [Track Source Changes](../frontend/web/ui.md#track-source-changes) | rule | checkable | `litespec ui` recomputes each digest and reports changed primitive source. |
| litespec/frontend/web/ui#use-the-source-update-procedure | [Use the Source Update Procedure](../frontend/web/ui.md#use-the-source-update-procedure) | convention | guidance | Each update branch pins the CLI, records the dry-run output, and reviews regenerated files and local composites separately. |

## quality

| ID | Provision | Kind | Mark | Evidence |
|:---|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| litespec/quality/ci#apply-the-documented-defaults | [Apply the Documented Defaults](../quality/ci.md#apply-the-documented-defaults) | convention | guidance | Each consumer workflow file matches one jobs-table job and name, or the workflow-layout convention names another arrangement. |
| litespec/quality/ci#approve-breaking-changes-by-the-highest-record-owner | [Approve Breaking Changes by the Highest Record Owner](../quality/ci.md#approve-breaking-changes-by-the-highest-record-owner) | rule | guidance | Each pull request with a breaking change shows approval from the team owning the highest record it touches. |
| litespec/quality/ci#check-documentation-on-every-pull-request | [Check Documentation on Every Pull Request](../quality/ci.md#check-documentation-on-every-pull-request) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails on a page that breaks the page contract, configuration, language, or prose rules. |
| litespec/quality/ci#enforce-the-supply-chain-gate | [Enforce the Supply-Chain Gate](../quality/ci.md#enforce-the-supply-chain-gate) | rule | guidance | The supply-chain report shows scan, pin, inventory, and license evidence with an exception for each unhandled high-severity advisory. |
| litespec/quality/ci#keep-a-canonical-job-graph | [Keep a Canonical Job Graph](../quality/ci.md#keep-a-canonical-job-graph) | rule | guidance | The consumer workflows use the job names and responsibilities the jobs table states. |
| litespec/quality/ci#keep-generated-contracts-fresh | [Keep Generated Contracts Fresh](../quality/ci.md#keep-generated-contracts-fresh) | rule | guidance | Regenerating OpenAPI and API types from source on the pull request produces no diff. |
| litespec/quality/ci#map-record-areas-to-owners | [Map Record Areas to Owners](../quality/ci.md#map-record-areas-to-owners) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `owner-unknown` for a record whose owner belongs to no team owning its path, directly or through its mapped handles. |
| litespec/quality/ci#promote-verified-artifacts | [Promote Verified Artifacts](../quality/ci.md#promote-verified-artifacts) | rule | guidance | The staging and production deployments reference the one artifact the release job passed. |
| litespec/quality/ci#protect-the-default-branch | [Protect the Default Branch](../quality/ci.md#protect-the-default-branch) | rule | guidance | The default branch settings require the jobs-table entries, a reviewed pull request, and a clean merge state. |
| litespec/quality/ci#publish-a-book-preview-for-every-change | [Publish a Book Preview for Every Change](../quality/ci.md#publish-a-book-preview-for-every-change) | rule | guidance | The pull request links a book preview built from the change. |
| litespec/quality/ci#publish-the-intent-diff-on-every-pull-request | [Publish the Intent Diff on Every Pull Request](../quality/ci.md#publish-the-intent-diff-on-every-pull-request) | rule | guidance | The pull request shows one intent-diff comment matching its latest push. |
| litespec/quality/ci#record-every-branch-protection-bypass | [Record Every Branch Protection Bypass](../quality/ci.md#record-every-branch-protection-bypass) | rule | guidance | Each bypass entry in the operations log or recovery decision names its actor, reason, and admitted change. |
| litespec/quality/ci#refuse-backend-calls-outside-the-generated-client | [Refuse Backend Calls Outside the Generated Client](../quality/ci.md#refuse-backend-calls-outside-the-generated-client) | rule | guidance | The contract job report shows a clean regeneration and a lint report with no backend call outside the generated client. |
| litespec/quality/ci#require-zero-surviving-mutants-on-changed-lines | [Require Zero Surviving Mutants on Changed Lines](../quality/ci.md#require-zero-surviving-mutants-on-changed-lines) | rule | guidance | The mutation job report for the pull request shows zero surviving mutants on its changed lines. |
| litespec/quality/ci#review-schema-artifacts | [Review Schema Artifacts](../quality/ci.md#review-schema-artifacts) | rule | guidance | Each persistence change carries a schema artifact naming its reviewer with every destructive operation and its reason. |
| litespec/quality/ci#run-applicable-gates-on-every-pull-request | [Run Applicable Gates on Every Pull Request](../quality/ci.md#run-applicable-gates-on-every-pull-request) | rule | guidance | The pull request checks list shows each selected job passing, with skipped jobs recorded with their reason. |
| litespec/quality/ci#run-measured-checks-on-every-pull-request | [Run Measured Checks on Every Pull Request](../quality/ci.md#run-measured-checks-on-every-pull-request) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `requirement-measure-missing` for a measured requirement with no check or no passing run. |
| litespec/quality/ci#show-intent-counts-in-the-job-summary | [Show Intent Counts in the Job Summary](../quality/ci.md#show-intent-counts-in-the-job-summary) | rule | guidance | The CI run summary for the pull request lists the breaking, removed, modified, and added counts. |
| litespec/quality/operations#apply-schema-changes-outside-request-startup | [Apply Schema Changes Outside Request Startup](../quality/operations.md#apply-schema-changes-outside-request-startup) | rule | guidance | The schema step runs before traffic shifts, with no schema work at replica startup. |
| litespec/quality/operations#attach-dashboard-links-to-production-objectives | [Attach Dashboard Links to Production Objectives](../quality/operations.md#attach-dashboard-links-to-production-objectives) | rule | guidance | Each production objective in the book links its dashboard. |
| litespec/quality/operations#bound-external-calls | [Bound External Calls](../quality/operations.md#bound-external-calls) | rule | guidance | Each network call declares a timeout, a cancellation path, and bounded retries for idempotent operations only. |
| litespec/quality/operations#cover-every-quality-requirement-with-an-alert-or-a-check | [Cover Every Quality Requirement with an Alert or a Check](../quality/operations.md#cover-every-quality-requirement-with-an-alert-or-a-check) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `quality-alert-missing` (level G) for a quality requirement that no alert rule names in its `requirement` label, whose `check` matches no measured-check or check result the model read, and that no approved gap exception covering `quality-alert-missing` lists in its `subject`. |
| litespec/quality/operations#cover-the-baseline-alert-set | [Cover the Baseline Alert Set](../quality/operations.md#cover-the-baseline-alert-set) | rule | guidance | The baseline alerts cover readiness failures, unexpected HTTP errors, release-flow latency, PostgreSQL outages, and failed deployments. |
| litespec/quality/operations#define-actionable-baseline-alerts | [Define Actionable Baseline Alerts](../quality/operations.md#define-actionable-baseline-alerts) | rule | guidance | Each baseline alert declares owner, threshold, window, severity, and runbook. |
| litespec/quality/operations#define-backup-and-restore-behavior | [Define Backup and Restore Behavior](../quality/operations.md#define-backup-and-restore-behavior) | rule | guidance | The release documentation states backup owner, schedule, retention, encryption, location, restore command, and recovery objectives. |
| litespec/quality/operations#emit-correlated-diagnostics | [Emit Correlated Diagnostics](../quality/operations.md#emit-correlated-diagnostics) | rule | guidance | Logs and traces carry correlation identifiers, and metrics carry service labels. |
| litespec/quality/operations#exercise-a-restore-before-the-first-production-data | [Exercise a Restore Before the First Production Data](../quality/operations.md#exercise-a-restore-before-the-first-production-data) | rule | guidance | The operations log holds a restore rehearsal on representative data before the first production data. |
| litespec/quality/operations#exercise-every-runbook-before-release | [Exercise Every Runbook Before Release](../quality/operations.md#exercise-every-runbook-before-release) | rule | guidance | Each required runbook carries a dated rehearsal in the operations log. |
| litespec/quality/operations#keep-deployment-descriptors-together | [Keep Deployment Descriptors Together](../quality/operations.md#keep-deployment-descriptors-together) | convention | guidance | The deployment descriptors sit under one `deploy/` tree, or the deployment-layout convention names another arrangement. |
| litespec/quality/operations#keep-each-alert-threshold-to-its-requirements-measure | [Keep Each Alert Threshold to Its Requirement's Measure](../quality/operations.md#keep-each-alert-threshold-to-its-requirements-measure) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `alert-threshold-drift` (level A) for an alert rule whose own text carries none of the decimal numbers its requirement's `measure` names. |
| litespec/quality/operations#keep-production-secrets-out-of-the-repository | [Keep Production Secrets Out of the Repository](../quality/operations.md#keep-production-secrets-out-of-the-repository) | rule | guidance | Production values live on the host, with only placeholders in the repository example file. |
| litespec/quality/operations#keep-rollback-executable | [Keep Rollback Executable](../quality/operations.md#keep-rollback-executable) | rule | guidance | The rollback runbook states its artifact reference, command, compatibility, data condition, and expand-and-contract sequence. |
| litespec/quality/operations#keep-runbooks-near-project-documentation | [Keep Runbooks Near Project Documentation](../quality/operations.md#keep-runbooks-near-project-documentation) | convention | guidance | Each runbook sits under `docs/behavior/runbooks/` with its sections, or the runbook-layout convention names another arrangement. |
| litespec/quality/operations#list-every-sub-processor | [List Every Sub-Processor](../quality/operations.md#list-every-sub-processor) | rule | guidance | Each provider the release calls appears on the generated sub-processor list. |
| litespec/quality/operations#name-the-requirement-and-runbook-on-every-alert | [Name the Requirement and Runbook on Every Alert](../quality/operations.md#name-the-requirement-and-runbook-on-every-alert) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `alert-link-missing` (level S) for an alert rule in the files `paths.alerts` names whose `requirement` label is missing or names no quality requirement, or whose `runbook` label is missing or names no runbook. |
| litespec/quality/operations#operate-background-work-independently | [Operate Background Work Independently](../quality/operations.md#operate-background-work-independently) | rule | guidance | The Worker publishes its own health, rates, counts, oldest pending age, and shutdown behavior. |
| litespec/quality/operations#pass-trace-context-in-the-w3c-format | [Pass Trace Context in the W3C Format](../quality/operations.md#pass-trace-context-in-the-w3c-format) | rule | guidance | One propagator reads and writes W3C trace context on every host. |
| litespec/quality/operations#point-each-operations-file-at-its-decision | [Point Each Operations File at Its Decision](../quality/operations.md#point-each-operations-file-at-its-decision) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `mark-missing` (level S) for a file in `paths.operations` with no `follows` mark and `mark-unknown` (level S) for a mark naming no decision, exception, or provision, and counts the file toward `decision-unimplemented`. |
| litespec/quality/operations#point-every-runtime-record-at-its-use-case | [Point Every Runtime Record at Its Use Case](../quality/operations.md#point-every-runtime-record-at-its-use-case) | rule | guidance | Which pipeline stage reads the registry and stamps `litespec.use_case` on the span, the log scope, and the error report of each mediated message? Look at the mediator pipeline and one error report per host. |
| litespec/quality/operations#record-each-drill-run-as-a-check-result | [Record Each Drill Run as a Check Result](../quality/operations.md#record-each-drill-run-as-a-check-result) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reads every check result under `paths.checkResults` beside the measured-check results under the test results folder and counts a passing one toward its requirement's measure. |
| litespec/quality/operations#report-liveness-from-every-recurring-job | [Report Liveness from Every Recurring Job](../quality/operations.md#report-liveness-from-every-recurring-job) | rule | guidance | Each recurring job reports liveness that pages a person on silence. |
| litespec/quality/operations#route-each-severity-to-a-declared-destination | [Route Each Severity to a Declared Destination](../quality/operations.md#route-each-severity-to-a-declared-destination) | rule | guidance | Each severity states its destination and expected response time on the limits page. |
| litespec/quality/operations#separate-liveness-and-readiness | [Separate Liveness and Readiness](../quality/operations.md#separate-liveness-and-readiness) | rule | guidance | Liveness answers without dependency checks while readiness includes the critical ones. |
| litespec/quality/operations#start-the-stack-with-one-local-command | [Start the Stack with One Local Command](../quality/operations.md#start-the-stack-with-one-local-command) | rule | guidance | One command named in the consumer `AGENTS.md` starts WebApi, PostgreSQL, the frontends, and the Worker. |
| litespec/quality/operations#state-availability-objectives-as-openslo-files | [State Availability Objectives as OpenSLO Files](../quality/operations.md#state-availability-objectives-as-openslo-files) | rule | guidance | The OpenSLO files pass Sloth validation with their generated alerts loaded. |
| litespec/quality/operations#use-a-repeatable-deployment | [Use a Repeatable Deployment](../quality/operations.md#use-a-repeatable-deployment) | rule | guidance | Each deployment names versioned artifacts, declared configuration, a schema step, readiness checks, and a passing release job. |
| litespec/quality/operations#use-stable-service-names | [Use Stable Service Names](../quality/operations.md#use-stable-service-names) | convention | guidance | Service, resource, meter, and trace-source names match across environments, or the service-naming convention names another arrangement. |
| litespec/quality/security#apply-erasure-and-export-to-every-store | [Apply Erasure and Export to Every Store](../quality/security.md#apply-erasure-and-export-to-every-store) | rule | guidance | Erasure and export reach every store on the inventory, with backups expiring inside their bound. |
| litespec/quality/security#authorize-each-target-resource | [Authorize Each Target Resource](../quality/security.md#authorize-each-target-resource) | rule | guidance | Each protected operation passes the application guard from its use case's declared permissions. |
| litespec/quality/security#bound-abuse-at-exposed-endpoints | [Bound Abuse at Exposed Endpoints](../quality/security.md#bound-abuse-at-exposed-endpoints) | rule | guidance | Each public, authentication, webhook, search, and upload endpoint declares its setting with key, owner, and rejection response. |
| litespec/quality/security#derive-the-actor-from-claims | [Derive the Actor from Claims](../quality/security.md#derive-the-actor-from-claims) | rule | guidance | Actor resolution in WebApi maps verified claims through the one current-actor accessor. |
| litespec/quality/security#keep-a-personal-data-inventory-per-store | [Keep a Personal-Data Inventory per Store](../quality/security.md#keep-a-personal-data-inventory-per-store) | rule | guidance | The inventory names the fields, purposes, and retention of each store holding personal data. |
| litespec/quality/security#keep-backend-authentication-provider-neutral | [Keep Backend Authentication Provider-Neutral](../quality/security.md#keep-backend-authentication-provider-neutral) | rule | guidance | The backend authentication setup validates issuer, audience, signature, lifetime, and required claims, with no identity header accepted. |
| litespec/quality/security#keep-one-limit-store-across-replicas | [Keep One Limit Store Across Replicas](../quality/security.md#keep-one-limit-store-across-replicas) | rule | guidance | The deployment reads rate and concurrency counters from one store on every replica. |
| litespec/quality/security#keep-personal-data-out-of-logs | [Keep Personal Data Out of Logs](../quality/security.md#keep-personal-data-out-of-logs) | rule | guidance | Logs, traces, and diagnostic records carry no personal data in clear. |
| litespec/quality/security#keep-secrets-out-of-tracked-and-observable-data | [Keep Secrets Out of Tracked and Observable Data](../quality/security.md#keep-secrets-out-of-tracked-and-observable-data) | rule | guidance | Source, logs, traces, messages, files, URLs, snapshots, layers, and browser storage hold no secret. |
| litespec/quality/security#keep-secure-headers-in-host-configuration | [Keep Secure Headers in Host Configuration](../quality/security.md#keep-secure-headers-in-host-configuration) | convention | guidance | The secure headers sit in one host location, or the secure-headers convention names another arrangement. |
| litespec/quality/security#limit-public-error-detail | [Limit Public Error Detail](../quality/security.md#limit-public-error-detail) | rule | guidance | Each public error carries the HTTP API error contract shape. |
| litespec/quality/security#minimize-sensitive-data | [Minimize Sensitive Data](../quality/security.md#minimize-sensitive-data) | rule | guidance | Each use case collects, returns, logs, exports, and retains only its declared fields. |
| litespec/quality/security#parameterize-database-input | [Parameterize Database Input](../quality/security.md#parameterize-database-input) | rule | guidance | Each external value reaches the database through a query API call or a SQL parameter. |
| litespec/quality/security#protect-browser-boundaries | [Protect Browser Boundaries](../quality/security.md#protect-browser-boundaries) | rule | guidance | Each frontend ships framework escaping, a Content Security Policy, secure headers, redirect validation, forgery protection, and rich-content sanitization. |
| litespec/quality/security#protect-live-data-at-rest | [Protect Live Data at Rest](../quality/security.md#protect-live-data-at-rest) | rule | guidance | Each live store carries encryption, or an exception record covers this provision. |
| litespec/quality/security#record-security-audit-events | [Record Security Audit Events](../quality/security.md#record-security-audit-events) | rule | guidance | Each security audit event records actor, action, target, outcome, timestamp, trace identifier, and reason. |
| litespec/quality/security#report-a-breach-within-its-deadline | [Report a Breach Within Its Deadline](../quality/security.md#report-a-breach-within-its-deadline) | rule | guidance | The breach procedure names an owner, a backup person, and the deadline the constraint record states. |
| litespec/quality/security#restrict-a-protected-collection-in-the-database | [Restrict a Protected Collection in the Database](../quality/security.md#restrict-a-protected-collection-in-the-database) | rule | guidance | Each protected collection query carries its authorization scope as a database predicate before paging. |
| litespec/quality/security#restrict-cross-origin-access | [Restrict Cross-Origin Access](../quality/security.md#restrict-cross-origin-access) | rule | guidance | The cross-origin policy lists origins, methods, headers, and credential mode with no wildcard origin. |
| litespec/quality/security#rotate-production-secrets | [Rotate Production Secrets](../quality/security.md#rotate-production-secrets) | rule | guidance | Each production secret carries a secret-inventory entry with owner, location, and rotation date beside its revocation runbook. |
| litespec/quality/security#route-tier-1-access-and-data-changes-through-the-security-reviewer | [Route Tier-1 Access and Data Changes Through the Security Reviewer](../quality/security.md#route-tier-1-access-and-data-changes-through-the-security-reviewer) | rule | guidance | Which cross-scope access grants, new calls effects, and new personal-data flows shipped without security review? Look at the security queue of the behavior diff. |
| litespec/quality/security#scan-every-pull-request-with-a-free-static-analyzer | [Scan Every Pull Request with a Free Static Analyzer](../quality/security.md#scan-every-pull-request-with-a-free-static-analyzer) | rule | guidance | The static analysis report for the pull request shows its findings with a gap exception for each accepted one. |
| litespec/quality/security#scan-every-release-with-the-zap-baseline | [Scan Every Release with the ZAP Baseline](../quality/security.md#scan-every-release-with-the-zap-baseline) | rule | guidance | The ZAP result file under `TestResults/` shows no medium or high alert for the pull request. |
| litespec/quality/security#set-an-asvs-level-as-the-security-target | [Set an ASVS Level as the Security Target](../quality/security.md#set-an-asvs-level-as-the-security-target) | rule | guidance | The security quality requirement names its ASVS level with a passing check. |
| litespec/quality/security#test-the-resource-authorization-matrix | [Test the Resource Authorization Matrix](../quality/security.md#test-the-resource-authorization-matrix) | convention | guidance | Each protected operation passes every caller in the matrix, or the authorization-matrix convention names another set. |
| litespec/quality/security#treat-an-unsalted-digest-as-personal-data | [Treat an Unsalted Digest as Personal Data](../quality/security.md#treat-an-unsalted-digest-as-personal-data) | rule | guidance | Each unsalted digest of personal data receives the handling its source data receives. |
| litespec/quality/security#use-deny-by-default-policies | [Use Deny-by-Default Policies](../quality/security.md#use-deny-by-default-policies) | convention | guidance | New endpoints require authentication by default, or the endpoint-protection convention names another arrangement. |
| litespec/quality/security#use-one-current-actor-abstraction | [Use One Current Actor Abstraction](../quality/security.md#use-one-current-actor-abstraction) | convention | guidance | WebApi exposes one current-actor accessor, or the current-actor convention names another arrangement. |
| litespec/quality/security#validate-at-trust-boundaries | [Validate at Trust Boundaries](../quality/security.md#validate-at-trust-boundaries) | rule | guidance | Each trust boundary validates transport shape and file metadata before use. |
