| litespec/workspace/config#validate-backend-configuration-at-startup | [Validate Backend Configuration at Startup](../workspace/config.md#validate-backend-configuration-at-startup) | rule | guidance | Each backend section binds a settings type that fails startup on a missing field, a bad range, or a bad format; read the settings types beside the backend host. |
| litespec/workspace/config#validate-frontend-environment-access | [Validate Frontend Environment Access](../workspace/config.md#validate-frontend-environment-access) | rule | guidance | Each frontend reads environment values only through `src/lib/env.ts`, with server-only values apart from browser-visible ones. |
| litespec/workspace/dependencies#accept-every-shipped-package-license-explicitly | [Accept Every Shipped Package License Explicitly](../workspace/dependencies.md#accept-every-shipped-package-license-explicitly) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `dependency-license-unknown` (level S) for a direct package whose license the project does not accept and no `admits` entry names. |
| litespec/workspace/dependencies#approve-new-packages-explicitly | [Approve New Packages Explicitly](../workspace/dependencies.md#approve-new-packages-explicitly) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `dependency-undeclared` (level S) for a direct package in the bill of materials that the manifest does not pin and no decision admits, and `admission-invalid` (level G) for an `admits` entry missing any of its six facts. |
| litespec/workspace/dependencies#assign-each-baseline-package-to-its-owning-layer | [Assign Each Baseline Package to Its Owning Layer](../workspace/dependencies.md#assign-each-baseline-package-to-its-owning-layer) | convention | guidance | Each baseline package sits in the owning layer the table in this section assigns; where replaced, the admitting decision names the project's own owning layer. |
| litespec/workspace/dependencies#follow-the-declared-dependency-graph | [Follow the Declared Dependency Graph](../workspace/dependencies.md#follow-the-declared-dependency-graph) | rule | guidance | Each layer and module dependency follows the declared graph; read the project references under `apps/api/src` and the imports in `apps/`. |
| litespec/workspace/dependencies#keep-application-free-of-provider-packages | [Keep Application Free of Provider Packages](../workspace/dependencies.md#keep-application-free-of-provider-packages) | rule | guidance | The Application project manifest references only messaging abstractions, read abstractions, and the declared read boundary. |
| litespec/workspace/dependencies#keep-domain-dependency-free | [Keep Domain Dependency-Free](../workspace/dependencies.md#keep-domain-dependency-free) | rule | guidance | The Domain project manifest references no external package or framework. |
| litespec/workspace/dependencies#keep-frontend-applications-isolated | [Keep Frontend Applications Isolated](../workspace/dependencies.md#keep-frontend-applications-isolated) | rule | guidance | No import in one frontend names another application's source or another module's internal feature files. |
| litespec/workspace/dependencies#keep-generated-packages-dependency-light | [Keep Generated Packages Dependency-Light](../workspace/dependencies.md#keep-generated-packages-dependency-light) | convention | guidance | The generated API types package carries no dependencies, and the shared client carries only the generator runtime, its query plugin, and those types; where replaced, the client package manifest names the project's own client dependency set. |
| litespec/workspace/dependencies#keep-providers-in-infrastructure | [Keep Providers in Infrastructure](../workspace/dependencies.md#keep-providers-in-infrastructure) | rule | guidance | Database providers, external SDKs, and resilience, discovery, and serialization adapters appear only in the Infrastructure project manifest. |
| litespec/workspace/dependencies#keep-test-dependencies-in-test-projects | [Keep Test Dependencies in Test Projects](../workspace/dependencies.md#keep-test-dependencies-in-test-projects) | convention | guidance | No production project manifest references an assertion, substitution, host, container, or architecture-test package; where replaced, the project documentation names the shipped helper and its reason. |
| litespec/workspace/dependencies#keep-the-approved-web-ui-dependency-boundary | [Keep the Approved Web UI Dependency Boundary](../workspace/dependencies.md#keep-the-approved-web-ui-dependency-boundary) | rule | guidance | Each React frontend manifest takes UI packages only from `uiBaseline`, and each Svelte one only from `uiBaselineSvelte`, in `litespec.manifest.json`. |
| litespec/workspace/dependencies#match-every-shipped-package-to-its-pin | [Match Every Shipped Package to Its Pin](../workspace/dependencies.md#match-every-shipped-package-to-its-pin) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `dependency-version-drift` (level S) for a direct package whose version differs from its manifest pin. |
| litespec/workspace/dependencies#pin-every-dependency-version-centrally | [Pin Every Dependency Version Centrally](../workspace/dependencies.md#pin-every-dependency-version-centrally) | rule | guidance | Every version resolves through central management or the committed lockfile at the manifest pin where one exists; no version sits inline in a module file. |
| litespec/workspace/naming#align-business-names-across-layers | [Align Business Names Across Layers](../workspace/naming.md#align-business-names-across-layers) | convention | guidance | One business name for each module spans Domain, Application, endpoints, features, and documentation; where replaced, the project documentation names the project's own per-layer names. |
| litespec/workspace/naming#anchor-aggregate-owned-types-on-the-aggregate-root | [Anchor Aggregate-Owned Types on the Aggregate Root](../workspace/naming.md#anchor-aggregate-owned-types-on-the-aggregate-root) | rule | guidance | Each aggregate-owned type name in `apps/api/src` begins with its aggregate root name, through nesting or prefix. |
| litespec/workspace/naming#avoid-generic-type-names | [Avoid Generic Type Names](../workspace/naming.md#avoid-generic-type-names) | convention | guidance | Each type name in `apps/api/src` avoids the listed generic names where a narrower name exists; where replaced, the project documentation names the kept name and its reason. |
| litespec/workspace/naming#derive-boundary-names-from-the-ubiquitous-term | [Derive Boundary Names from the Ubiquitous Term](../workspace/naming.md#derive-boundary-names-from-the-ubiquitous-term) | convention | guidance | Each route segment and JSON field name derives from its current aggregate or ubiquitous term; where replaced, the project documentation names the project's own boundary naming. |
| litespec/workspace/naming#group-event-handlers-by-the-fact-they-handle | [Group Event Handlers by the Fact They Handle](../workspace/naming.md#group-event-handlers-by-the-fact-they-handle) | rule | guidance | Each event handler in `apps/api/src` lives in its `On{Fact}` folder and carries `{Action}On{Fact}Handler`. |
| litespec/workspace/naming#keep-namespaces-aligned-with-folders | [Keep Namespaces Aligned with Folders](../workspace/naming.md#keep-namespaces-aligned-with-folders) | convention | guidance | Each namespace starts with its project name and follows the folders beneath the project root; where replaced, the project documentation names the project's own namespace root. |
| litespec/workspace/naming#name-asynchronous-operations-completely | [Name Asynchronous Operations Completely](../workspace/naming.md#name-asynchronous-operations-completely) | rule | guidance | Each asynchronous operation name reveals asynchrony, and each cancelable operation signature takes cancellation explicitly; read the public signatures in `apps/api/src`. |
| litespec/workspace/naming#name-exceptions-by-failed-rule | [Name Exceptions by Failed Rule](../workspace/naming.md#name-exceptions-by-failed-rule) | rule | guidance | Each exception name states its failed rule, with Domain rejections in `{DomainType}{Reason}Exception` form leading with the aggregate root. |
| litespec/workspace/naming#name-repository-variables-after-their-interface | [Name Repository Variables After Their Interface](../workspace/naming.md#name-repository-variables-after-their-interface) | rule | guidance | Each repository parameter, field, and local in `apps/api/src` carries its interface name without the `I` prefix. |
| litespec/workspace/naming#name-result-parts-after-their-result | [Name Result Parts After Their Result](../workspace/naming.md#name-result-parts-after-their-result) | rule | guidance | Each type nested in a use-case result in `apps/api/src` leads with its result name. |
| litespec/workspace/naming#use-architectural-suffixes | [Use Architectural Suffixes](../workspace/naming.md#use-architectural-suffixes) | rule | checkable | `dotnet build` reports `LS2005` for a handler missing its command or query suffix and `LS2006` for an endpoint missing its suffix, in the dotnet stack; each other type name in `apps/api/src` and each frontend file name carries the suffix its role assigns in the Role Suffixes table. |
| litespec/workspace/naming#use-current-language-features | [Use Current Language Features](../workspace/naming.md#use-current-language-features) | rule | guidance | Production code targets the pinned language version and uses its current constructs; read the toolchain pins and the language idioms in `apps/api/src`. |
| litespec/workspace/naming#use-intent-revealing-boolean-names | [Use Intent-Revealing Boolean Names](../workspace/naming.md#use-intent-revealing-boolean-names) | rule | guidance | Each boolean property and method name in `apps/api/src` starts with `Is`, `Has`, `Can`, or a precise verb. |
| litespec/workspace/naming#use-predictable-frontend-names | [Use Predictable Frontend Names](../workspace/naming.md#use-predictable-frontend-names) | rule | guidance | Each frontend folder uses kebab-case, each component file and export uses PascalCase, and each React hook file uses `use-{name}`. |
| litespec/workspace/structure#declare-size-and-application-stacks-in-project-config | [Declare Size and Application Stacks in Project Config](../workspace/structure.md#declare-size-and-application-stacks-in-project-config) | rule | guidance | `litespec.project.json` at the workspace root declares `size` and one stack for the backend and for each frontend. |
| litespec/workspace/structure#keep-consumer-documentation-at-the-root | [Keep Consumer Documentation at the Root](../workspace/structure.md#keep-consumer-documentation-at-the-root) | rule | guidance | Every record path sits under the root `docs/` directory. |
| litespec/workspace/structure#keep-generated-api-contracts-in-packages | [Keep Generated API Contracts in Packages](../workspace/structure.md#keep-generated-api-contracts-in-packages) | convention | guidance | Generated OpenAPI types sit in `packages/api-types/` and the thin client in `packages/api-client/` once two frontends consume the API; with one frontend, that frontend owns both outputs. |
| litespec/workspace/structure#keep-orientation-documents-separate-from-canonical-records | [Keep Orientation Documents Separate from Canonical Records](../workspace/structure.md#keep-orientation-documents-separate-from-canonical-records) | rule | guidance | Each `README.md` under `docs/` links to its approved record and restates no record content. |
| litespec/workspace/structure#keep-production-and-test-code-separate | [Keep Production and Test Code Separate](../workspace/structure.md#keep-production-and-test-code-separate) | rule | guidance | Production files sit under `apps/api/src/` and test files under `apps/api/tests/`, with no test helper shipped beside production code. |
| litespec/workspace/structure#keep-runnable-applications-under-apps | [Keep Runnable Applications Under Apps](../workspace/structure.md#keep-runnable-applications-under-apps) | rule | guidance | Each directory under `apps/` runs on its own, and each directory under `packages/` is imported by an application. |
| litespec/workspace/structure#keep-scripts-at-the-root | [Keep Scripts at the Root](../workspace/structure.md#keep-scripts-at-the-root) | convention | guidance | Bootstrap, release, and CI helper scripts sit under root `scripts/`; where replaced, the project documentation names the project's own script layout. |
| litespec/workspace/structure#keep-the-generated-model-out-of-source-control | [Keep the Generated Model Out of Source Control](../workspace/structure.md#keep-the-generated-model-out-of-source-control) | rule | guidance | No path under `.model/` is tracked in git; `git ls-files .model` prints nothing. |
| litespec/workspace/structure#limit-shared-typescript-packages | [Limit Shared TypeScript Packages](../workspace/structure.md#limit-shared-typescript-packages) | rule | guidance | Each package under `packages/` is imported by at least two consumers, or carries generated output shared by design; read the imports in `apps/`. |
| litespec/workspace/structure#name-frontends-by-audience | [Name Frontends by Audience](../workspace/structure.md#name-frontends-by-audience) | convention | guidance | Each directory under `apps/` carries an audience name; where replaced, `paths.frontends` in `litespec.project.json` names the project's own frontend names. |
| litespec/workspace/structure#use-the-canonical-root-tree | [Use the Canonical Root Tree](../workspace/structure.md#use-the-canonical-root-tree) | rule | guidance | The workspace tree matches the canonical tree in this section, with each record at its type's home under `docs/`. |
